 import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import AddCircleOutlineIcon from '@mui/icons-material/AddCircleOutline';
import { Modal, Form, Button } from 'react-bootstrap'
import { Save } from '@mui/icons-material';
import DraggableModal from '../../../General/components/DraggableModal';
import { toast } from 'react-hot-toast';
import CRValue from '../../../General/components/CRValue';
import ClearInputs from '../../../General/components/ClearInputs';
import Hoot from '../../../General/components/Hoot';
import axios from 'axios';
import CourseDataOptions, { GetInstituteCode } from '../../../General/components/InstituteData';
import { SettingsApplicationsRounded, Update } from '@material-ui/icons';
import RefreshList from '../../../General/components/RefreshList';
import UserOptionDropDown from '../elements/UserOptionDropDown';
import SaveButton from '../elements/SaveButton';
import { ModalAnimationClose, ModalAnimationShow } from '../../../General/components/ModalAnimations';




function NewCourseModal(props) {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({});
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});
  const Data = [];
  const [RegisterStatus, setRegisterStatus] = useState(null);
  const [Founded, SetFounded] = useState(null);


  const [showBox, SetshowBox] = useState(false);
  const handleClose = () => {
    SetshowBox(false);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setShow(false);
    }, 1200);
  }

  const handleShow = () => {
    SetshowBox(true);
    setShow(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      GET_DATA();
    }, 1000);
  };


  const FORMURL = [
    Hoot() + "eduallcourseregisterapi/post/",
    props.url ? props.url : ''
  ]

  const INPUTS = {
    course_title: CRValue("#course_title"),
    course_category: CRValue("#course_category"),
    course_code: CRValue("#course_code"),
    institute_code: GetInstituteCode()
  };


  const GET_DATA = async () => {
    if (props.update) {
      try {
        const response = await axios.get(props.get);
        if (response.data.length > 0) {
          document.querySelector("#course_title").value = response.data[0].ed_course_title;
          document.querySelector("#course_category").value = response.data[0].ed_course_category;
          INPUTS.course_title = response.data[0].ed_course_title
          INPUTS.course_category = response.data[0].ed_course_category
        } else {
          toast.error("Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel encontrar a informação selecionada !");
        }
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error("Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel encontrar a informação selecionada !");
      }
    }
  }


  const setField = (field, value) => {
    setForm({
      ...form,
      [field]: value
    })

    if (!!errors[field]) {
      setErrors({
        ...errors,
        [field]: null
      });
    }
  }


  const validateForm = () => {
    const { course_title, course_code, course_category } = form;
    const NewErrors = {};
    console.clear();
    setRegisterStatus(0);

    if (INPUTS.course_title === "" || INPUTS.course_title === " ") {
      if (!course_title || course_title === '') NewErrors.course_title = 'Curso  invalido';
    } else { if (!course_title) { setField("course_title", INPUTS.course_title); } }

    if (INPUTS.course_category === "" || INPUTS.course_category === " ") {
      if (!course_category || course_category === '') NewErrors.course_category = 'Categoria  invalida';
    } else { if (!course_category) { setField("course_category", INPUTS.course_category); } }

    return NewErrors;
  }


  const FormSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const formErrors = validateForm();
    if (Object.keys(formErrors).length > 0) {
      setErrors(formErrors);
      console.log(formErrors);
      toast.error("Verifique todos os  campos !");
      setRegisterStatus(1);
    } else {
      if (!props.update) {
        try {
          axios.post(FORMURL[0], {
            course_title: INPUTS.course_title,
            course_code: INPUTS.course_code,
            course_category: INPUTS.course_category,
          }).then(() => {
            toast.success("Curso adicionado com sucesso !");
            setForm({});
            ClearInputs();
            setRegisterStatus(2);
            RefreshList(`.el-refresh-list`)
          })
            .catch((error) => {
              const msg = error?.response?.data?.msg || "Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel executar esta ação !";
              toast.error(msg);
              setRegisterStatus(1);
            });
        } catch (error) {
          const msg = error?.response?.data?.msg || "Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel executar esta ação !";
          toast.error(msg);
          setRegisterStatus(1);
        }
      } else {
        try {
          axios.put(FORMURL[1], {
            course_title: INPUTS.course_title,
            course_code: INPUTS.course_code,
            course_category: INPUTS.course_category,
          }).then(() => {
            toast.success("Curso atualizado com sucesso !");
            setForm({});
            ClearInputs();
            RefreshList(`.el-refresh-list`);
            setRegisterStatus(2);
            setTimeout(() => {
              handleClose();
            }, 1000);
          }).catch((error) => {
            setRegisterStatus(1);
            const msg = error?.response?.data?.msg || "Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel executar esta ação !";
            toast.error(msg);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          setRegisterStatus(1);
          const msg = error?.response?.data?.msg || "Lamentamos mas não foi  possivel executar esta ação !";
          toast.error(msg);
        }
      }
    }
  };


  const handleInput = (e) => {
    switch (e.target.id) {
      case "course_title":
        setField("course_title", e.target.value)
        INPUTS.course_title = e.target.value
        break;
      case "course_code":
        setField("course_code", e.target.value)
        INPUTS.course_code = e.target.value
        break;
      case "course_category":
        setField("course_category", e.target.value)
        INPUTS.course_category = e.target.value
        break;
      default:
    }
  }



  return (
    <div>
      <div onClick={handleShow}>
        {
          props.toggle_btn ? props.toggle_btn : <button className='btn btn-main bg-main'><AddCircleOutlineIcon /> Registrar curso </button>
        }
      </div>
      <Modal size='lg' centered className={showBox ? ModalAnimationShow() : ModalAnimationClose()} dialogAs={DraggableModal} show={show} onHide={handleClose}>
        <Modal.Header closeButton>
          <Modal.Title>
            <div className="ed-wrap align-center">
              <UserOptionDropDown />
              <h5> {props.title ? props.title + ' Curso' : 'Registrar curso'}  </h5>
            </div>
          </Modal.Title>
        </Modal.Header>
        <Form onSubmit={FormSubmit}>
          <Modal.Body className='scrollLimit'>
            <Form.Group className="mb-3">
              <Form.Label>Nome do curso<span className="text-danger ml-2">*</span> </Form.Label>
              <Form.Control type="text" onChange={handleInput} className={!!errors.course_title && 'is-invalid'} value={form.course_title}
                isInvalid={!!errors.course_title} id="course_title" />
              <Form.Control.Feedback type='invalid'>{errors.course_title}</Form.Control.Feedback>
            </Form.Group>
            <Form.Group className="mb-3" >
              <Form.Label>Categoria do curso<span className="text-danger ml-2">*</span> </Form.Label>
              <Form.Select onChange={handleInput} className={!!errors.course_category && 'is-invalid'} value={form.course_category}
                isInvalid={!!errors.course_category} id="course_category" >
                <option selected disabled>selecionar categoria...</option>
                <option value="1">Designer</option>
                <option value="2">Arte</option>
                <option value="3">Engenharia</option>
                <option value="4">Saúde</option>
                <option value="5">Ciências</option>
                <option value="6">Tecnologia</option>
                <option value="7">Educação</option>
                <option value="8">Negócios</option>
                <option value="9">Direito</option>
                <option value="10">Arquitetura</option>
                <option value="11">Administração</option>
                <option value="12">Matemática</option>
                <option value="13">Comunicação</option>
                <option value="14">Agricultura</option>
                <option value="15">Desporto</option>
                <option value="16">Meio Ambiente</option>
                <option value="17">Humanidades</option>
                <option value="18">Turismo</option>
                <option value="19">Transporte</option>
                <option value="20">Outra</option>
              </Form.Select>
              <Form.Control.Feedback type='invalid'>{errors.course_category}</Form.Control.Feedback>
            </Form.Group>
          </Modal.Body>
          <Modal.Footer>
            <div className="ed-space">
              <div className="ed-flex"></div>
              <div className="ed-flex">
                <Button className='bg-light text-dark' onClick={handleClose}> Cancelar </Button>
                {props.update ? (Founded === false ? <div></div> :
                  <SaveButton icon={props.title ? <Update /> : <Save />} title={props.title ? props.title : 'Salvar'} status={RegisterStatus} />) :
                  <SaveButton icon={props.title ? <Update /> : <Save />} title={props.title ? props.title : 'Salvar'} status={RegisterStatus} />}
              </div>
            </div>
          </Modal.Footer>
        </Form>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  )
}

export default NewCourseModal

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// BEHAVIOUR LOGIC
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const BEHAVIOUR_LEVELS = ["NS", "S", "B", "MB", "E"];
const BEHAVIOUR_LABELS = {
  NS: "Não Satisfatório", S: "Satisfatório", B: "Bom", MB: "Muito Bom", E: "Excelente",
};

function calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, column, passValue = 10, maxValue = 20) {
  const totalAbsences = grades.reduce((acc, g) => acc + (g.absences ?? 0), 0);
  if (column === "fal") {
    if (totalAbsences === 0) return "E";
    if (totalAbsences <= 3) return "MB";
    if (totalAbsences <= 7) return "B";
    if (totalAbsences <= 14) return "S";
    return "NS";
  }
  const getVal = (g) => {
    if (column === "mt1") return g.mt1 !== null && g.mt1 !== undefined ? g.mt1 : null;
    if (column === "mt2") return g.mt2 !== null && g.mt2 !== undefined ? g.mt2 : null;
    if (column === "mt3") return g.mt3 !== null && g.mt3 !== undefined ? g.mt3 : null;
    if (column === "mdf") {
      if (g.mdf !== null && g.mdf !== undefined) return g.mdf;
      const parts = [g.mt1, g.mt2, g.mt3].filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
      return parts.length === 3 ? parts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 3 : null;
    }
    if (column === "mt") return g.mt !== null && g.mt !== undefined ? g.mt : null;
    return null;
  };
  let weightedSum = 0, totalWeight = 0;
  for (const g of grades) {
    const v = getVal(g);
    if (v === null) continue;
    const w = g.is_key ? 2 : 1;
    weightedSum += v * w;
    totalWeight += w;
  }
  if (totalAbsences >= 20) return "NS";
  if (totalAbsences >= 12) return "S";
  if (totalWeight === 0) return "B";
  const avg = weightedSum / totalWeight;
  const pct = avg / maxValue;
  if (pct >= 0.90) return "E";
  if (pct >= 0.75) return "MB";
  if (pct >= 0.55) return "B";
  if (pct >= passValue / maxValue) return "S";
  return "NS";
}

function calcBehaviour(grades, passValue = 10, maxValue = 20) {
  return calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mdf", passValue, maxValue);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// GRADE FORMATTING — max 1 decimal place
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Format a grade value with at most 1 decimal place.
 * e.g. 14.0 → "14"  |  14.5 → "14.5"  |  14.567 → "14.6"
 */
const fmt = (v, zeroFill = false) => {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return zeroFill ? "0" : "";
  const n = parseFloat(String(v));
  if (isNaN(n)) return zeroFill ? "0" : String(v);
  // Round to 1 decimal, then strip trailing .0
  const r = Math.round(n * 10) / 10;
  return Number.isInteger(r) ? String(r) : r.toFixed(1);
};

// MDF shown as integer (it's a rounded average)
const fmtMdf = (v, zeroFill = false) => {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return zeroFill ? "0" : "";
  const n = parseFloat(String(v));
  if (isNaN(n)) return zeroFill ? "0" : "";
  return String(Math.round(n));
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// COLOUR HELPERS — BLUE for positive, RED for negative
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const POSITIVE_COLOR_CSS  = "#072AC8"; // blue
const NEGATIVE_COLOR_CSS  = "#FF0000"; // red
const POSITIVE_COLOR_RGB  = [7, 42, 200];
const NEGATIVE_COLOR_RGB  = [255, 0, 0];

const makeGc  = (passValue = 10) => (v) => {
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? "#111" : n >= passValue ? POSITIVE_COLOR_CSS : NEGATIVE_COLOR_CSS;
};
const makeGcR = (passValue = 10) => (v) => {
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? [17, 17, 17] : n >= passValue ? POSITIVE_COLOR_RGB : NEGATIVE_COLOR_RGB;
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// OTHER HELPERS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const resolveMdf = (g, zeroFill) => {
  if (g.mdf !== null && g.mdf !== undefined) return g.mdf;
  if (!zeroFill) return null;
  const t1 = g.mt1 ?? 0, t2 = g.mt2 ?? 0, t3 = g.mt3 ?? 0;
  return Math.round(((t1 + t2 + t3) / 3) * 10) / 10;
};

const todayPT = () =>
  new Date().toLocaleDateString("pt-PT", { day: "2-digit", month: "long", year: "numeric" });

const behaviourStyle = (level) => {
  const map = {
    NS: { bg: "#fff1f2", border: "#fca5a5", color: "#991b1b" },
    S:  { bg: "#fefce8", border: "#fde047", color: "#713f12" },
    B:  { bg: "#f0fdf4", border: "#86efac", color: "#166534" },
    MB: { bg: "#eff6ff", border: "#93c5fd", color: "#1e40af" },
    E:  { bg: "#f5f3ff", border: "#c4b5fd", color: "#5b21b6" },
  };
  return map[level] || { bg: "#f8fafc", border: "#cbd5e1", color: "#64748b" };
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SEARCHABLE MULTI-SELECT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function StudentMultiSelect({ options, value, onChange }) {
  const [search, setSearch] = React.useState("");
  const filtered = options.filter(o => o.label.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
  const selectedSet = new Set(value.map(v => v.value));

  const toggle = (opt) => {
    if (selectedSet.has(opt.value)) onChange(value.filter(v => v.value !== opt.value));
    else onChange([...value, opt]);
  };

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value))) {
      const filteredSet = new Set(filtered.map(f => f.value));
      onChange(value.filter(v => !filteredSet.has(v.value)));
    } else {
      const merged = [...value];
      filtered.forEach(o => { if (!selectedSet.has(o.value)) merged.push(o); });
      onChange(merged);
    }
  };

  const allFilteredSelected = filtered.length > 0 && filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value));

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'DM Sans', sans-serif" }}>
      <div style={{ position: "relative", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <input
          type="text" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Pesquisar aluno…"
          style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 32px 8px 12px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "'DM Sans',sans-serif", outline: "none", color: "#0f172a", background: "#fff", boxSizing: "border-box" }}
          onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)"}
          onBlur={e => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}
        />
        {search && (
          <button onClick={() => setSearch("")} style={{ position: "absolute", right: 10, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 16, lineHeight: 1, padding: 0 }}>×</button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "4px 8px", marginBottom: 4, background: "#f8fafc", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 11.5, color: "#64748b" }}>
        <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 500 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={allFilteredSelected} onChange={toggleAll} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)", cursor: "pointer" }} />
          {allFilteredSelected ? "Desseleccionar todos" : "Seleccionar todos"}
        </label>
        <span style={{ color: "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)", fontWeight: 600 }}>{value.length}/{options.length}</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{ maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, background: "#fff" }}>
        {filtered.length === 0
          ? <div style={{ padding: "12px 14px", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 13 }}>Nenhum aluno encontrado.</div>
          : filtered.map(opt => {
            const checked = selectedSet.has(opt.value);
            return (
              <label key={opt.value} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: "7px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid #f1f5f9", background: checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff", fontSize: 13, color: "#0f172a", transition: "background .1s" }}
                onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.07)" : "#f8fafc"}
                onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff"}>
                <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={() => toggle(opt)} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)", cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0 }} />
                <span style={{ fontWeight: checked ? 600 : 400 }}>{opt.label}</span>
              </label>
            );
          })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SUBJECT REORDER PANEL
// Drag-and-drop to swap subjects + A-Z / Z-A sort buttons
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function SubjectReorderPanel({ subjects, order, onOrderChange }) {
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(null); // index being dragged
  const [over, setOver]         = useState(null);  // index being hovered over
  const dragItem                = useRef(null);
  const dragOverItem            = useRef(null);

  if (!subjects || subjects.length === 0) return null;

  // Build ordered list of {id, title}
  const orderedSubjects = order.map(id => subjects.find(s => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);

  const handleDragStart = (idx) => {
    dragItem.current = idx;
    setDragging(idx);
  };
  const handleDragEnter = (idx) => {
    dragOverItem.current = idx;
    setOver(idx);
  };
  const handleDragEnd = () => {
    if (dragItem.current === null || dragOverItem.current === null || dragItem.current === dragOverItem.current) {
      setDragging(null); setOver(null); dragItem.current = null; dragOverItem.current = null;
      return;
    }
    const newOrder = [...order];
    const from = dragItem.current, to = dragOverItem.current;
    const [moved] = newOrder.splice(from, 1);
    newOrder.splice(to, 0, moved);
    onOrderChange(newOrder);
    setDragging(null); setOver(null); dragItem.current = null; dragOverItem.current = null;
  };

  const sortAZ = () => {
    const sorted = [...order].sort((a, b) => {
      const sa = subjects.find(s => s.id === a)?.title || "";
      const sb = subjects.find(s => s.id === b)?.title || "";
      return sa.localeCompare(sb, "pt");
    });
    onOrderChange(sorted);
  };
  const sortZA = () => {
    const sorted = [...order].sort((a, b) => {
      const sa = subjects.find(s => s.id === a)?.title || "";
      const sb = subjects.find(s => s.id === b)?.title || "";
      return sb.localeCompare(sa, "pt");
    });
    onOrderChange(sorted);
  };
  const resetOrder = () => {
    onOrderChange(subjects.map(s => s.id));
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'DM Sans', sans-serif" }}>
      {/* Sort action buttons */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginBottom: 10, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
        <button onClick={sortAZ} style={sortBtnStyle(false)} title="Ordenar A → Z">
          <TbSortAZ size={14} /> A → Z
        </button>
        <button onClick={sortZA} style={sortBtnStyle(false)} title="Ordenar Z → A">
          <TbSortZA size={14} /> Z → A
        </button>
        <button onClick={resetOrder} style={{ ...sortBtnStyle(false), marginLeft: "auto", color: "#94a3b8", borderColor: "#e2e8f0" }} title="Repor ordem original">
          Repor
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Drag-and-drop list */}
      <div style={{
        border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, overflow: "hidden",
        background: "#fff", maxHeight: 260, overflowY: "auto",
      }}>
        {orderedSubjects.map((subj, idx) => {
          if (!subj) return null;
          const isDraggingThis = dragging === idx;
          const isOver = over === idx && dragging !== null && dragging !== idx;
          return (
            <div
              key={subj.id}
              draggable
              onDragStart={() => handleDragStart(idx)}
              onDragEnter={() => handleDragEnter(idx)}
              onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
              onDragOver={e => e.preventDefault()}
              style={{
                display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8,
                padding: "8px 12px",
                borderBottom: idx < orderedSubjects.length - 1 ? "1px solid #f1f5f9" : "none",
                background: isDraggingThis
                  ? "rgba(108,63,197,.07)"
                  : isOver
                    ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)"
                    : "#fff",
                cursor: "grab",
                userSelect: "none",
                opacity: isDraggingThis ? 0.6 : 1,
                borderLeft: isOver ? "3px solid #6c3fc5" : "3px solid transparent",
                transition: "background .1s, border-left .1s",
              }}
            >
              <MdDragIndicator size={16} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
              <span style={{
                fontSize: 12.5, color: "#334155",
                fontWeight: isDraggingThis ? 600 : 400, flex: 1,
              }}>
                {subj.title}
              </span>
              <span style={{
                fontSize: 10, color: "#94a3b8", fontFamily: "monospace",
                background: "#f8fafc", border: "1px solid #e2e8f0",
                padding: "1px 5px", borderRadius: 4, flexShrink: 0,
              }}>
                #{idx + 1}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: "#94a3b8", marginTop: 6 }}>
        Arraste para reordenar · A ordem aplica-se ao PDF e à pré-visualização
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const sortBtnStyle = (active) => ({
  display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 5,
  padding: "6px 14px", borderRadius: 999,
  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 500,
  border: "1.5px solid " + (active ? "#6c3fc5" : "#e2e8f0"),
  background: active ? "#6c3fc5" : "#f8fafc",
  color: active ? "#fff" : "#64748b",
  cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "'DM Sans',sans-serif",
  transition: "all .15s",
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FIELD VISIBILITY MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function FieldVisibilityControl({ visible, onToggle }) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const fields = [
    { key: "studentName", label: "Nome do Aluno" },
    { key: "className", label: "Classe/Turma" },
    { key: "classroom", label: "Sala" },
    { key: "period", label: "Turno" },
    { key: "directorName", label: "Director de Turma" },
    { key: "academicYear", label: "Ano Lectivo" },
    { key: "principal", label: "Director Geral" },
  ];

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#f8fafc", border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, padding: "10px 12px" }}>
      <button onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
        style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 10px", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: "#334155", transition: "color .15s" }}
        onMouseEnter={e => e.target.style.color = "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)"}
        onMouseLeave={e => e.target.style.color = "#334155"}>
        <MdOutlineVisibility size={14} />
        Visibilidade de Campos
        <span style={{ marginLeft: "auto", opacity: 0.5, fontSize: 10 }}>{expanded ? "▼" : "▶"}</span>
      </button>
      {expanded && (
        <div style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #e2e8f0", marginTop: 8, paddingTop: 8, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 8 }}>
          {fields.map(f => (
            <label key={f.key} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 7, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11.5, color: "#475569", padding: "4px 6px", borderRadius: 6, transition: "background .1s" }}
              onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = "#fff"}
              onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={visible[f.key] !== false} onChange={() => onToggle(f.key)}
                style={{ width: 13, height: 13, accentColor: "var(--ed-purple,#6c3fc5)", cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0 }} />
              <span style={{ fontWeight: visible[f.key] !== false ? 500 : 400 }}>{f.label}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const API = {
  classes:            () => `${Hoot()}eduallclassapi/get`,
  studentsByClass:    (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallgetstudentsbyclass/${id}`,
  bulletinIndividual: () => `${Hoot()}eduallreports/bulletin-individual`,
  bulletinAnnual:     () => `${Hoot()}eduallreports/bulletin-annual`,
};

const QUARTER_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 1, label: "1º Trimestre" },
  { value: 2, label: "2º Trimestre" },
  { value: 3, label: "3º Trimestre" },
];

const PROG_OPTIONS = ["", "Bom", "Muito Bom", "Suficiente", "Mau"];
const SIT_OPTIONS  = ["TRANSITA", "NÃO TRANSITA"];

const PURPLE_HEX  = "#6c3fc5";
const GREEN_HEX   = "#22a06b";
const F           = "'Poppins', sans-serif";
const ANGOLA_COAT = "https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/ff/Emblem_of_Angola.svg/240px-Emblem_of_Angola.svg.png";

const INFO_BG_PDF     = [245, 245, 245];
const INFO_BORDER_PDF = [200, 200, 200];

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Apply subject ordering to grades array
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function applySubjectOrder(grades, subjectOrder) {
  if (!subjectOrder || subjectOrder.length === 0) return grades;
  const orderMap = new Map(subjectOrder.map((id, idx) => [id, idx]));
  return [...grades].sort((a, b) => {
    const ia = orderMap.has(a.subject_id) ? orderMap.get(a.subject_id) : 9999;
    const ib = orderMap.has(b.subject_id) ? orderMap.get(b.subject_id) : 9999;
    return ia - ib;
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// INDIVIDUAL BULLETIN
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function IndividualBulletin({ data, school, cls, subDir, indType, quarter, customLogoUrl, passValue = 10, fieldVisibility = {}, zeroFill = false, behaviourByCol = {}, autoByCol = {}, bulletinTitle = "BOLETIM DE NOTAS", editMode = false, onBehaviourChange, subjectOrder = [] }) {
  const gc     = makeGc(passValue);
  const qLabel = QUARTER_OPTIONS.find(q => q.value === quarter)?.label || "1º Trimestre";
  const cidade = school?.state || "";
  const rawGrades = data?.grades || [];
  const grades = applySubjectOrder(rawGrades, subjectOrder);
  const isMac  = indType !== "final";

  const centreInsignia = customLogoUrl || school?.coat_of_arms_url || ANGOLA_COAT;
  const schoolLogo     = school?.logo_url || null;

  const B   = "1px solid #000";
  const BL  = "1px solid #ccc";
  const B15 = "1.5px solid #000";

  const showStudentName  = fieldVisibility.studentName  !== false;
  const showClassName    = fieldVisibility.className    !== false;
  const showClassroom    = fieldVisibility.classroom    !== false;
  const showDirector     = fieldVisibility.directorName !== false;
  const showAcademicYear = fieldVisibility.academicYear !== false;

  return (
    <div style={{ width: "148.5mm", minHeight: "210mm", border: B15, padding: "7mm 8mm 6mm 8mm", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", fontFamily: F, background: "#fff", boxSizing: "border-box" }}>
      {/* HEADER */}
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "54px 1fr 94px", gap: "6px", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>

        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: "3px", textAlign: "center" }}>
          <img src={centreInsignia} alt="Insignia" style={{ width: 42, height: 42, objectFit: "contain", display: "block" }} />

          </p>

          <div style={{ width: 66, borderBottom: "1px solid #000", margin: "10px auto 2px" }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TITLE */}
      <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "10.5pt", fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "center", textTransform: "uppercase", textDecoration: "underline", color: "#000", margin: "12px 0 9px" }}>
        {bulletinTitle || "Boletim de Notas"}
      </div>

      {/* STUDENT INFO BOX */}
      <div style={{ border: BL, background: "#f5f5f5", padding: "8px 14px 8px", marginBottom: "7px", borderRadius: "10px" }}>
        <table className="bec-info-tbl" style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontFamily: F, fontSize: "8pt" }}>
          <colgroup><col style={{ width: "55%" }} /><col style={{ width: "45%" }} /></colgroup>
          <tbody>
            {showStudentName && (
              <tr>
                <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Nome: </span>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#cc0000" }}>{data?.student_name || ""}</span>
                </td>
                <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Turma: </span>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{showClassName ? (cls?.title || cls?.code || "") : "—"}</span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            )}
            <tr>
              <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}><span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Nº: </span><span>{data?.student_code || ""}</span></td>
              <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}><span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Período: </span><span>{data?.period_label || data?.period || ""}</span></td>
            </tr>
            {showAcademicYear && (
              <tr>
                <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}><span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Ano lectivo: </span><span>{cls?.academic_year || ""}</span></td>
                <td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }}><span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Sala: </span><span>{showClassroom ? (cls?.classroom || "") : "—"}</span></td>
              </tr>
            )}
            {data?.course && (
              <tr><td style={{ padding: "1.5px 0", lineHeight: 1.85 }} colSpan={2}><span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Curso: </span><span>{data.course}</span></td></tr>
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {/* SECTION TITLE */}
      <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "8pt", fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "center", textTransform: "uppercase", textDecoration: "underline", color: "#000", margin: "2px 0 5px" }}>
        Descriminação das Notas – {qLabel}
      </div>

      {/* GRADES TABLE */}
      <table className="bec-grades-tbl" style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", tableLayout: "fixed", border: "1px solid #000", outline: "1px solid #000", flex: 1 }}>
        <colgroup>
          {isMac
            ? <><col style={{ width: "50%" }} /><col style={{ width: "13%" }} /><col style={{ width: "13%" }} /><col style={{ width: "13%" }} /><col style={{ width: "11%" }} /></>
            : <><col style={{ width: "50%" }} /><col style={{ width: "15%" }} /><col style={{ width: "18%" }} /><col style={{ width: "17%" }} /></>}
        </colgroup>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th style={TH({ textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 8, borderRight: B })}>{isMac ? "DISCIPLINAS" : "ÁREA CURRICULAR"}</th>
            {isMac ? (
              <>
                <th style={TH({ borderRight: B })}>MAC</th>
                <th style={TH({ borderRight: B })}>NPT</th>
                <th style={TH({ borderRight: B })}>MT{quarter}</th>

              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <th style={TH({ fontSize: "7pt", lineHeight: 1.2, borderRight: B })}>CLAS.<br />FINAL</th>
                <th style={TH({ borderRight: B })}>COMPORT.</th>
                <th style={TH({})}>OBS:</th>
              </>
            )}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {grades.map((g, i) => (
            <tr key={i}>
              <td style={TD({ textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 8, borderRight: B })}>{g.subject_title}</td>
              {isMac ? (
                <>
                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mac !== null && g.mac !== undefined ? g.mac : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.mac, zeroFill)}</td>
                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.npt !== null && g.npt !== undefined ? g.npt : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.npt, zeroFill)}</td>
                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mt  !== null && g.mt  !== undefined ? g.mt  : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.mt, zeroFill)}</td>
                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, color: (g.absences ?? 0) > 0 ? "#cc0000" : "#555" })}>{g.absences ?? 0}</td>

              ) : (

                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "left", verticalAlign: "top", paddingLeft: 6, borderRight: B })}>
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mt !== null && g.mt !== undefined ? g.mt : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)) }}>{fmt(g.mt, zeroFill)}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td>
                  <td style={TD({})}></td>
                </>
              )}
            </tr>
          ))}
          {[0, 1].map(i => (
            <tr key={`e${i}`} style={{ height: 14 }}>
              <td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td>
              {isMac
                ? <><td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({})}></td></>
                : <><td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({ borderRight: "none" })}></td><td style={TD({})}></td></>}
            </tr>
          ))}
          {isMac && (
            <>
              {["FALTAS POR INDISCIPLINA", "FALTAS POR NÃO COMPARÊNCIA"].map((lbl, i) => (
                <tr key={`b${i}`}>
                  <td style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 8, borderRight: B })}>{lbl}</td>
                  <td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td>
                  <td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({})}></td>
                </tr>
              ))}
              <tr>
                <td style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 8, borderRight: B })}>COMPORTAMENTO</td>

                <td style={TD({ borderRight: B })}></td><td style={TD({})}></td>
              </tr>
            </>
          )}
          <tr>
            <td colSpan={isMac ? 5 : 4} style={TD({ fontSize: "7pt", padding: "3px 8px" })}>Para os devidos efeitos, passou-se o presente boletim de notas que vai por mim assinado.</td>
          </tr>
          <tr>
            <td colSpan={isMac ? 5 : 4} style={TD({ fontSize: "7pt", textAlign: "center", fontStyle: "italic", padding: "2px 8px", color: "#000", background: "#fff" })}>
              {cidade ? `${cidade}, aos ` : ""}{todayPT()}
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginTop: "auto", paddingTop: "12px" }}>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
          <div className="bec-sig-line" style={{ width: 130, borderBottom: B, marginBottom: "4px" }} />
          <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "7.5pt", fontWeight: 600 }}>O (A) Director(a) de Turma</div>
          {showDirector && cls?.director_name && <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "7pt", color: "#444" }}>{cls.director_name}</div>}
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
          <div className="bec-sig-line" style={{ width: 130, borderBottom: B, marginBottom: "4px" }} />
          <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "7.5pt", fontWeight: 600 }}>O (A) Encarregado(a)</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ANNUAL BULLETIN
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function AnnualBulletin({ data, school, cls, subDir, customLogoUrl, passValue = 10, maxValue = 20, editMode = false, onProgressChange, onSituationChange, onBehaviourChange, fieldVisibility = {}, zeroFill = false, behaviourByCol = {}, autoByCol = {}, bulletinTitle = "BOLETIM ANUAL", subjectOrder = [] }) {
  const cidade = school?.state || "";

  const finSit  = data?.overrideSituation ?? data?.summary?.final_situation ?? "TRANSITA";
  const progLbl = data?.progressLabel ?? "";

  const B  = "1px solid #000";
  const B2 = "1.5px solid #000";

  const panelLogo = customLogoUrl || school?.logo_url || null;

  const TD = (extra = {}) => ({ border: B, fontFamily: F, fontSize: "7.5pt", padding: "3px 5px", color: "#000", background: "#fff", ...extra });

  const showPrincipal = fieldVisibility.principal !== false;
  const showClassName = fieldVisibility.className !== false;
  const showClassroom = fieldVisibility.classroom !== false;
  const showPeriod    = fieldVisibility.period    !== false;

  const progColor = {
    "Bom":        { bg: "#f0fdf4", border: "#86efac", color: "#166534" },
    "Muito Bom":  { bg: "#eff6ff", border: "#93c5fd", color: "#1e40af" },
    "Suficiente": { bg: "#fefce8", border: "#fde047", color: "#713f12" },
    "Mau":        { bg: "#fff1f2", border: "#fca5a5", color: "#991b1b" },
    "":           { bg: "#f8fafc", border: "#cbd5e1", color: "#64748b" },
  };
  const pc = progColor[progLbl] || progColor[""];

  const COLS = ["mt1", "mt2", "mt3", "mdf", "fal"];
  const bLevelForCol = (col) => behaviourByCol[col] || "";

  return (
    <div style={{ width: editMode ? "100%" : "148.5mm", minHeight: "210mm", border: B2, display: "flex", flexDirection: "row", fontFamily: F, background: "#fff", boxSizing: "border-box" }}>
      {/* LEFT PANEL */}
      <div style={{ width: editMode ? 200 : 110, borderRight: B, padding: "10px 8px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: 2, flexShrink: 0, transition: "width .2s" }}>
        {panelLogo && <img src={panelLogo} alt="" style={{ width: 60, height: 38, objectFit: "contain", display: "block", margin: "0 auto 4px" }} />}
        <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "6pt", color: "#000", textAlign: "center", textTransform: "uppercase", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "6pt", color: "#000", textAlign: "center", textTransform: "uppercase", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "6.5pt", fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "center", color: "#000", margin: "1px 0", lineHeight: 1.4 }}>"{school?.name || ""}"</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "6pt", fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "center", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#000", lineHeight: 1.5, marginTop: 2 }}>BOLETIM DE NOTAS DO ENSINO PRIMÁRIO</div>
        {showPrincipal && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 8, textAlign: "center", fontSize: "6.5pt", color: "#000", width: "100%", fontFamily: F }}>
            <div>O Director Geral</div>
            <div style={{ borderBottom: B, width: 80, margin: "13px auto 3px" }} />
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{school?.principal || ""}</div>
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ borderTop: B, width: "100%", margin: "8px 0 4px" }} />
        <div style={{ width: "100%", fontFamily: F, fontSize: "6.5pt", color: "#000", lineHeight: 1.75 }}>
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 2 }}><span>Nome: </span><span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#cc0000" }}>{data?.student_name || ""}</span></div>
          {showClassName && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 2 }}>
              <span>Classe: </span><span style={{ color: "#1a56a0", fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline" }}>{cls?.title || ""}</span>
              {showClassroom && <><span style={{ marginLeft: 5 }}>Sala: </span><span style={{ color: "#1a56a0", fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline" }}>{cls?.classroom || ""}</span></>}
            </div>
          )}
          <div>
            <span>Turma: </span><span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{cls?.code || ""} </span>
            {showPeriod && cls?.period && <><span>Turno: </span><span style={{ color: "#cc5500", fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline" }}>{cls.period}</span></>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* RIGHT PANEL */}
      <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", minWidth: 0 }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: F, fontSize: "9pt", fontWeight: 800, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "2px", background: "#f0f0f0", padding: "5px 4px", borderBottom: B2, color: "#000", textAlign: "center" }}>
          {bulletinTitle || "BOLETIM ANUAL"}
        </div>
        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontFamily: F }}>
          <thead>
            <tr style={{ borderBottom: B2 }}>
              <th style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 6, borderRight: B, borderBottom: B2, width: "34%", background: "#fff" })}>DISCIPLINAS</th>
              {["MT1", "MT2", "MT3", "MDF", "FAL"].map(h => (
                <th key={h} style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", textAlign: "center", borderRight: B, borderBottom: B2, width: h === "FAL" ? "10%" : "12.4%", background: "#fff" })}>{h}</th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {grades.map((g, i) => (
              <tr key={i} style={{ borderTop: B }}>
                <td style={TD({ textAlign: "left", paddingLeft: 6, borderRight: B })}>
                  {g.subject_title}
                  {g.is_key && <span style={{ marginLeft: 4, fontSize: "5.5pt", background: "#f59e0b", color: "#fff", padding: "1px 4px", borderRadius: 99, fontWeight: 700 }}>CHAVE</span>}
                </td>
                <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mt1 !== null && g.mt1 !== undefined ? g.mt1 : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.mt1, zeroFill)}</td>
                <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mt2 !== null && g.mt2 !== undefined ? g.mt2 : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.mt2, zeroFill)}</td>
                <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: makeGc(passValue)(g.mt3 !== null && g.mt3 !== undefined ? g.mt3 : (zeroFill ? 0 : null)), fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmt(g.mt3, zeroFill)}</td>
                {(() => { const mdf = resolveMdf(g, zeroFill); return (
                  <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", color: mdf !== null ? makeGc(passValue)(mdf) : "#111", fontWeight: 700, borderRight: B })}>{fmtMdf(mdf, zeroFill)}</td>
                ); })()}
                <td style={TD({ textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, color: (g.absences ?? 0) > 0 ? "#cc0000" : "#555" })}>{g.absences ?? 0}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}

            {/* BEHAVIOUR ROW */}
            <tr style={{ borderTop: B2 }}>
              <td style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, paddingLeft: 6, borderRight: B, background: "#f0f0f0", fontSize: "7pt", verticalAlign: "middle" })}>Avaliação do Comportamento</td>
              {COLS.map((col, idx) => {
                const colLevel = bLevelForCol(col);
                const lbs = colLevel ? behaviourStyle(colLevel) : { bg: "#f0f0f0", color: "#94a3b8" };
                return (
                  <td key={col} style={TD({ borderRight: idx < 4 ? B : "none", padding: 0, verticalAlign: "middle", background: "#f0f0f0", position: "relative" })}>
                    <select value={colLevel} onChange={e => onBehaviourChange?.(data.student_id, col, e.target.value)}
                      style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, width: "100%", height: "100%", border: "none", borderRadius: 0, fontFamily: F, fontSize: "7pt", fontWeight: colLevel ? 700 : 400, background: lbs.bg, color: lbs.color, cursor: "pointer", outline: "none", textAlign: "center", padding: "0 2px" }}>
                      <option value="">—</option>
                      {BEHAVIOUR_LEVELS.map(opt => <option key={opt} value={opt}>{opt} — {BEHAVIOUR_LABELS[opt]}</option>)}
                    </select>
                  </td>
                );
              })}
            </tr>

            <tr style={{ borderTop: B }}>
              <td style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "6.5pt", lineHeight: 1.5, borderRight: B, padding: "5px 5px" })}>Obs. Progressão<br />qualitativa do aluno<br />na aula</td>
              <td colSpan={5} style={TD({ padding: 0, background: progLbl ? pc.bg : "#fff" })}>
                {editMode ? (
                  <select value={progLbl} onChange={e => onProgressChange?.(data.student_id, e.target.value)}
                    style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", border: "none", borderRadius: 0, padding: "6px 8px", fontFamily: F, fontSize: "8pt", fontWeight: 700, background: progLbl ? pc.bg : "#fff", color: progLbl ? pc.color : "#64748b", cursor: "pointer", outline: "none" }}>
                    {PROG_OPTIONS.map(o => <option key={o} value={o}>{o || "— seleccionar —"}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : (
                  <span style={{ display: "block", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: "9pt", textAlign: "center", letterSpacing: "4px", padding: "4px 6px", color: progLbl ? pc.color : "#cbd5e1", minHeight: 18 }}>
                    {progLbl ? progLbl.toUpperCase().split("").join(" ") : ""}
                  </span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>

            <tr style={{ borderTop: B }}>
              <td style={TD({ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "6.5pt", lineHeight: 1.45, borderRight: B, padding: "5px 5px" })}>Assinatura do<br />Encarregado de Educação</td>
              <td colSpan={5} style={TD({})}></td>
            </tr>

            <tr style={{ borderTop: B }}>
              <td style={TD({ background: finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#70ad47" : "#cc0000", color: "#fff", fontWeight: 700, textAlign: "center", borderRight: B })}>Situação final</td>
              <td colSpan={5} style={TD({ padding: "3px 5px", background: finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#fff" : "#fff4f4" })}>
                {editMode ? (
                  <select value={finSit} onChange={e => onSituationChange?.(data.student_id, e.target.value)}
                    style={{ width: "100%", border: `1.5px solid ${finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#70ad47" : "#cc0000"}`, borderRadius: 6, padding: "3px 6px", fontFamily: F, fontSize: "8pt", fontWeight: 700, background: finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#f0fdf4" : "#fff4f4", color: finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#15803d" : "#cc0000", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                    {SIT_OPTIONS.map(o => <option key={o} value={o}>{o}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : (
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: "9pt", letterSpacing: "4px", display: "block", textAlign: "center", color: finSit === "TRANSITA" ? "#000" : "#cc0000" }}>
                    {finSit.split("").join(" ")}
                  </span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>

            <tr style={{ borderTop: B }}>
              <td colSpan={6} style={TD({ textAlign: "center", fontSize: "7pt", padding: "3px 5px" })}>{cidade ? `${cidade}, aos ` : ""}{todayPT()}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr style={{ borderTop: B }}>
              <td colSpan={6} style={TD({ textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "7.5pt", background: "#f2f2f2", padding: "4px 5px" })}>O Sub-Director Pedagógico{subDir ? ` — ${subDir}` : ""}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PDF EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function exportPDF(payload, bulletinType, indType, quarter, customLogoUrl, zeroFill = false, bulletinTitle = "BOLETIM ANUAL", subjectOrder = []) {
  const isAnnual  = bulletinType === "annual";
  const passValue = payload.pass_value ?? 10;
  const maxValue  = payload.max_value  ?? 20;
  const gcR       = makeGcR(passValue);
  const PW = 297, PH = 210, HALF = 148.5;
  const BK = [0, 0, 0];

  const pdf = new jsPDF({ orientation: "landscape", unit: "mm", format: "a4", compress: true });

  const cache = {};
  async function imgFetch(src) {
    if (!src) return null;
    if (cache[src]) return cache[src];
    try {
      const blob = await (await fetch(src, { mode: "cors" })).blob();
      return new Promise(ok => {
        const fr = new FileReader();
        fr.onload = () => { cache[src] = fr.result; ok(fr.result); };
        fr.readAsDataURL(blob);
      });
    } catch { return null; }
  }

  function drawHalfDivider() {
    pdf.setDrawColor(180, 180, 180); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.line(HALF, 2, HALF, PH - 2);
  }

  function drawInd(student, school, cls, subDir, hX, schoolLogoB64, insigniaB64) {
    const rawGrades = student.grades || [];
    const grades    = applySubjectOrder(rawGrades, subjectOrder);
    const qLabel    = QUARTER_OPTIONS.find(q => q.value === quarter)?.label || "1º Trimestre";
    const cidade    = school?.state || "";
    const isMac     = indType !== "final";
    const cX        = hX + 8;
    const cW        = HALF - 16;
    const midX      = hX + HALF / 2;

    pdf.setDrawColor(120, 120, 120); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.rect(hX + 0.5, 0.5, HALF - 1, PH - 1);

    let y = 8;

    if (schoolLogoB64) { try { pdf.addImage(schoolLogoB64, "JPEG", hX + 3, y, 18, 12); } catch {} }

    const insX = midX - 8;
    if (insigniaB64) { try { pdf.addImage(insigniaB64, "PNG", insX, y, 16, 16); } catch {} }

    const vx = hX + HALF - 6 - 34;
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.45); pdf.setDrawColor(...BK);
    pdf.rect(vx, y, 34, 22);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(7); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Visto", vx + 17, y + 5, { align: "center" });
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(6);
    pdf.text("O Subdirector", vx + 17, y + 9, { align: "center" });
    pdf.text("Pedagógico", vx + 17, y + 12.5, { align: "center" });
    if (subDir) { pdf.setFont("helvetica", "italic"); pdf.text(subDir, vx + 17, y + 16.5, { align: "center", maxWidth: 32 }); }
    pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.line(vx + 4, y + 20, vx + 30, y + 20);

    const instY = y + 18;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6.8); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    ["REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA", "MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO / SAÚDE", school?.name || ""].forEach((l, i) =>
      pdf.text(l, midX, instY + i * 3.7, { align: "center" })
    );

    y = Math.max(instY + 14, y + 30);
    y += 3;
    const indTitle = (bulletinTitle || "BOLETIM DE NOTAS").toUpperCase();
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(10.5); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text(indTitle, midX, y, { align: "center" });
    const tw = pdf.getTextWidth(indTitle);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.4); pdf.line(midX - tw / 2, y + 0.9, midX + tw / 2, y + 0.9);
    y += 8;

    const infoH = 16;
    pdf.setFillColor(...INFO_BG_PDF); pdf.setDrawColor(...INFO_BORDER_PDF); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.roundedRect(cX, y, cW, infoH, 2, 2, "FD");
    const hw = cW / 2;

    const r1y = y + 3.5;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(7); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Nome: ", cX + 2, r1y);
    pdf.setTextColor(204, 0, 0);
    pdf.text(student.student_name || "", cX + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Nome: "), r1y);
    pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Turma: ", cX + hw + 2, r1y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    pdf.text(cls?.title || cls?.code || "", cX + hw + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Turma: "), r1y);

    const r2y = y + 3.5 + 4.2;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.text("Nº: ", cX + 2, r2y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.text(student.student_code || "", cX + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Nº: "), r2y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.text("Período: ", cX + hw + 2, r2y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.text(student.period_label || student.period || "", cX + hw + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Período: "), r2y);

    const r3y = y + 3.5 + 8.4;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.text("Ano lectivo: ", cX + 2, r3y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.text(cls?.academic_year || "", cX + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Ano lectivo: "), r3y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.text("Sala: ", cX + hw + 2, r3y);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.text(cls?.classroom || "", cX + hw + 2 + pdf.getTextWidth("Sala: "), r3y);
    y += infoH + 4;

    const sec = `DESCRIMINAÇÃO DAS NOTAS – ${qLabel.toUpperCase()}`;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(7); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text(sec, midX, y, { align: "center" });
    const sw = pdf.getTextWidth(sec);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.3); pdf.line(midX - sw / 2, y + 0.6, midX + sw / 2, y + 0.6);
    y += 5;

    const head = isMac
      ? [["DISCIPLINAS", "MAC", "NPT", "MT", "FAL"]]
      : [["ÁREA CURRICULAR", "CLAS. FINAL", "COMPORT.", "OBS:"]];

    const body = grades.map(g => isMac
      ? [g.subject_title, fmt(g.mac, zeroFill), fmt(g.npt, zeroFill), fmt(g.mt, zeroFill), String(g.absences ?? 0)]
      : [g.subject_title, fmt(g.mt, zeroFill), "", ""]
    );
    body.push(
      isMac ? ["", "", "", "", ""] : ["", "", "", ""],
      isMac ? ["", "", "", "", ""] : ["", "", "", ""],
    );
    if (isMac) {
      body.push(
        ["FALTAS POR INDISCIPLINA", "", "", "", ""],
        ["FALTAS POR NÃO COMPARÊNCIA", "", "", "", ""],
        ["COMPORTAMENTO", "", "", "", ""],
      );
    }

    autoTable(pdf, {
      startY: y,
      margin: { left: cX, right: PW - cX - cW },
      tableWidth: cW,
      head, body,
      theme: "grid",
      styles: { fontSize: 7, cellPadding: 1.5, lineColor: [0, 0, 0], lineWidth: 0.3, textColor: [0, 0, 0], font: "helvetica" },
      headStyles: { fillColor: [255, 255, 255], textColor: [0, 0, 0], fontStyle: "bold", halign: "center", fontSize: 7.5, lineWidth: 0.3, lineColor: [0, 0, 0] },
      columnStyles: { 0: { halign: "left" }, 1: { halign: "center" }, 2: { halign: "center" }, 3: { halign: "center" }, 4: { halign: "center" } },
      didParseCell(d) {
        if (d.section !== "body") return;
        const ri = d.row.index, ci = d.column.index, ng = grades.length;
        if (!isMac) {
          if (ri < ng && ci === 1) {
            const v = grades[ri]?.mt;
            d.cell.styles.textColor = gcR(v !== null && v !== undefined ? v : (zeroFill ? 0 : v));
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold";
          }
          return;
        }
        if (ri < ng && ci >= 1 && ci <= 4) {
          if (ci === 4) {
            const abs = grades[ri]?.absences ?? 0;
            d.cell.styles.textColor = abs > 0 ? [204, 0, 0] : [80, 80, 80];
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = abs > 0 ? "bold" : "normal";
          } else {
            const v = [grades[ri]?.mac, grades[ri]?.npt, grades[ri]?.mt][ci - 1];
            d.cell.styles.textColor = gcR(v !== null && v !== undefined ? v : (zeroFill ? 0 : v));
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold";
          }
        }
        if (ri >= ng + 2 && ci === 0) { d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold"; }
      },
    });

    const tEY = pdf.lastAutoTable.finalY;

    pdf.setFillColor(255, 255, 255); pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.38);
    pdf.rect(cX, tEY, cW, 6, "FD");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Para os devidos efeitos, passou-se o presente boletim de notas que vai por mim assinado.", cX + 2, tEY + 4, { maxWidth: cW - 3 });

    pdf.setFillColor(255, 255, 255);
    pdf.rect(cX, tEY + 6, cW, 5, "FD");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "italic"); pdf.setTextColor(0, 0, 0);
    pdf.text(`${cidade ? cidade + ", aos " : ""}${todayPT()}`, midX, tEY + 9.5, { align: "center" });

    const fY = PH - 10;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(7); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("O (A) Director(a) de Turma", hX + 30, fY, { align: "center" });
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.4); pdf.line(hX + 8, fY + 1.5, hX + 52, fY + 1.5);
    if (cls?.director_name) {
      pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5);
      pdf.text(cls.director_name, hX + 30, fY + 5, { align: "center" });
    }
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(7);
    pdf.text("O (A) Encarregado(a)", hX + HALF - 30, fY, { align: "center" });
    pdf.line(hX + HALF - 52, fY + 1.5, hX + HALF - 8, fY + 1.5);
  }

  function drawAnn(student, school, cls, subDir, hX, logoB64) {
    const rawGrades = student.grades || [];
    const grades    = applySubjectOrder(rawGrades, subjectOrder);
    const cidade    = school?.state || "";
    const finSit    = student.overrideSituation ?? student.summary?.final_situation ?? "TRANSITA";
    const progLbl   = student.progressLabel ?? "";
    const bCols = ["mt1", "mt2", "mt3", "mdf", "fal"].map(c => student.behaviourByCol?.[c] || "");
    const lW = 38, rX = hX + lW + 2, rW = HALF - lW - 9;
    let y = 8;

    pdf.setDrawColor(120, 120, 120); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.rect(hX + 0.5, 0.5, HALF - 1, PH - 1);

    if (logoB64) { try { pdf.addImage(logoB64, "JPEG", hX + 9, y, 20, 12); } catch {} }

    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(5.8); pdf.setTextColor(40, 40, 40);
    pdf.text("REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA",    hX + lW / 2, y + 14,   { align: "center" });
    pdf.text("MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO", hX + lW / 2, y + 17.5, { align: "center" });
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text(`"${school?.name || ""}"`, hX + lW / 2, y + 21.5, { align: "center", maxWidth: lW - 2 });
    pdf.setFontSize(5.5);
    pdf.text("BOLETIM DE NOTAS DO ENSINO PRIMÁRIO", hX + lW / 2, y + 26, { align: "center", maxWidth: lW - 2 });
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(6);
    pdf.text("O Director Geral", hX + lW / 2, y + 33, { align: "center" });
    pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.25);
    pdf.line(hX + 4, y + 37, hX + lW - 4, y + 37);
    if (school?.principal) pdf.text(school.principal, hX + lW / 2, y + 40.5, { align: "center", maxWidth: lW - 2 });
    pdf.line(hX + 3, y + 44, hX + lW - 3, y + 44);

    let ly = y + 49;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5); pdf.setTextColor(192, 0, 0);
    pdf.text(student.student_name || "", hX + 3, ly, { maxWidth: lW - 4 });
    ly += 5;
    pdf.setTextColor(...BK); pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(5.8);
    const turnoPrint = cls?.period ? `Turno: ${cls.period}` : "";
    [`Classe: ${cls?.title || ""}`, `Sala: ${cls?.classroom || ""}`, `Turma: ${cls?.code || ""}`, turnoPrint].filter(Boolean)
      .forEach((t, i) => pdf.text(t, hX + 3, ly + i * 4.3));

    pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.line(hX + lW + 1, 0.5, hX + lW + 1, PH - 0.5);

    pdf.setFillColor(240, 240, 240); pdf.rect(rX, y, rW, 8, "F");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(8.5); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text((bulletinTitle || "BOLETIM ANUAL").toUpperCase(), rX + rW / 2, y + 5.8, { align: "center" });
    y += 10;

    autoTable(pdf, {
      startY: y,
      margin: { left: rX, right: PW - rX - rW },
      tableWidth: rW,
      head: [["DISCIPLINAS", "MT1", "MT2", "MT3", "MDF", "FAL"]],
      body: [
        ...grades.map(g => {
          const mdf = resolveMdf(g, zeroFill);
          return [
            g.subject_title,
            fmt(g.mt1, zeroFill), fmt(g.mt2, zeroFill), fmt(g.mt3, zeroFill),
            fmtMdf(mdf, zeroFill),
            String(g.absences ?? 0),
          ];
        }),
        ["Avaliação do Comportamento", ...bCols],
      ],
      theme: "grid",
      styles: { fontSize: 6.5, cellPadding: 1, lineColor: [0, 0, 0], lineWidth: 0.35, font: "helvetica" },
      headStyles: { fillColor: [255, 255, 255], textColor: [0, 0, 0], fontStyle: "bold", halign: "center", fontSize: 6.5, lineWidth: 0.42, lineColor: [0, 0, 0] },
      columnStyles: { 0: { halign: "left" }, 1: { halign: "center" }, 2: { halign: "center" }, 3: { halign: "center" }, 4: { halign: "center" }, 5: { halign: "center" } },
      didParseCell(d) {
        const ri = d.row.index, ci = d.column.index, ng = grades.length;
        if (d.section !== "body") return;
        if (ri < ng) {
          if (ci === 0) { d.cell.styles.textColor = [0, 0, 0]; d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "normal"; }
          if (ci >= 1 && ci <= 3) {
            const v = [grades[ri]?.mt1, grades[ri]?.mt2, grades[ri]?.mt3][ci - 1];
            d.cell.styles.textColor = gcR(v !== null && v !== undefined ? v : (zeroFill ? 0 : v));
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold";
          }
          if (ci === 4) {
            const mdf = resolveMdf(grades[ri], zeroFill);
            d.cell.styles.textColor = mdf !== null ? gcR(mdf) : [17, 17, 17];
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold";
          }
          if (ci === 5) {
            const abs = grades[ri]?.absences ?? 0;
            d.cell.styles.textColor = abs > 0 ? [204, 0, 0] : [80, 80, 80];
            d.cell.styles.fontStyle = abs > 0 ? "bold" : "normal";
          }
        }
        if (ri === ng) {
          d.cell.styles.fillColor = [240, 240, 240];
          d.cell.styles.halign    = ci === 0 ? "left" : "center";
          d.cell.styles.fontStyle = "bold";
          if (ci > 0) {
            const colLvl = bCols[ci - 1];
            if (colLvl) {
              const lvlColors = { NS: [204, 0, 0], S: [113, 63, 18], B: [22, 101, 52], MB: [30, 64, 175], E: [91, 33, 182] };
              d.cell.styles.textColor = lvlColors[colLvl] || [0, 0, 0];
            } else {
              d.cell.styles.textColor = [240, 240, 240];
            }
          }
        }
      },
    });

    let ty = pdf.lastAutoTable.finalY;
    const rH = 9;

    pdf.setFillColor(0, 176, 240); pdf.rect(rX + rW * 0.37, ty, rW * 0.63, rH, "F");
    pdf.setFillColor(255, 255, 255); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW * 0.37, rH, "F");
    pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.35); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW, rH);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(5.8); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Obs. Progressão\nqualitativa do aluno na aula", rX + 1.5, ty + 3.5, { maxWidth: rW * 0.34, lineHeightFactor: 1.3 });
    if (progLbl) {
      pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(255, 255, 255);
      pdf.text(progLbl.toUpperCase().split("").join(" "), rX + rW * 0.69, ty + 6, { align: "center" });
    }
    ty += rH;

    pdf.setFillColor(255, 255, 255); pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.setLineWidth(0.35);
    pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW, 9, "FD");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text("Assinatura do Encarregado de Educação", rX + 1.5, ty + 5);
    ty += 9;

    const isTransita = finSit === "TRANSITA";
    const sitLblRgb  = isTransita ? [112, 173, 71] : [204, 0, 0];
    const sitValRgb  = isTransita ? [255, 255, 255] : [255, 244, 244];
    const sitTxtRgb  = isTransita ? [0, 0, 0]       : [204, 0, 0];
    pdf.setFillColor(...sitLblRgb); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW * 0.37, 7, "F");
    pdf.setFillColor(...sitValRgb); pdf.rect(rX + rW * 0.37, ty, rW * 0.63, 7, "F");
    pdf.setDrawColor(...BK); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW, 7);
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5); pdf.setTextColor(255, 255, 255);
    pdf.text("Situação final", rX + 2, ty + 4.5);
    pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(...sitTxtRgb);
    pdf.text(finSit.split("").join(" "), rX + rW * 0.69, ty + 5, { align: "center" });
    ty += 7;

    pdf.setFillColor(255, 255, 255); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW, 5, "FD");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text(`${cidade ? cidade + ", aos " : ""}${todayPT()}`, rX + rW / 2, ty + 3.5, { align: "center" });
    ty += 5;

    pdf.setFillColor(242, 242, 242); pdf.rect(rX, ty, rW, 6, "FD");
    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(6.5);
    pdf.text(`O Sub-Director Pedagógico${subDir ? " — " + subDir : ""}`, rX + rW / 2, ty + 4.2, { align: "center" });
  }

  const students      = payload.students || [];
  const school        = payload.school   || {};
  const cls           = payload.class    || {};
  const subDir        = payload.sub_director_pedagogical || "";
  const schoolLogoB64 = await imgFetch(school.logo_url);
  const insigniaSrc   = customLogoUrl || school.coat_of_arms_url || ANGOLA_COAT;
  const insigniaB64   = await imgFetch(insigniaSrc);

  for (let i = 0; i < students.length; i += 2) {
    if (i > 0) pdf.addPage([PW, PH], "landscape");
    drawHalfDivider();
    const L = students[i], R = students[i + 1] || null;
    if (isAnnual) {
      drawAnn(L, school, cls, subDir, 0,    schoolLogoB64);
      if (R) drawAnn(R, school, cls, subDir, HALF, schoolLogoB64);
    } else {
      drawInd(L, school, cls, subDir, 0,    schoolLogoB64, insigniaB64);
      if (R) drawInd(R, school, cls, subDir, HALF, schoolLogoB64, insigniaB64);
    }
  }

  pdf.save(`Boletins_${bulletinType}_${moment().format("YYYY-MM-DD_HH-mm")}.pdf`);
}
