// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MAIN COMPONENT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export default function BulletinExportComponent() {
  document.title = "Boletins de Notas";

  const [loading,           setLoading]           = useState(false);
  const [exportingPdf,      setExportingPdf]      = useState(false);
  const [allClasses,        setAllClasses]        = useState([]);
  const [selectedClass,     setSelectedClass]     = useState("");
  const [bulletinType,      setBulletinType]      = useState("individual");
  const [indType,           setIndType]           = useState("mac");
  const [roundMode,         setRoundMode]         = useState("default");
  const [quarter,           setQuarter]           = useState(1);
  const [studentsOptions,   setStudentsOptions]   = useState([]);
  const [selectedStudents,  setSelectedStudents]  = useState([]);
  const [payload,           setPayload]           = useState(null);
  const [logoFile,          setLogoFile]          = useState(null);
  const [logoPreviewUrl,    setLogoPreviewUrl]    = useState("");
  const [fieldVisibility,   setFieldVisibility]   = useState({
    studentName: true, className: true, classroom: true, period: true,
    directorName: true, academicYear: true, principal: true,
  });
  const [editMode,          setEditMode]          = useState(false);
  const [studentOverrides,  setStudentOverrides]  = useState({});
  const [bulletinTitle,     setBulletinTitle]     = useState("BOLETIM ANUAL");
  const [sortOrder,         setSortOrder]         = useState("none");
  const [zeroFill,          setZeroFill]          = useState(false);

  // Subject ordering state
  // subjectOrder is an array of subject IDs in the desired display order
  const [subjectOrder,      setSubjectOrder]      = useState([]);
  // allSubjects: [{id, title}] extracted from first loaded payload
  const [allSubjects,       setAllSubjects]       = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    axios.get(API.classes())
      .then(r => setAllClasses(Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data.map(c => ({ value: c.ed_class_id, label: c.ed_class_title })) : []))
      .catch(() => setAllClasses([]));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!logoFile) { setLogoPreviewUrl(""); return; }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(logoFile);
    setLogoPreviewUrl(url);
    return () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, [logoFile]);

  const handleClassChange = (e) => {
    const val = e.target.value;
    setSelectedClass(val);
    setPayload(null);
    setStudentOverrides({});
    setEditMode(false);
    setSubjectOrder([]);
    setAllSubjects([]);
    if (!val) { setStudentsOptions([]); setSelectedStudents([]); return; }
    axios.get(API.studentsByClass(val)).then(r => {
      const list = (r.data || []).map(s => ({ value: String(s.ed_student_id), label: s.ed_student_name }));
      setStudentsOptions(list);
      setSelectedStudents(list);
    }).catch(() => { setStudentsOptions([]); setSelectedStudents([]); });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedClass || !selectedStudents?.length) { setPayload(null); return; }
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    setStudentOverrides({});

    const body = { classId: Number(selectedClass), studentIds: selectedStudents.map(s => Number(s.value)), roundMode };
    if (bulletinType === "individual") body.quarter = quarter;
    const url = bulletinType === "individual" ? API.bulletinIndividual() : API.bulletinAnnual();

    axios.post(url, body).then(res => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      if (res.data?.success) {
        setPayload(res.data);
        // Extract subjects from first student for ordering UI
        const firstStudent = (res.data.students || [])[0];
        if (firstStudent?.grades?.length) {
          const subjs = firstStudent.grades.map(g => ({
            id: g.subject_id ?? g.subject_title, // fallback to title if no id
            title: g.subject_title,
          }));
          setAllSubjects(subjs);
          // Only reset order if it's empty or class changed
          setSubjectOrder(prev =>
            prev.length === 0 ? subjs.map(s => s.id) : prev
          );
        }
      } else {
        toast.error(res.data?.message || "Erro.");
        setPayload(null);
      }
    }).catch(() => {
      if (!cancelled) { toast.error("Erro de comunicação."); setPayload(null); }
    }).finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setLoading(false); });

    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [selectedClass, selectedStudents, bulletinType, roundMode, quarter]);

  const handleProgressChange = (studentId, value) => {
    setStudentOverrides(prev => ({ ...prev, [studentId]: { ...prev[studentId], progressLabel: value } }));
  };
  const handleSituationChange = (studentId, value) => {
    setStudentOverrides(prev => ({ ...prev, [studentId]: { ...prev[studentId], overrideSituation: value } }));
  };
  const handleBehaviourChange = (studentId, column, value) => {
    setStudentOverrides(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [studentId]: {
        ...prev[studentId],
        behaviourByCol: { ...(prev[studentId]?.behaviourByCol || {}), [column]: value },
      },
    }));
  };
  const handleFieldVisibilityToggle = (field) => {
    setFieldVisibility(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: !prev[field] }));
  };

  const passValue = payload?.pass_value ?? 10;
  const maxValue  = payload?.max_value  ?? 20;

  const studentsWithOverrides = (payload?.students || []).map(s => {
    const ov = studentOverrides[s.student_id] || {};
    const grades = s.grades || [];
    const autoByCol = {
      mt1: calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mt1", passValue, maxValue),
      mt2: calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mt2", passValue, maxValue),
      mt3: calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mt3", passValue, maxValue),
      mdf: calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mdf", passValue, maxValue),
      fal: calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "fal", passValue, maxValue),
      mt:  calcBehaviourForColumn(grades, "mt",  passValue, maxValue),
    };
    return {
      ...s,
      progressLabel:     ov.progressLabel     ?? "",
      overrideSituation: ov.overrideSituation ?? undefined,
      behaviourByCol:    ov.behaviourByCol    ?? {},
      autoByCol,
      autoBehaviour: autoByCol.mdf,
    };
  });

  const avgMdf = (s) => {
    const grades = s.grades || [];
    const vals = grades.map(g => {
      const v = resolveMdf(g, zeroFill);
      if (v !== null) return v;
      return g.mt !== null && g.mt !== undefined ? g.mt : null;
    }).filter(v => v !== null);
    return vals.length ? vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length : 0;
  };
  const totalAbs = (s) => (s.grades || []).reduce((acc, g) => acc + (g.absences ?? 0), 0);

  const sortedStudents = [...studentsWithOverrides].sort((a, b) => {
    switch (sortOrder) {
      case "grade_asc":  return avgMdf(a) - avgMdf(b);
      case "grade_desc": return avgMdf(b) - avgMdf(a);
      case "abs_asc":    return totalAbs(a) - totalAbs(b);
      case "abs_desc":   return totalAbs(b) - totalAbs(a);
      case "name_asc":   return (a.student_name || "").localeCompare(b.student_name || "", "pt");
      case "name_desc":  return (b.student_name || "").localeCompare(a.student_name || "", "pt");
      default:           return 0;
    }
  });

  const nStudents = sortedStudents.length;
  const nSheets   = Math.ceil(nStudents / 2);
  const qLabel    = QUARTER_OPTIONS.find(q => q.value === quarter)?.label || "1º Trimestre";

  const shared = payload ? {
    bulletinType, indType, passValue, maxValue,
    school: payload.school || {},
    cls: payload.class || {},
    subDir: payload.sub_director_pedagogical || "",
    quarter: payload.quarter || quarter,
    customLogoUrl: logoPreviewUrl || null,
    fieldVisibility,
  } : {};

  const handleExportPDF = async () => {
    if (!payload) return;
    setExportingPdf(true);
    try {
      const payloadWithOverrides = { ...payload, students: sortedStudents };
      await exportPDF(payloadWithOverrides, bulletinType, indType, quarter, logoPreviewUrl || null, zeroFill, bulletinTitle, subjectOrder);
      toast.success("PDF exportado!");
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      toast.error("Erro ao exportar PDF.");
    } finally {
      setExportingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  const hasOverrides  = Object.keys(studentOverrides).length > 0;
  const overrideCount = Object.values(studentOverrides).filter(o =>
    o.progressLabel || o.overrideSituation || Object.keys(o.behaviourByCol || {}).length > 0
  ).length;

  return (
    <DashboardContainerWrapper content={
      <div className="bec-root">
        <style>{UI_CSS}</style>

        <div className="bec-topbar">
          <div className="bec-topbar-icon"><TbReportAnalytics size={22} color="#fff" /></div>
          <div>
            <div className="bec-topbar-title">Boletins de Notas</div>
            <div className="bec-topbar-sub">Boletim de notas, anual, trimestral individual</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* BULLETIN TYPE */}
        <div className="bec-panel">
          <div className="bec-phdr">
            <div className="bec-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineDescription size={14} /></div>
            <span className="bec-pttl">Tipo de Boletim</span>
          </div>
          <div className="bec-pbody">
            <div className="bec-pills" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
              {[{ v: "individual", l: "Individual" }, { v: "annual", l: "Anual" }].map(o => (
                <button key={o.v} className={`bec-pill${bulletinType === o.v ? " pp" : ""}`} onClick={() => {
                  setBulletinType(o.v);
                  setEditMode(false);
                  setStudentOverrides({});
                  setBulletinTitle(o.v === "annual" ? "BOLETIM ANUAL" : "BOLETIM DE NOTAS");
                }}>
                  {bulletinType === o.v ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14} /> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14} />} {o.l}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            {bulletinType === "individual" && (
              <>
                <div className="bec-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>Formato das notas</div>
                <div className="bec-pills">
                  {[{ v: "mac", l: "MAC / NPT / MT" }, { v: "final", l: "Classificação Final" }].map(o => (
                    <button key={o.v} className={`bec-pill${indType === o.v ? " pd" : ""}`} onClick={() => setIndType(o.v)}>
                      {indType === o.v ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14} /> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14} />} {o.l}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SETTINGS */}
        <div className="bec-panel">
          <div className="bec-phdr">
            <div className="bec-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineTune size={14} /></div>
            <span className="bec-pttl">Configurações</span>
          </div>

          <div className="bec-pbody">
            <div className="bec-row">
              <div className="bec-col">
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlineClass size={13} /> Turma</div>
                <select className="bec-sel" value={selectedClass} onChange={handleClassChange}>
                  <option value="">Selecione uma turma…</option>
                  {allClasses.map(c => <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>{c.label}</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="bec-col">
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlineTune size={13} /> Arredondamento</div>
                <select className="bec-sel" value={roundMode} onChange={e => setRoundMode(e.target.value)}>
                  <option value="default">Por defeito</option>
                  <option value="ceil">Por excesso</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Zero-fill */}
          <div className="bec-divider" />
          <div className="bec-pbody-sm">
            <div className="bec-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>Campos sem notas lançadas</div>
            <div className="bec-pills">
              <button className={`bec-pill${!zeroFill ? " pg" : ""}`} onClick={() => setZeroFill(false)}>
                {!zeroFill ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14} /> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14} />} Deixar vazio
              </button>
              <button className={`bec-pill${zeroFill ? " pt" : ""}`} onClick={() => setZeroFill(true)}>
                {zeroFill ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14} /> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14} />} Preencher com zero (0)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {bulletinType === "individual" && (
            <>
              <div className="bec-divider" />
              <div className="bec-pbody-sm">
                <div className="bec-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}><MdOutlineCalendarToday size={13} /> Trimestre</div>
                <div className="bec-pills">
                  {QUARTER_OPTIONS.map(q => (
                    <button key={q.value} className={`bec-pill${quarter === q.value ? " pg" : ""}`} onClick={() => setQuarter(q.value)}>
                      {q.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* Bulletin title + student sort order */}
          <div className="bec-divider" />
          <div className="bec-pbody">
            <div className="bec-row">
              <div className="bec-col" style={{ flex: 2 }}>
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlineDescription size={13} /> Título do Boletim</div>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={bulletinTitle}
                  onChange={e => setBulletinTitle(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder={bulletinType === "annual" ? "BOLETIM ANUAL" : "BOLETIM DE NOTAS"}
                  maxLength={60}
                  style={{
                    width: "100%", padding: "9px 12px",
                    border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10,
                    fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "'DM Sans',sans-serif",
                    color: "#0f172a", background: "#fff", outline: "none",
                    boxSizing: "border-box", textTransform: "uppercase",
                    fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: "0.5px",
                  }}
                  onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
                  onBlur={e => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}
                />
                <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: "#94a3b8", marginTop: 4 }}>Aplicado a todos os boletins gerados</div>
              </div>
              <div className="bec-col">
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlineTune size={13} /> Ordenação dos alunos</div>
                <select className="bec-sel" value={sortOrder} onChange={e => setSortOrder(e.target.value)}>
                  <option value="none">— Sem ordenação —</option>
                  <optgroup label="Por nota">
                    <option value="grade_desc">Nota mais alta primeiro</option>
                    <option value="grade_asc">Nota mais baixa primeiro</option>
                  </optgroup>
                  <optgroup label="Por faltas">
                    <option value="abs_desc">Mais faltas primeiro</option>
                    <option value="abs_asc">Menos faltas primeiro</option>
                  </optgroup>
                  <optgroup label="Alfabética">
                    <option value="name_asc">Nome A → Z</option>
                    <option value="name_desc">Nome Z → A</option>
                  </optgroup>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ── SUBJECT REORDER ── */}
          {allSubjects.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="bec-divider" />
              <div className="bec-pbody-sm">
                <div className="bec-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
                  <MdSortByAlpha size={13} /> Ordenação das Disciplinas
                  <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 400, color: "#94a3b8", background: "#f1f5f9", borderRadius: 4, padding: "1px 6px" }}>
                    Arraste ou use A→Z / Z→A
                  </span>
                </div>
                <SubjectReorderPanel
                  subjects={allSubjects}
                  order={subjectOrder}
                  onOrderChange={setSubjectOrder}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* Students + Logo */}
          <div className="bec-divider" />
          <div className="bec-pbody">
            <div className="bec-row">
              <div className="bec-col" style={{ flex: 2 }}>
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlinePersonSearch size={13} /> Alunos</div>
                {selectedClass ? (
                  <StudentMultiSelect options={studentsOptions} value={selectedStudents} onChange={setSelectedStudents} />
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 12.5, color: "#94a3b8", padding: "10px 0" }}>Selecione uma turma para carregar os alunos.</div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="bec-col">
                <div className="bec-lbl"><MdOutlineImage size={13} /> Insignia personalizada</div>
                <div className="bec-file-wrap">
                  <div className="bec-file-btn"><MdOutlineImage size={15} />{logoFile ? logoFile.name : "Carregar insignia…"}</div>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="bec-file-input" onChange={e => setLogoFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
                </div>
                {logoFile && (
                  <span className="bec-logo-chip">
                    <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={12} />
                    {logoFile.name.length > 22 ? logoFile.name.slice(0, 22) + "…" : logoFile.name}
                    <button className="bec-logo-x" onClick={() => setLogoFile(null)}>×</button>
                  </span>
                )}
                {logoPreviewUrl && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <img src={logoPreviewUrl} alt="Preview" style={{ width: 32, height: 32, objectFit: "contain", border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 6 }} />
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8" }}>Substituirá a insignia de Angola</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bec-divider" />
          <div className="bec-pbody">
            <FieldVisibilityControl visible={fieldVisibility} onToggle={handleFieldVisibilityToggle} />
          </div>

          {(nStudents > 0 || loading) && (
            <>
              <div className="bec-divider" />
              {editMode && (
                <div className="bec-edit-banner">
                  <div className="bec-edit-banner-icon"><MdOutlineEdit size={13} color="#fff" /></div>
                  <span>
                    Modo de edição activo — pode alterar o Comportamento{bulletinType === "annual" ? ", a Progressão qualitativa e a Situação Final" : ""} de cada aluno.
                    {hasOverrides && overrideCount > 0 && (
                      <strong style={{ marginLeft: 6, color: "#92400e" }}>
                        {overrideCount} aluno{overrideCount !== 1 ? "s" : ""} com alterações.
                      </strong>
                    )}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="bec-action-bar">
                <div className="bec-stats">
                  {loading ? (
                    <span className="bec-chip cs"><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 12, height: 12, borderWidth: 2 }} /> A gerar…</span>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span className="bec-chip cp">{nStudents} aluno{nStudents !== 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>
                      <span className="bec-chip cg">{nSheets} folha{nSheets !== 1 ? "s" : ""} A4</span>
                      {bulletinType === "individual" && <span className="bec-chip cs">{qLabel}</span>}
                      {zeroFill && <span className="bec-chip ct">zeros</span>}
                      {subjectOrder.length > 0 && <span className="bec-chip cs">{subjectOrder.length} disciplinas ordenadas</span>}
                      {hasOverrides && overrideCount > 0 && (
                        <span className="bec-chip cy"><MdOutlineEdit size={11} /> {overrideCount} editado{overrideCount !== 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="bec-action-btns">
                  {bulletinType === "annual" && !loading && payload && (
                    <button className={`bec-edit-btn${editMode ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => setEditMode(v => !v)}>
                      {editMode ? <MdOutlineEditOff size={15} /> : <MdOutlineEdit size={15} />}
                      {editMode ? "Fechar Edição" : "Editar Boletins"}
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button className="bec-pdf-btn" onClick={handleExportPDF} disabled={exportingPdf || loading || !payload}>
                    {exportingPdf
                      ? <><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 14, height: 14, borderWidth: 2 }} /> A gerar PDF…</>
                      : <><MdOutlinePictureAsPdf size={17} /> Exportar PDF</>}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* PREVIEW */}
        <CustomTableContainer
          title={
            bulletinType === "individual"
              ? `Boletins Individuais — ${qLabel} · ${indType === "mac" ? "MAC/NPT/MT" : "Classificação Final"}${editMode ? " · Modo de Edição" : ""}`
              : `Boletins Anuais${editMode ? " · Modo de Edição" : ""}`
          }
          content={
            loading ? (
              <div className="bec-loading"><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" /> A gerar boletins…</div>
            ) : studentsWithOverrides.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="bec-empty">
                <div className="bec-empty-icon"><MdOutlineFileDownload size={24} /></div>
                <div className="bec-empty-title">Nenhum boletim gerado</div>
                <div className="bec-empty-sub">{selectedClass ? "Selecione pelo menos um aluno." : "Selecione uma turma para começar."}</div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="bec-prev-area">
                <div className={`bec-prev-flex${editMode && bulletinType === "annual" ? " edit-mode" : ""}`}>
                  {sortedStudents.map((student, idx) => {
                    const isEditAnnual = editMode && bulletinType === "annual";
                    return (
                      <div key={idx} style={isEditAnnual ? { width: "100%" } : {}}>
                        {isEditAnnual && (
                          <div className="bec-edit-student-label">
                            <div className="bec-edit-student-num">{idx + 1}</div>
                            <span>{student.student_name}</span>
                            {student.student_code && <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8", fontWeight: 400 }}>#{student.student_code}</span>}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className={`bec-blt-wrap${isEditAnnual ? " edit-mode" : ""}`}>
                          <div className={`bec-blt-scaler${isEditAnnual ? " edit-mode" : ""}`}>
                            {bulletinType === "annual"
                              ? <AnnualBulletin
                                  data={student}
                                  school={shared.school}
                                  cls={shared.cls}
                                  subDir={shared.subDir}
                                  customLogoUrl={shared.customLogoUrl}
                                  passValue={passValue}
                                  maxValue={maxValue}
                                  editMode={editMode}
                                  onProgressChange={handleProgressChange}
                                  onSituationChange={handleSituationChange}
                                  onBehaviourChange={handleBehaviourChange}
                                  fieldVisibility={fieldVisibility}
                                  zeroFill={zeroFill}
                                  behaviourByCol={student.behaviourByCol}
                                  autoByCol={student.autoByCol}
                                  bulletinTitle={bulletinTitle}
                                  subjectOrder={subjectOrder}
                                />
                              : <IndividualBulletin
                                  data={student}
                                  school={shared.school}
                                  cls={shared.cls}
                                  subDir={shared.subDir}
                                  indType={shared.indType}
                                  quarter={shared.quarter}
                                  customLogoUrl={shared.customLogoUrl}
                                  passValue={passValue}
                                  fieldVisibility={fieldVisibility}
                                  zeroFill={zeroFill}
                                  behaviourByCol={student.behaviourByCol}
                                  autoByCol={student.autoByCol}
                                  bulletinTitle={bulletinTitle}
                                  editMode={editMode}
                                  onBehaviourChange={handleBehaviourChange}
                                  subjectOrder={subjectOrder}
                                />
                            }
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          }
        />
      </div>
    } />
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CONSTANTES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const PURPLE_HEX   = "var(--ed-purple)";
const PURPLE_RAW   = "var(--ed-purple)";
const BLUE_POS_CSS = "#072AC8";
const BLUE_POS_RGB = [7, 42, 200];
const RED_CSS      = "#cc0000";
const RED_RGB      = [204, 0, 0];
const F_SERIF      = "'Times New Roman', Times, serif";
const F_SANS       = "'DM Sans', sans-serif";
const ANGOLA_COAT  = "https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9d/Coat_of_arms_of_Angola.svg/800px-Coat_of_arms_of_Angola.svg.png";
const PERIODS      = ["Manhã", "Tarde", "Nocturno"];

// Dois modelos de certificado
const CERT_MODELS = [
  { value: "primario",    label: "Ensino Primário",              subtitle: "I / II / III Ciclo" },
  { value: "secundario",  label: "I Ciclo do Ensino Secundário", subtitle: "7ª / 8ª / 9ª Classe" },
];

// Colunas pré-definidas por modelo
const DEFAULT_COLS_PRIMARIO = [
  { id: "c1", label: "I Ciclo",   sublabel: "2ª Classe" },
  { id: "c2", label: "II Ciclo",  sublabel: "4ª Classe" },
  { id: "c3", label: "III Ciclo", sublabel: "6ª Classe" },
];
const DEFAULT_COLS_SECUNDARIO = [
  { id: "c1", label: "7ª Classe", sublabel: "" },
  { id: "c2", label: "8ª Classe", sublabel: "" },
  { id: "c3", label: "9ª Classe", sublabel: "" },
];

// Disciplinas pré-definidas por modelo
const PRESET_SUBJ_PRIMARIO = [
  "Língua Portuguesa","Matemática","Estudo do Meio","Ciências da Natureza",
  "História","Geografia","Educação Moral e Cívica","Educação Manual e Plástica",
  "Educação Musical","Educação Física","Línguas de Origem Africana",
];
const PRESET_SUBJ_SECUNDARIO = [
  "Língua Portuguesa","Língua Inglesa","Língua Francesa","Matemática",
  "Biologia","Física","Química","Geografia","História",
  "Educação Física","Educação Moral e Cívica","Educação Visual e Plástica",
  "Educação Laboral","Empreendedorismo",
];

// Disciplinas sem média (só traço) — Empreendedorismo e Línguas de Origem Africana
const NO_GRADE_SUBJECTS = ["empreendedorismo","línguas de origem africana"];

/* ── Endpoints ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const API = {
  classes:         ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallclassapi/get`,
  studentsByClass: (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallgetstudentsbyclass/${id}`,
  studentUpdate:   (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallstudentupdate/update/${id}`,
  studentGet:      (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallsinglestudentapi/get/${id}`,
  subjects:        ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallsubjectsapi/get/`,
  academicLevels:  ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallacademiclevelsapi/get/`,
  institute:       ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallgetcurentuserinstitute/get/`,
  certs:           ()   => `${Hoot()}eduall-certificates/get`,
  certSave:        ()   => `${Hoot()}eduall-certificates/post`,
  certUpd:         (id) => `${Hoot()}eduall-certificates/update/${id}`,
};

const MESES = ["Janeiro","Fevereiro","Março","Abril","Maio","Junho",
               "Julho","Agosto","Setembro","Outubro","Novembro","Dezembro"];
const formatDatePT = (iso) => {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return iso;
  return `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")} de ${MESES[d.getMonth()]} de ${d.getFullYear()}`;
};
const todayPT = () => { const d = new Date(); return `${d.getDate()} de ${MESES[d.getMonth()]} de ${d.getFullYear()}`; };

/* ── Números por extenso ──────────────────────────────────── */
const UNITS = ["","Um","Dois","Três","Quatro","Cinco","Seis","Sete","Oito","Nove","Dez",
               "Onze","Doze","Treze","Catorze","Quinze","Dezasseis","Dezassete","Dezoito","Dezanove"];
const TENS  = ["","","Vinte","Trinta","Quarenta","Cinquenta","Sessenta","Setenta","Oitenta","Noventa"];
const numPT = (n) => {
  if (n === undefined || n === null || n === "") return "";
  const v = Math.ceil(Number(n));
  if (isNaN(v) || v < 0 || v > 100) return String(n);
  if (v <= 19) return UNITS[v];
  const t = Math.floor(v / 10), u = v % 10;
  return u === 0 ? TENS[t] : `${TENS[t]} e ${UNITS[u]}`;
};
const gradeWord = (n) => { const w = numPT(n); return w ? `${w} valores` : ""; };

const calcMedia = (grades, cols) => {
  const vals = cols.map(c => grades?.[c.id])
    .filter(v => v !== "" && v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "-")
    .map(Number).filter(n => !isNaN(n));
  if (vals.length === 0) return null;
  return vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length;
};

const gcColor = (v, pv = 10, colorGrades = true) => {
  if (!colorGrades) return "#000";
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? "#000" : n >= pv ? BLUE_POS_CSS : RED_CSS;
};
const gcRGB = (v, pv = 10, colorGrades = true) => {
  if (!colorGrades) return [0,0,0];
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? [0,0,0] : n >= pv ? BLUE_POS_RGB : RED_RGB;
};

const isNoGradeSubject = (name) =>
  NO_GRADE_SUBJECTS.some(s => (name || "").toLowerCase().includes(s));

const getMissingFields = (s) => {
  const m = [];
  if (!s?.student_name?.trim())         m.push("Nome");
  if (!s?.student_birthday)             m.push("Data de nascimento");
  if (!s?.student_identityCard?.trim()) m.push("B.I.");
  if (!s?.student_naturalness?.trim())  m.push("Naturalidade");
  return m;
};

const detectPassValue = (label) => {
  if (!label) return 10;
  const n = label.toLowerCase().normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,"");
  const low6 = ["1","1a","primeira","2","2a","segunda","3","3a","terceira",
                 "4","4a","quarta","5","5a","quinta","6","6a","sexta"];
  for (const w of low6) { if (n.includes(w)) return 10; }
  return 20;
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   AVATAR MUI
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function StudentAvatar({ src, name, size = 30, className = "" }) {
  const initials = (name || "?").split(" ").slice(0,2).map(w => w[0]).join("").toUpperCase();
  if (src) return (
    <Avatar src={src} alt={name} className={className}
      sx={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size * 0.4, border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", flexShrink: 0 }}
      onError={e => { e.target.src = ""; }}>
      {initials}
    </Avatar>
  );
  return (
    <Avatar className={className}
      sx={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size * 0.38, flexShrink: 0,
            background: "rgba(108,63,197,.13)", color: PURPLE_RAW,
            border: "1.5px solid rgba(108,63,197,.2)", fontWeight: 700, fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      {initials}
    </Avatar>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EDITABLE TEXT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function EditableText({ value, onChange, placeholder = "—", style = {}, multiline = false, tag = "span", isNd = false }) {
  const Tag = tag;
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [local, setLocal]     = useState(value || "");
  useEffect(() => { setLocal(value || ""); }, [value]);
  const commit = () => { setEditing(false); if (local !== value) onChange(local); };
  const cancel = () => { setEditing(false); setLocal(value || ""); };
  const isEmpty = !local || local === "N/D";
  const ds = {
    cursor: "text",
    borderBottom: isEmpty || isNd ? "1.5px dashed #ef4444" : "1px dashed #aaa",
    minWidth: 40, display: "inline",
    background: isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.06)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.04)",
    borderRadius: 2, padding: "0 2px", transition: "background .15s",
    color: isEmpty || isNd ? "#ef4444" : "inherit", ...style,
  };
  if (!editing) return (
    <Tag onClick={() => setEditing(true)} title="Clique para editar" style={ds}
      onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.14)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.12)"; }}
      onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.06)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.04)"; }}>
      {local || <span style={{ fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: "0.92em" }}>{placeholder}</span>}
    </Tag>
  );
  return multiline
    ? <textarea autoFocus value={local} rows={3} onChange={e => setLocal(e.target.value)} onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === "Escape") cancel(); }}
        style={{ fontFamily: "inherit", fontSize: "inherit", color: "inherit", width: "100%",
                 border: `1px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}`, borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 4px",
                 outline: "none", background: "#fffff0", resize: "vertical", ...style }}/>
    : <input autoFocus value={local} onChange={e => setLocal(e.target.value)} onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === "Enter") commit(); if (e.key === "Escape") cancel(); }}
        style={{ fontFamily: "inherit", fontSize: "inherit", color: "inherit", minWidth: 60,
                 border: `1px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}`, borderRadius: 4, padding: "0 4px",
                 outline: "none", background: "#fffff0", display: "inline", ...style }}/>;
}

/* ── Bloco removível ──────────────────────────────────────── */
function RemovableBlock({ children, onRemove, fullEditMode, label = "" }) {
  if (!fullEditMode) return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <div style={{ position: "relative", marginBottom: 2 }}>
      <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: -10, right: -8, zIndex: 10, display: "flex", gap: 3, alignItems: "center" }}>
        {label && <span style={{ fontSize: 8, background: "#f1f5f9", color: "#64748b", borderRadius: 4, padding: "1px 5px", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600, border: "1px solid #e2e8f0" }}>{label}</span>}
        <button onClick={onRemove} title="Remover bloco"
          style={{ width: 18, height: 18, borderRadius: "50%", border: "none", background: "#ef4444", color: "#fff", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 0 }}>×</button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ border: "1px dashed rgba(239,68,68,.35)", borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 4px", background: "rgba(239,68,68,.02)" }}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SELECT STYLES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const SEL = {
  control: (b, s) => ({ ...b, borderRadius: 10, borderColor: s.isFocused ? PURPLE_HEX : "#e2e8f0",
    boxShadow: s.isFocused ? "0 0 0 3px rgba(108,63,197,.13)" : "none",
    fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 13, minHeight: 40 }),
  option:  (b, s) => ({ ...b, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 13,
    background: s.isSelected ? PURPLE_HEX : s.isFocused ? "rgba(108,63,197,.07)" : "#fff",
    color: s.isSelected ? "#fff" : "#0f172a", cursor: "pointer" }),
  menuPortal: b => ({ ...b, zIndex: 99999 }),
  menu:       b => ({ ...b, borderRadius: 10, boxShadow: "0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.12)" }),
  placeholder:b => ({ ...b, fontFamily: F_SANS, color: "#94a3b8" }),
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STUDENT MULTI-SELECT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function StudentMultiSelect({ options, value, onChange }) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const filtered   = options.filter(o => o.label.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
  const selectedSet = useMemo(() => new Set(value.map(v => v.value)), [value]);
  const toggle = useCallback((opt) => {
    if (selectedSet.has(opt.value)) onChange(value.filter(v => v.value !== opt.value));
    else onChange([...value, opt]);
  }, [selectedSet, value, onChange]);
  const toggleAll = useCallback(() => {
    if (filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value))) {
      const fs = new Set(filtered.map(f => f.value));
      onChange(value.filter(v => !fs.has(v.value)));
    } else {
      const m = [...value];
      filtered.forEach(o => { if (!selectedSet.has(o.value)) m.push(o); });
      onChange(m);
    }
  }, [filtered, selectedSet, value, onChange]);
  const allSel = filtered.length > 0 && filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value));
  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <div style={{ position: "relative", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <input value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Pesquisar aluno..."
          style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 32px 8px 12px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0",
                   borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: F_SANS, outline: "none",
                   color: "#0f172a", background: "#fff", boxSizing: "border-box" }}
          onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
          onBlur={e  => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}/>
        {search && <button onClick={() => setSearch("")}
          style={{ position: "absolute", right: 10, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 16, padding: 0 }}>×</button>}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "4px 8px", marginBottom: 4, background: "#f8fafc", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 11.5, color: "#64748b" }}>
        <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 500 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={allSel} onChange={toggleAll} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: PURPLE_HEX }}/>
          {allSel ? "Desseleccionar todos" : "Seleccionar todos"}
        </label>
        <span style={{ color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600 }}>{value.length}/{options.length}</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{ maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, background: "#fff" }}>
        {filtered.length === 0
          ? <div style={{ padding: "12px 14px", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 13 }}>Nenhum aluno encontrado.</div>
          : filtered.map(opt => {
              const checked = selectedSet.has(opt.value);
              const pic = opt.picture ? (opt.picture.startsWith("http") ? opt.picture : Hoot() + opt.picture) : null;
              return (
                <label key={opt.value}
                  style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: "7px 12px", cursor: "pointer",
                           borderBottom: "1px solid #f1f5f9", background: checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff",
                           fontSize: 13, color: "#0f172a" }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.07)" : "#f8fafc"}
                  onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff"}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={() => toggle(opt)}
                    style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: PURPLE_HEX, flexShrink: 0 }}/>
                  <StudentAvatar src={pic} name={opt.label} size={24}/>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: checked ? 600 : 400 }}>{opt.label}</span>
                </label>
              );
            })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   GRADE CELL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function GradeCell({ value, onChange, passValue, editMode, colorGrades = true, noGrade = false }) {
  const [local, setLocal] = useState(value ?? "");
  useEffect(() => { setLocal(value ?? ""); }, [value]);
  if (noGrade) return <div style={{ textAlign: "center", color: "#64748b" }}>—</div>;
  const n = parseFloat(local);
  const hasVal = local !== "" && local !== null && local !== undefined;
  const color  = hasVal && !isNaN(n) ? gcColor(n, passValue, colorGrades) : "#000";
  if (!editMode) return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
      <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color }}>{hasVal ? String(local) : ""}</span>
      {hasVal && !isNaN(n) && colorGrades && <div style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: "50%", background: color, margin: "2px auto 0" }}/>}
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <input type="number" min={0} max={passValue} step={0.5} value={local}
      onChange={e => { setLocal(e.target.value); onChange(e.target.value); }}
      style={{ width: 52, border: `1.5px solid ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 6, padding: "2px 4px",
               textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, color, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: "8pt", outline: "none" }}/>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DOC HEADER (partilhado entre modelos)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function DocHeader({ headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload, fontSize }) {
  const sc = fontSize / 12;
  return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center", fontFamily: F_SERIF, lineHeight: 1.8, marginBottom: 8 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <label title="Clique para alterar logótipo" style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>
          <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: "none" }}
            onChange={e => {
              const f = e.target.files?.[0];
              if (f) { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = ev => onLogoUpload?.(ev.target.result); r.readAsDataURL(f); }
            }}/>
          <img src={logoUrl || ANGOLA_COAT} alt="Logótipo" crossOrigin="anonymous"
            style={{ height: 54 * sc, objectFit: "contain", border: "2px dashed transparent", borderRadius: 4, transition: "border-color .15s" }}
            onMouseEnter={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_RAW}
            onMouseLeave={e => e.target.style.borderColor = "transparent"}
            onError={e => e.target.src = ANGOLA_COAT}/>
        </label>
      </div>
      {(headerLines || []).map((hl, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{ fontSize: `${(hl.bold ? 13 : 11) * sc}pt`, fontWeight: hl.bold ? 700 : 400, lineHeight: 1.65 }}>
          {hl.text}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   HEADER EDITOR
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function HeaderEditor({ headerLines, onChange }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        <strong>N</strong> = negrito · <strong>×</strong> = remover
      </div>
      {(headerLines || []).map((hl, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, marginBottom: 5 }}>
          <input defaultValue={hl.text}
            onBlur={e => onChange((headerLines||[]).map((x,xi) => xi===i ? {...x,text:e.target.value} : x))}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "5px 8px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 7, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12, outline: "none", fontWeight: hl.bold ? 700 : 400 }}
            onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}/>
          <button onClick={() => onChange((headerLines||[]).map((x,xi) => xi===i ? {...x,bold:!x.bold} : x))}
            style={{ width:28,height:28,border:`1.5px solid ${hl.bold?PURPLE_HEX:"#e2e8f0"}`,borderRadius:6,background:hl.bold?PURPLE_HEX:"#fff",color:hl.bold?"#fff":"#64748b",fontWeight:700,fontSize:12,cursor:"pointer" }}>N</button>
          <button onClick={() => onChange((headerLines||[]).filter((_,xi)=>xi!==i))}
            style={{ width:28,height:28,border:"1.5px solid #fee2e2",borderRadius:6,background:"#fff",color:"#ef4444",fontWeight:700,fontSize:14,cursor:"pointer",lineHeight:1 }}>×</button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <button onClick={() => onChange([...(headerLines||[]),{text:"Nova linha",bold:false}])}
        style={{ marginTop:4,padding:"5px 12px",border:`1.5px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:7,background:`rgba(108,63,197,.05)`,color:PURPLE_HEX,fontWeight:600,fontSize:12,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:F_SANS }}>
        + Adicionar linha
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SUBJECTS EDITOR PANEL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function GradesColumnsPanel({
  subjects, onSubjectsChange,
  columns, onColumnsChange,
  tableHeaders, onTableHeadersChange,
  allSubjects, allAcLevels,
}) {
  const subjOpts = allSubjects.map(s => ({ value: s.id, label: s.title }));
  const lvlOpts  = allAcLevels.map(l => ({ value: l.id, label: l.title }));

  const addSubj = (opt) => {
    if (!opt || subjects.find(s => s.id === opt.value)) return;
    onSubjectsChange([...subjects, { id: opt.value, name: opt.label }]);
  };
  const remSubj = (id) => onSubjectsChange(subjects.filter(s => s.id !== id));
  const addCol  = () => {
    if (columns.length >= 4) return toast.error("Máximo de 4 colunas.");
    onColumnsChange([...columns, { id: `c${Date.now()}`, label: "", sublabel: "" }]);
  };
  const remCol  = (i) => onColumnsChange(columns.filter((_, xi) => xi !== i));
  const updCol  = (i, patch) => onColumnsChange(columns.map((c, xi) => xi === i ? { ...c, ...patch } : c));

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>

      {/* Colunas */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: .5, marginBottom: 8 }}>
          Colunas / Classes (máx. 4)
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
          {columns.map((col, i) => (
            <div key={col.id} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "6px 10px", background: "#f8fafc", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, minWidth: 230 }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                <Select
                  options={lvlOpts}
                  value={col.label ? (lvlOpts.find(o => o.label === col.label) || { value: col.id, label: col.label }) : null}
                  onChange={opt => updCol(i, { id: opt?.value || col.id, label: opt?.label || "" })}
                  placeholder="Classe / Ciclo..." isClearable styles={SEL}
                  menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed"/>
                <input value={col.sublabel || ""} onChange={e => updCol(i, { sublabel: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Sub-rótulo (ex: 2ª Classe)..."
                  style={{ padding: "4px 8px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 7, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 11, outline: "none", color: "#64748b" }}
                  onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
                  onBlur={e  => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}/>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => remCol(i)}
                style={{ width: 24, height: 24, border: "1.5px solid #fee2e2", borderRadius: 6, background: "#fff", color: "#ef4444", fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>×</button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {columns.length < 4 && (
            <button onClick={addCol}
              style={{ padding: "7px 14px", border: `1.5px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 10, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
              <MdOutlineAdd size={14}/> Coluna
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <button onClick={() => onColumnsChange(DEFAULT_COLS_PRIMARIO)}
            style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.3)`, borderRadius: 7, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.06)`, color: PURPLE_RAW, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            Pré-definir: Primário (I/II/III Ciclo)
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onColumnsChange(DEFAULT_COLS_SECUNDARIO)}
            style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.3)`, borderRadius: 7, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.06)`, color: PURPLE_RAW, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            Pré-definir: Secundário (7ª/8ª/9ª)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Disciplinas */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: .5, marginBottom: 8 }}>Disciplinas</div>
        <Select options={subjOpts.filter(o => !subjects.find(s => s.id === o.value))}
          onChange={opt => addSubj(opt)} value={null}
          placeholder="Adicionar disciplina da lista..." isClearable styles={SEL}
          menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed"/>
        {subjects.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 8 }}>
            {subjects.map(s => (
              <div key={s.id} style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "4px 10px", background: `rgba(108,63,197,.07)`, border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.2)`, borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 12, color: PURPLE_RAW }}>
                {s.name}
                <button onClick={() => remSubj(s.id)}
                  style={{ width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: "50%", border: "none", background: "rgba(108,63,197,.2)", color: PURPLE_RAW, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 0 }}>×</button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <button onClick={() => onSubjectsChange(PRESET_SUBJ_PRIMARIO.map((n, i) => ({ id: `dp${i}`, name: n })))}
            style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.3)`, borderRadius: 7, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.06)`, color: PURPLE_RAW, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            Pré-definir: Ensino Primário
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onSubjectsChange(PRESET_SUBJ_SECUNDARIO.map((n, i) => ({ id: `ds${i}`, name: n })))}
            style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.3)`, borderRadius: 7, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.06)`, color: PURPLE_RAW, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            Pré-definir: Ensino Secundário
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onSubjectsChange([])}
            style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: "1px solid #fee2e2", borderRadius: 7, background: "#fff5f5", color: "#ef4444", fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            Limpar todas
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Cabeçalhos da tabela */}
      <div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: .5, marginBottom: 8 }}>
          Cabeçalhos da tabela (editáveis)
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: 8 }}>
          {[
            ["th_disc",    tableHeaders.th_disc    || "Disciplina",       "Coluna: Disciplina"],
            ["th_media_f", tableHeaders.th_media_f || "Média final",      "Coluna: Média final"],
            ["th_extenso", tableHeaders.th_extenso || "Média por extenso","Coluna: Média por extenso"],
          ].map(([key, val, lbl]) => (
            <div key={key}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: "#64748b", fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 4 }}>{lbl}</div>
              <input value={val} onChange={e => onTableHeadersChange({ ...tableHeaders, [key]: e.target.value })}
                style={{ width: "100%", padding: "6px 8px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 7, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12, outline: "none", boxSizing: "border-box" }}
                onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
                onBlur={e  => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}/>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BORDER UTILS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const getBorderStyle = (m) => {
  if (!m || m === "none")  return {};
  // FIX: increased lateral padding (left/right) from 38px to 52px
  if (m === "single")      return { border: "1.5px solid #000", padding: "32px 52px" };
  if (m === "double")      return { border: "3px double #000",  padding: "34px 54px" };
  if (m === "certificate") return { border: "6px solid #000", outline: "2px solid #000", outlineOffset: "-10px", padding: "42px 60px" };
  return {};
};
const drawPdfBorder = (pdf, m, PW, PH, M) => {
  if (!m || m === "none") return;
  pdf.setDrawColor(0,0,0);
  if (m === "single") { pdf.setLineWidth(0.5); pdf.rect(M-2,M-2,PW-(M-2)*2,PH-(M-2)*2); }
  else if (m === "double") {
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.8); pdf.rect(M-4,M-4,PW-(M-4)*2,PH-(M-4)*2);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.3); pdf.rect(M-2,M-2,PW-(M-2)*2,PH-(M-2)*2);
  } else if (m === "certificate") {
    pdf.setLineWidth(2); pdf.rect(M-6,M-6,PW-(M-6)*2,PH-(M-6)*2);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.5); pdf.rect(M-3,M-3,PW-(M-3)*2,PH-(M-3)*2);
  }
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   IMAGE CACHE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const _imgCache = {};
async function loadImgB64(src) {
  if (!src) return null;
  if (_imgCache[src] !== undefined) return _imgCache[src];
  try {
    const b64 = await new Promise((res,rej) => {
      const img = new Image(); img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
      img.onload  = () => { try { const c=document.createElement("canvas"); c.width=img.naturalWidth||img.width; c.height=img.naturalHeight||img.height; c.getContext("2d").drawImage(img,0,0); res(c.toDataURL("image/png")); } catch(e){rej(e);} };
      img.onerror = rej; img.src = src;
    });
    _imgCache[src] = b64; return b64;
  } catch { _imgCache[src] = null; return null; }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CERTIFICADO — ENSINO PRIMÁRIO
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function CertPrimario({
  data, overrides, onOverrideChange, headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload,
  editMode, fullEditMode, fontSize, passValue, borderMode, colorGrades,
  subjects, columns, tableHeaders, classInfo, institute, studentGrades,
  onStudentGradeChange, removedBlocks, onToggleBlock,
}) {
  const d  = { ...(data||{}), ...(overrides||{}) };
  const ch = (f, v) => onOverrideChange?.(f, v);
  const fs = fontSize || 11;
  const pv = passValue || 10;
  // FIX: increased lateral padding
  const bs = borderMode && borderMode !== "none"
    ? getBorderStyle(borderMode)
    : { padding: "28px 52px 32px" };
  const city = institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda";

  const E = (f, val, ph="—", bold=false) => {
    const v = val || ""; const nd = !v || v === "N/D";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={v} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder={ph} isNd={nd}/>;
    if (nd) return <span style={{color:"#ef4444",fontStyle:"italic"}}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong>{v}</strong> : <span>{v}</span>;
  };
  const D = (f, sv, ph="—", bold=true) => {
    const val = d[f]!==undefined ? d[f] : sv;
    const nd = !val||val==="N/D"||val==="";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val||""} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder={ph} isNd={nd}/>;
    if (nd) return <span style={{color:"#ef4444",fontStyle:"italic"}}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong>{val}</strong> : <span>{val}</span>;
  };
  const T = (f, def) => {
    const val = d[f]!==undefined ? d[f] : def;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder="Editar..."/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };
  const RB = (id, label, content) => {
    if (removedBlocks?.includes(id)) return null;
    if (!fullEditMode) return content;
    return <RemovableBlock key={id} onRemove={()=>onToggleBlock?.(id)} fullEditMode={fullEditMode} label={label}>{content}</RemovableBlock>;
  };

  const cols = columns || DEFAULT_COLS_PRIMARIO;
  const rows = studentGrades || [];

  /* média global */
  const allMedias = rows
    .filter(g => !isNoGradeSubject(g.name))
    .map(g => calcMedia(g.grades, cols))
    .filter(v => v !== null);
  const mediaFinalCalc = allMedias.length > 0
    ? Math.ceil(allMedias.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / allMedias.length)
    : null;

  // FIX: media final manual override support
  const mediaFinalDisplay = d.media_override !== undefined && d.media_override !== ""
    ? d.media_override
    : (mediaFinalCalc !== null ? mediaFinalCalc : null);
  const mediaFinalColor = mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? gcColor(mediaFinalDisplay, pv, colorGrades) : "#000";

  return (
    <div style={{ width:"100%", background:"#fff", fontFamily:F_SERIF, color:"#000", fontSize:`${fs}pt`, lineHeight:1.85, ...bs }}>
      <DocHeader headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl} onLogoUpload={onLogoUpload} fontSize={fs}/>

      {/* Sub-título — FIX: more margin below */}
      {RB("sb_title","§ Sub-título",
        <div style={{ textAlign:"center", margin:"10px 0 6px" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize:`${fs+1}pt`, fontWeight:400, letterSpacing:".06em" }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.cert_subtitle||"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO"} onChange={v=>ch("cert_subtitle",v)} style={{ fontWeight:400 }}/>
              : (d.cert_subtitle||"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO")}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Título CERTIFICADO — FIX: more margin top */}
      <div style={{ textAlign:"center", margin:"10px 0 18px" }}>
        {fullEditMode
          ? <EditableText value={d.cert_title||"CERTIFICADO"} onChange={v=>ch("cert_title",v)}
              style={{ fontSize:`${fs+6}pt`, fontWeight:700 }}/>
          : <span style={{ fontSize:`${fs+6}pt`, fontWeight:700 }}>{d.cert_title||"CERTIFICADO"}</span>
        }
      </div>

      {/* § Diretor */}
      {RB("p_director","§ Director",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("dir_pre","a)")} {E("director_name", d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal, "Nome do Director", true)},{" "}
          {T("dir_role","Director d")}{T("school_article","a")}{" "}
          {E("school_full_name", d.school_full_name||institute?.ed_institute_name, "Nome da Escola", true)},{" "}
          {T("decree_pre","criada sob Decreto Executivo nº")}{" "}
          {E("decree_number", d.decree_number||"", "nº Decreto")}{" "}
          {T("decree_suf","de")}{" "}
          {E("decree_date", d.decree_date||"", "Data do Decreto")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Certifica */}
      {RB("p_cert","§ Certifica",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("cert_verb","  Certifica que")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.student_name||data?.student_name||""} onChange={v=>ch("student_name",v)} placeholder="Nome do aluno" style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}/>
              : (d.student_name||data?.student_name||<span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>)
            }
          </strong>,{" "}
          {T("filiation_label","filho(a) de")}{" "}{D("father_name",data?.father_name,"Nome do pai")}{" "}
          {T("and_label","e de")}{" "}{D("mother_name",data?.mother_name,"Nome da mãe")},{" "}
          {T("born_pre","nascido(a) a")}{" "}
          {(() => {
            const bday = d.student_birthday||data?.student_birthday;
            const txt  = d.student_birthday_text||(bday?formatDatePT(bday):"");
            if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={txt} onChange={v=>ch("student_birthday_text",v)} placeholder="Data de nascimento" isNd={!txt}/>;
            return txt ? <strong>{txt}</strong> : <span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>;
          })()},{" "}
          {T("nat_label","natural de")}{" "}{D("student_naturalness",data?.student_naturalness,"Naturalidade")},{" "}
          {T("muni_label","Município de")}{" "}{E("municipality",d.municipality,"Município")},{" "}
          {T("prov_label","Província de")}{" "}{E("province",d.province||institute?.ed_institute_state,"Província")},{" "}
          {T("nat2_label","nacionalidade")}{" "}{D("student_nationality",data?.student_nationality||"Angolana","Nacionalidade")},{" "}
          {T("bi_label","portador(a) do B.I ou Cédula nº")}{" "}
          {D("student_identityCard",data?.student_identityCard,"Nº B.I.")},{" "}
          {T("bi_issued","emitido pela Direcção Nacional de Identificação aos")}{" "}
          {E("bi_issued_date",d.bi_issued_date,"Data emissão BI")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Concluiu — FIX: média final editável manualmente */}
      {RB("p_concluded","§ Concluiu",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("concluded_pre","  Concluiu no Ano Lectivo")}{" "}
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year||""} onChange={v=>ch("academic_year_override",v)} placeholder="Ano lectivo" isNd={!(d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year)}/>
            : <strong>{d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year||<span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          },{" "}
          {T("cycle_label","o")}{" "}
          <strong>{fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.cycle_name||"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO"} onChange={v=>ch("cycle_name",v)}/>
            : (d.cycle_name||"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO")
          }</strong>,{" "}
          {T("law_ref","conforme o disposto na alínea b) do artigo 109º da LBSEE 17/16 de 7 de Outubro, com a")}{" "}
          {T("media_label","Média Final de")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color: mediaFinalColor }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText
                  value={String(mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? mediaFinalDisplay : (d.media_override || ""))}
                  onChange={v => ch("media_override", v)}
                  placeholder="Média"
                  style={{ color: mediaFinalColor }}
                />
              : (mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? mediaFinalDisplay : <span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>)
            }
          </strong>{" "}
          {T("media_suffix","Valores, obtida nas seguintes classificações por ciclos de aprendizagem:")}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* Tabela de notas */}
      {!removedBlocks?.includes("t_grades") ? (
        <div style={{ position:"relative" }}>
          {fullEditMode && (
            <button onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")} title="Remover tabela"
              style={{ position:"absolute",top:-10,right:-8,zIndex:10,width:18,height:18,borderRadius:"50%",border:"none",background:"#ef4444",color:"#fff",cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",padding:0 }}>×</button>
          )}
          <table style={{ width:"100%", borderCollapse:"collapse", margin:"8px 0 12px", fontSize:`${fs-1}pt` }}>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th rowSpan={2} style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 8px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",verticalAlign:"middle" }}>
                  {fullEditMode ? <EditableText value={d.th_disc||"Disciplina"} onChange={v=>ch("th_disc",v)}/> : (d.th_disc||"Disciplina")}
                </th>
                {cols.map((col, ci) => (
                  <th key={ci} colSpan={1} style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"3px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt` }}>
                    <div>{col.label}</div>
                    {col.sublabel && <div style={{ fontWeight:400, fontSize:`${fs-3}pt` }}>{col.sublabel}</div>}
                  </th>
                ))}
                <th rowSpan={2} style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",verticalAlign:"middle",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt` }}>
                  {fullEditMode ? <EditableText value={d.th_media_f||"Média final"} onChange={v=>ch("th_media_f",v)}/> : (d.th_media_f||"Média final")}
                </th>
                <th rowSpan={2} style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",verticalAlign:"middle",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt` }}>
                  {fullEditMode ? <EditableText value={d.th_extenso||"Média por extenso"} onChange={v=>ch("th_extenso",v)}/> : (d.th_extenso||"Média por extenso")}
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.length > 0
                ? rows.map((g, i) => {
                    const noGrade = isNoGradeSubject(g.name);
                    const media   = noGrade ? null : calcMedia(g.grades, cols);
                    const mediaRnd = media !== null ? Math.ceil(media) : null;
                    const clr     = mediaRnd !== null ? gcColor(mediaRnd, pv, colorGrades) : "#000";
                    return (
                      <tr key={i} style={{ background: noGrade ? "#f5f5f5" : (i%2===0?"#fff":"#fafafa") }}>
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 8px",fontWeight:noGrade?400:400 }}>{g.name}</td>
                        {noGrade
                          ? cols.map((_,ci) => <td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 4px",textAlign:"center",color:"#64748b" }}>—</td>)
                          : cols.map((col,ci) => (
                              <td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 4px",minWidth:55 }}>
                                <GradeCell value={g.grades?.[col.id]??""} onChange={v=>onStudentGradeChange?.(g.id,col.id,v)}
                                  passValue={pv} editMode={editMode||fullEditMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                              </td>
                            ))
                        }
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,color:noGrade?"#64748b":clr }}>
                          {noGrade ? "—" : (mediaRnd !== null ? mediaRnd : "")}
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 8px",textAlign:"center",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt`,color:noGrade?"#64748b":clr }}>
                          {noGrade ? "—" : (mediaRnd !== null ? gradeWord(mediaRnd) : "")}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                : [0,1,2,3,4].map(i => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 8px",color:"#ccc" }}>—</td>
                      {cols.map((_,ci) => <td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 6px" }}></td>)}
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 6px" }}></td>
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 8px" }}></td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
              }
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      ) : fullEditMode ? (
        <button onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")} style={{ padding:"4px 12px",fontSize:11,border:`1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:6,background:`rgba(108,63,197,.05)`,color:PURPLE_HEX,cursor:"pointer",margin:"8px 0",fontFamily:F_SANS }}>+ Restaurar tabela</button>
      ) : null}

      {/* § Para efeitos legais */}
      {RB("p_legal","§ Efeitos legais",
        <p style={{ margin:"10px 0", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("legal_pre","Para efeitos legais lhe é passado o presente")}{" "}
          <strong>{T("legal_cert","CERTIFICADO")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_mid","que consta no livro de registo nº")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>{E("reg_number",d.reg_number,"nº registo")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_folha","folha")}{" "}<strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>{E("reg_folha",d.reg_folha,"folha")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_suf","assinado por mim e autenticado com o carimbo a óleo/selo branco em uso neste estabelecimento de ensino.")}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Data */}
      {RB("p_date","§ Data",
        <p style={{ textAlign:"center", fontWeight:400, margin:"14px 0 20px" }}>
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.school_location_full||city} onChange={v=>ch("school_location_full",v)} placeholder="Cidade"/>
            : <span>{d.school_location_full||city}</span>
          }{T("date_connector",", aos ")}{todayPT()}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Assinaturas — FIX: centradas */}
      {RB("p_sigs","§ Assinaturas",
        <div style={{ display:"flex", justifyContent:"center", gap:"80px", marginTop:4, fontSize:`${fs-1}pt` }}>
          {/* Conferido por */}
          <div style={{ display:"flex", flexDirection:"column", alignItems:"center", minWidth:180 }}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom:28, textAlign:"center" }}>{T("sig_left_label","Conferido por")}</div>
            <div style={{ borderTop:"1px solid #000", width:180, paddingTop:4, textAlign:"center" }}>
              {fullEditMode
                ? <EditableText value={d.sig_left_name||""} onChange={v=>ch("sig_left_name",v)} placeholder="Nome conferente"/>
                : <span>{d.sig_left_name||""}</span>
              }
            </div>
          </div>
          {/* O Director */}
          <div style={{ display:"flex", flexDirection:"column", alignItems:"center", minWidth:200 }}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom:28, textAlign:"center" }}>{T("sig_right_label","O Director")}</div>
            <div style={{ borderTop:"1px solid #000", width:200, paddingTop:4, textAlign:"center" }}>
              {fullEditMode
                ? <EditableText value={d.sig_right_name||d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal||""} onChange={v=>ch("sig_right_name",v)} placeholder="Nome do Director"/>
                : <span>{d.sig_right_name||d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal||""}</span>
              }
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Restaurar blocos removidos */}
      {fullEditMode && (removedBlocks||[]).length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop:12,padding:"8px 12px",background:"#f8fafc",border:"1px dashed #cbd5e1",borderRadius:8,fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:11 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b",marginBottom:6 }}>Blocos removidos:</div>
          <div style={{ display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap" }}>
            {removedBlocks.map(id => (
              <button key={id} onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.(id)}
                style={{ padding:"3px 10px",border:`1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:6,background:`rgba(108,63,197,.05)`,color:PURPLE_HEX,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,fontFamily:F_SANS }}>
                + {id.replace("p_","§ ").replace("t_","Tabela ")}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CERTIFICADO — ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO (I Ciclo 7ª/8ª/9ª)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function CertSecundario({
  data, overrides, onOverrideChange, headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload,
  editMode, fullEditMode, fontSize, passValue, borderMode, colorGrades,
  subjects, columns, tableHeaders, classInfo, institute, studentGrades,
  onStudentGradeChange, removedBlocks, onToggleBlock,
}) {
  const d   = { ...(data||{}), ...(overrides||{}) };
  const ch  = (f,v) => onOverrideChange?.(f,v);
  const fs  = fontSize || 11;
  const pv  = passValue || 20;
  // FIX: increased lateral padding
  const bs  = borderMode && borderMode !== "none"
    ? getBorderStyle(borderMode)
    : { padding: "28px 52px 32px" };
  const city = institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda";

  const E = (f,val,ph="—",bold=false) => {
    const v=val||""; const nd=!v||v==="N/D";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={v} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder={ph} isNd={nd}/>;
    if (nd) return <span style={{color:"#ef4444",fontStyle:"italic"}}>N/D</span>;
    return bold?<strong>{v}</strong>:<span>{v}</span>;
  };
  const D = (f,sv,ph="—",bold=true) => {
    const val=d[f]!==undefined?d[f]:sv; const nd=!val||val==="N/D"||val==="";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val||""} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder={ph} isNd={nd}/>;
    if (nd) return <span style={{color:"#ef4444",fontStyle:"italic"}}>N/D</span>;
    return bold?<strong>{val}</strong>:<span>{val}</span>;
  };
  const T = (f,def) => {
    const val=d[f]!==undefined?d[f]:def;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={x=>ch(f,x)} placeholder="Editar..."/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };
  const RB = (id,label,content) => {
    if (removedBlocks?.includes(id)) return null;
    if (!fullEditMode) return content;
    return <RemovableBlock key={id} onRemove={()=>onToggleBlock?.(id)} fullEditMode={fullEditMode} label={label}>{content}</RemovableBlock>;
  };

  const cols = columns || DEFAULT_COLS_SECUNDARIO;
  const rows = studentGrades || [];

  const allMedias = rows
    .filter(g => !isNoGradeSubject(g.name))
    .map(g => calcMedia(g.grades, cols))
    .filter(v => v !== null);
  const mediaFinalCalc = allMedias.length > 0
    ? Math.ceil(allMedias.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/allMedias.length)
    : null;

  // FIX: media final manual override support
  const mediaFinalDisplay = d.media_override !== undefined && d.media_override !== ""
    ? d.media_override
    : (mediaFinalCalc !== null ? mediaFinalCalc : null);
  const mediaFinalColor = mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? gcColor(mediaFinalDisplay, pv, colorGrades) : "#000";

  return (
    <div style={{ width:"100%",background:"#fff",fontFamily:F_SERIF,color:"#000",fontSize:`${fs}pt`,lineHeight:1.85,...bs }}>
      <DocHeader headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl} onLogoUpload={onLogoUpload} fontSize={fs}/>

      {/* Sub-título — FIX: more margin below */}
      {RB("sb_title","§ Sub-título",
        <div style={{ textAlign:"center", margin:"10px 0 6px" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize:`${fs+1}pt`, fontWeight:400, letterSpacing:".05em" }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.cert_subtitle||"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO"} onChange={v=>ch("cert_subtitle",v)} style={{ fontWeight:400 }}/>
              : (d.cert_subtitle||"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO")}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Título — FIX: more margin top */}
      <div style={{ textAlign:"center", margin:"10px 0 18px" }}>
        {fullEditMode
          ? <EditableText value={d.cert_title||"CERTIFICADO"} onChange={v=>ch("cert_title",v)} style={{ fontSize:`${fs+6}pt`,fontWeight:700 }}/>
          : <span style={{ fontSize:`${fs+6}pt`,fontWeight:700 }}>{d.cert_title||"CERTIFICADO"}</span>
        }
      </div>

      {/* § Director */}
      {RB("p_director","§ Director",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("dir_pre","  a)")} {E("director_name",d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal,"Nome do Director",true)},{" "}
          {T("dir_role","Director d")}{T("school_article","o")}{" "}
          {E("school_full_name",d.school_full_name||institute?.ed_institute_name,"Nome da Escola",true)},{" "}
          {T("decree_pre","criada sob Decreto Executivo nº")}{" "}
          {E("decree_number",d.decree_number||"","nº Decreto")}{" "}
          {T("decree_suf","de")}{" "}
          {E("decree_date",d.decree_date||"","Data do Decreto")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Certifica */}
      {RB("p_cert","§ Certifica",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("cert_verb","  Certifica que")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.student_name||data?.student_name||""} onChange={v=>ch("student_name",v)} placeholder="Nome do aluno" style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}/>
              : (d.student_name||data?.student_name||<span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>)
            }
          </strong>,{" "}
          {T("filiation_label","filho(a) de")}{" "}{D("father_name",data?.father_name,"Nome do pai")}{" "}
          {T("and_label","e de")}{" "}{D("mother_name",data?.mother_name,"Nome da mãe")},{" "}
          {T("born_pre","nascido (a) aos")}{" "}
          {(()=>{ const bday=d.student_birthday||data?.student_birthday; const txt=d.student_birthday_text||(bday?formatDatePT(bday):"");
            if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={txt} onChange={v=>ch("student_birthday_text",v)} placeholder="Data" isNd={!txt}/>;
            return txt?<strong>{txt}</strong>:<span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>; })()}{" de "}{" "}
          {T("nat_label","natural de")}{" "}{E("municipality",d.municipality,"Município")},{" "}
          {T("muni_label","Município de")}{" "}{E("municipality2",d.municipality2||d.municipality,"Município")},{" "}
          {T("prov_label","Província de")}{" "}{E("province",d.province||institute?.ed_institute_state,"Província")},{" "}
          {T("nat2_label","nacionalidade")}{" "}{D("student_nationality",data?.student_nationality||"","Nacionalidade")},{" "}
          {T("bi_label","portador(a) do B.I nº")}{" "}{D("student_identityCard",data?.student_identityCard,"Nº B.I.")},{" "}
          {T("bi_passado","passado")}{" "}{E("bi_issued_date",d.bi_issued_date,"Data emissão")},{" "}
