          {T("bi_suf","aos")}{" "}{E("bi_issued_date2",d.bi_issued_date2,"Data")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Concluiu — FIX: média final editável manualmente */}
      {RB("p_concluded","§ Concluiu",
        <p style={{ margin:"0 0 10px", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("concluded_pre","  Concluiu no ano lectivo de")}{" "}
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year||""} onChange={v=>ch("academic_year_override",v)} placeholder="Ano lectivo" isNd={!(d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year)}/>
            : <strong>{d.academic_year_override||classInfo?.academic_year||data?.academic_year||<span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          },{" "}
          {T("cycle_label","o")}{" "}
          <strong>{fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.cycle_name||"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO GERAL"} onChange={v=>ch("cycle_name",v)}/>
            : (d.cycle_name||"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO GERAL")
          }</strong>,{" "}
          {T("law_ref","conforme o disposto na alínea c) do artigo 109º da LBSEE nº 17/16, de 07 de Outubro, com a")}{" "}
          {T("media_label","Média Final de")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color: mediaFinalColor }}>
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText
                  value={String(mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? mediaFinalDisplay : (d.media_override || ""))}
                  onChange={v => ch("media_override", v)}
                  placeholder="Média"
                  style={{ color: mediaFinalColor }}
                />
              : (mediaFinalDisplay !== null ? mediaFinalDisplay : <span style={{color:"#ef4444"}}>N/D</span>)
            }
          </strong>{" "}
          {T("media_suffix","Valores, obtida nas seguintes classificações por ciclos de aprendizagem:")}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* Tabela */}
      {!removedBlocks?.includes("t_grades") ? (
        <div style={{ position:"relative" }}>
          {fullEditMode && (
            <button onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")} title="Remover tabela"
              style={{ position:"absolute",top:-10,right:-8,zIndex:10,width:18,height:18,borderRadius:"50%",border:"none",background:"#ef4444",color:"#fff",cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",padding:0 }}>×</button>
          )}
          <table style={{ width:"100%",borderCollapse:"collapse",margin:"8px 0 12px",fontSize:`${fs-1}pt` }}>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"5px 8px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",verticalAlign:"middle" }}>
                  {fullEditMode?<EditableText value={d.th_disc||"Disciplina"} onChange={v=>ch("th_disc",v)}/>:(d.th_disc||"Disciplina")}
                </th>
                {cols.map((col,ci)=>(
                  <th key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8" }}>{col.label}</th>
                ))}
                <th style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt` }}>
                  {fullEditMode?<EditableText value={d.th_media_f||"Média final"} onChange={v=>ch("th_media_f",v)}/>:(d.th_media_f||"Média final")}
                </th>
                <th style={{ border:"1px solid #000",padding:"4px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,background:"#f8f8f8",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt` }}>
                  {fullEditMode?<EditableText value={d.th_extenso||"Média por extenso"} onChange={v=>ch("th_extenso",v)}/>:(d.th_extenso||"Média por extenso")}
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.length>0
                ? rows.map((g,i)=>{
                    const noGrade = isNoGradeSubject(g.name);
                    const media   = noGrade?null:calcMedia(g.grades,cols);
                    const mediaRnd = media!==null?Math.ceil(media):null;
                    const clr     = mediaRnd!==null?gcColor(mediaRnd,pv,colorGrades):"#000";
                    return (
                      <tr key={i} style={{ background:noGrade?"#f5f5f5":(i%2===0?"#fff":"#fafafa") }}>
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 8px" }}>{g.name}</td>
                        {noGrade
                          ? cols.map((_,ci)=><td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 4px",textAlign:"center",color:"#64748b" }}>—</td>)
                          : cols.map((col,ci)=>(
                              <td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 4px",minWidth:60 }}>
                                <GradeCell value={g.grades?.[col.id]??""} onChange={v=>onStudentGradeChange?.(g.id,col.id,v)}
                                  passValue={pv} editMode={editMode||fullEditMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                              </td>
                            ))
                        }
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 6px",textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700,color:noGrade?"#64748b":clr }}>
                          {noGrade?"—":(mediaRnd!==null?mediaRnd:"")}
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"3px 8px",textAlign:"center",fontSize:`${fs-2}pt`,color:noGrade?"#64748b":clr }}>
                          {noGrade?"—":(mediaRnd!==null?gradeWord(mediaRnd):"")}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                : [0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>(
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 8px",color:"#ccc" }}>—</td>
                      {cols.map((_,ci)=><td key={ci} style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 6px" }}></td>)}
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 6px" }}></td>
                      <td style={{ border:"1px solid #999",padding:"4px 8px" }}></td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
              }
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      ) : fullEditMode ? (
        <button onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")} style={{ padding:"4px 12px",fontSize:11,border:`1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:6,background:`rgba(108,63,197,.05)`,color:PURPLE_HEX,cursor:"pointer",margin:"8px 0",fontFamily:F_SANS }}>+ Restaurar tabela</button>
      ) : null}

      {/* § Para efeitos legais */}
      {RB("p_legal","§ Efeitos legais",
        <p style={{ margin:"10px 0", textAlign:"justify" }}>
          {T("legal_pre","Para efeitos legais, lhe é passado o presente")}{" "}
          <strong>{T("legal_cert","CERTIFICADO")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_mid","que consta no livro de registo nº")}{" "}
          <strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>{E("reg_number",d.reg_number,"nº/livro")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_folha","folha,")}{" "}<strong style={{ color:"#cc0000" }}>{E("reg_folha",d.reg_folha,"folha")}</strong>,{" "}
          {T("legal_suf","assinado por mim e autenticado com o carimbo a óleo em uso neste estabelecimento de ensino.")}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Data */}
      {RB("p_date","§ Data",
        <p style={{ textAlign:"center", fontWeight:400, margin:"14px 0 20px" }}>
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.school_location_full||city} onChange={v=>ch("school_location_full",v)} placeholder="Cidade"/>
            : <span>{d.school_location_full||city}</span>
          }{T("date_connector",", aos ")}{todayPT()}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* § Assinaturas — FIX: centradas */}
      {RB("p_sigs","§ Assinaturas",
        <div style={{ display:"flex",justifyContent:"center",gap:"80px",marginTop:4,fontSize:`${fs-1}pt` }}>
          {/* Conferido por */}
          <div style={{ display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",minWidth:180 }}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom:28,textAlign:"center" }}>{T("sig_left_label","Conferido por")}</div>
            <div style={{ borderTop:"1px solid #000",width:180,paddingTop:4,textAlign:"center" }}>
              {fullEditMode
                ? <EditableText value={d.sig_left_name||""} onChange={v=>ch("sig_left_name",v)} placeholder="Nome conferente"/>
                : <span>{d.sig_left_name||""}</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
          {/* O Director */}
          <div style={{ display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",minWidth:200 }}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom:28,textAlign:"center" }}>{T("sig_right_label","O Director")}</div>
            <div style={{ borderTop:"1px solid #000",width:200,paddingTop:4,textAlign:"center" }}>
              {fullEditMode
                ? <EditableText value={d.sig_right_name||d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal||""} onChange={v=>ch("sig_right_name",v)} placeholder="Nome do Director"/>
                : <span>{d.sig_right_name||d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal||""}</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {fullEditMode && (removedBlocks||[]).length>0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop:12,padding:"8px 12px",background:"#f8fafc",border:"1px dashed #cbd5e1",borderRadius:8,fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:11 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b",marginBottom:6 }}>Blocos removidos:</div>
          <div style={{ display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap" }}>
            {removedBlocks.map(id=>(
              <button key={id} onClick={()=>onToggleBlock?.(id)}
                style={{ padding:"3px 10px",border:`1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:6,background:`rgba(108,63,197,.05)`,color:PURPLE_HEX,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,fontFamily:F_SANS }}>
                + {id.replace("p_","§ ").replace("t_","Tabela ")}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STUDENT EDIT MODAL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function StudentEditModal({ show, onHide, studentId, onSaved }) {
  const [showBox,   setShowBox]   = useState(false);
  const [form,      setForm]      = useState({});
  const [errors,    setErrors]    = useState({});
  const [saving,    setSaving]    = useState(false);
  const [loading,   setLoading]   = useState(false);
  const [picture,   setPicture]   = useState(null);
  const [cover,     setCover]     = useState(null);
  const [guardion,  setGuardion]  = useState(null);
  const [toggleHlth,setToggleHlth]= useState(0);
  const [currentId, setCurrentId] = useState(null);
  useEffect(()=>{ if(show) setShowBox(true); else setTimeout(()=>setShowBox(false),1200); },[show]);
  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!show||!studentId) return;
    setLoading(true); setErrors({}); setPicture(null);
    axios.get(API.studentGet(studentId))
      .then(r=>{
        if(!r.data?.length){toast.error("Aluno não encontrado.");onHide();return;}
        const d=r.data[0]; setCurrentId(d.ed_student_id); setCover(d.ed_student_picture?Hoot()+d.ed_student_picture:null);
        setToggleHlth(d.ed_student_health_problems*1);
        if(d.ed_student_father_is_guardion==1) setGuardion(1);
        else if(d.ed_student_mother_is_guardion==1) setGuardion(0);
        else if(d.ed_student_guardion_is_guardion==1) setGuardion(2);
        else setGuardion(null);
        setForm({
          student_name:d.ed_student_name||"",student_code:d.ed_student_code||"",
          student_identityCard:d.ed_student_identityCard||"",
          student_birthday:d.ed_student_birthday?moment(d.ed_student_birthday).format("YYYY-MM-DD"):"",
          student_gender:d.ed_student_gender||"",student_naturalness:d.ed_student_naturalness||"",
          student_nacionality:d.ed_student_nacionality||"",student_address:d.ed_student_address||"",
          student_phone:d.ed_student_phone||"",student_email:d.ed_student_email||"",
          student_religion:d.ed_student_religion||"",
          student_scholarshipHolder:String(d.ed_student_scholarshipHolder??"0"),
          student_status:String(d.ed_student_status??"1"),student_class:d.ed_student_class||"",
          student_health_problems:String(d.ed_student_health_problems??"0"),
          student_health_problems_description:d.ed_student_health_problems_description||"",
          student_lastschool:d.ed_student_lastschool||"",student_lastclass:d.ed_student_lastclass||"",
          father_name:d.ed_student_father_name||"",father_phone:d.ed_student_father_phone||"",
          father_email:d.ed_student_father_email||"",father_job:d.ed_student_father_job||"",
          father_emergency_phone:d.ed_student_father_emergency_phone||"",father_address:d.ed_student_father_address||"",
          mother_name:d.ed_student_mother_name||"",mother_phone:d.ed_student_mother_phone||"",
          mother_email:d.ed_student_mother_email||"",mother_job:d.ed_student_mother_job||"",
          mother_emergency_phone:d.ed_student_mother_emergency_phone||"",mother_address:d.ed_student_mother_address||"",
          guardion_name:d.ed_student_guardion_name||"",guardion_phone:d.ed_student_guardion_phone||"",
          guardion_email:d.ed_student_guardion_email||"",guardion_job:d.ed_student_guardion_job||"",
          guardion_emergency_phone:d.ed_student_guardion_emergency_phone||"",guardion_address:d.ed_student_guardion_address||"",
          guardion_gender:String(d.ed_student_guardion_gender??"0"),guardion_degree_of_kinship:String(d.ed_student_guardion_degree??"0"),
        });
      })
      .catch(()=>toast.error("Erro ao carregar dados."))
      .finally(()=>setLoading(false));
  },[show,studentId]);
  const sf=(k,v)=>{setForm(p=>({...p,[k]:v}));if(errors[k])setErrors(p=>({...p,[k]:null}));};
  const hi=(e)=>{sf(e.target.id||e.target.name,e.target.value);if((e.target.id||e.target.name)==="student_health_problems")setToggleHlth(parseInt(e.target.value)||0);};
  const validate=()=>{const e={};if(!form.student_name?.trim())e.student_name="Campo obrigatório";if(!form.student_birthday?.trim())e.student_birthday="Campo obrigatório";if(!form.student_identityCard?.trim())e.student_identityCard="Campo obrigatório";if(!form.student_naturalness?.trim())e.student_naturalness="Campo obrigatório";return e;};
  const handleSave=async(e)=>{
    e?.preventDefault();const errs=validate();if(Object.keys(errs).length){setErrors(errs);toast.error("Verifique os campos obrigatórios!");return;}
    setSaving(true);
    try{
      const fd=new FormData();
      ['student_name','student_code','student_identityCard','student_birthday','student_gender','student_naturalness','student_nacionality','student_address','student_phone','student_email','student_religion','student_scholarshipHolder','student_status','student_class','student_health_problems','student_health_problems_description','student_lastschool','student_lastclass','father_name','father_phone','father_email','father_job','father_emergency_phone','father_address','mother_name','mother_phone','mother_email','mother_job','mother_emergency_phone','mother_address','guardion_name','guardion_phone','guardion_email','guardion_job','guardion_emergency_phone','guardion_address','guardion_gender','guardion_degree_of_kinship']
        .forEach(k=>fd.append(k,form[k]??""));
      fd.append('father_is_guardion',guardion===1?"1":"0");fd.append('mother_is_guardion',guardion===0?"1":"0");fd.append('guardion_is_guardion',guardion===2?"1":"0");
      if(picture)fd.append('student_picture',picture);
      await axios.put(API.studentUpdate(currentId||studentId),fd);
      toast.success("Dados actualizados!");onSaved?.();onHide();
    }catch(err){toast.error(err?.response?.data?.msg||"Erro ao guardar.");}
    finally{setSaving(false);}
  };
  const SECT=(label)=>(<div style={{fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:PURPLE_HEX,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1,marginBottom:8,marginTop:12,paddingBottom:4,borderBottom:"1px solid #e2e8f0"}}>{label}</div>);
  const PF=(prefix,title)=>(
    <div style={{marginBottom:8}}>{SECT(title)}
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:8}}>
        {[["Nome","name","text"],["Telefone","phone","number"],["E-mail","email","email"],["Profissão","job","text"],["Tel. emergência","emergency_phone","number"],["Morada","address","text"]].map(([lbl,sf2,type])=>(
          <Form.Group key={sf2}><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>{lbl}</Form.Label>
            <Form.Control size="sm" type={type} id={`${prefix}_${sf2}`} name={`${prefix}_${sf2}`} value={form[`${prefix}_${sf2}`]||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <Modal className={showBox?ModalAnimationShow():ModalAnimationClose()} centered size="xl" dialogAs={DraggableModal} show={show} onHide={onHide} scrollable>
      <Modal.Header closeButton><Modal.Title><div className="ed-wrap align-center"><UserOptionDropDown/><h5>Editar Dados do Aluno</h5></div></Modal.Title></Modal.Header>
      <Form onSubmit={handleSave}>
        <Modal.Body className="scrollLimit">
          {loading?(<div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,padding:32,justifyContent:"center"}}><Spinner animation="border" size="sm"/> A carregar...</div>):(
            <>
              {SECT("Dados Pessoais")}
              <div className="ed-flex mb-3 gap-3">
                <ImagePreview Cover={cover} Picture={f=>setPicture(f)}/>
                <div className="block col">
                  <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:10}}>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Nº Matrícula</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_code" value={form.student_code||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Nome completo *</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_name" value={form.student_name||""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_name}/><Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{fontSize:10}}>{errors.student_name}</Form.Control.Feedback></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Nº B.I. *</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_identityCard" value={form.student_identityCard||""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_identityCard}/><Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{fontSize:10}}>{errors.student_identityCard}</Form.Control.Feedback></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Data de Nascimento *</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" type="date" id="student_birthday" value={form.student_birthday||""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_birthday}/><Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{fontSize:10}}>{errors.student_birthday}</Form.Control.Feedback></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Género</Form.Label><Form.Select size="sm" id="student_gender" value={form.student_gender||""} onChange={hi}><option value="">Seleccionar</option><option value="male">Masculino</option><option value="female">Feminino</option></Form.Select></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Telefone</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" type="number" id="student_phone" value={form.student_phone||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>E-mail</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" type="email" id="student_email" value={form.student_email||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Morada</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_address" value={form.student_address||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
                  </div>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2 mt-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Naturalidade *</Form.Label><NaturalnessSelect value={form.student_naturalness||""} onChange={v=>sf("student_naturalness",v)} isInvalid={!!errors.student_naturalness} errorMsg={errors.student_naturalness}/></Form.Group>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Nacionalidade</Form.Label><NationalitySelect value={form.student_nacionality||""} onChange={v=>sf("student_nacionality",v)}/></Form.Group>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2"><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Religião</Form.Label><Form.Select size="sm" id="student_religion" value={form.student_religion||""} onChange={hi}><option value="">Seleccionar</option><ReligionOptions/></Form.Select></Form.Group>
                </div>
              </div>
              {SECT("Dados Escolares")}
              <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:10,marginBottom:8}}>
                <Form.Group><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Turma</Form.Label><Form.Select size="sm" id="student_class" value={form.student_class||""} onChange={hi}><ClassDataOptions/></Form.Select></Form.Group>
                <Form.Group><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Estado</Form.Label><Form.Select size="sm" id="student_status" value={form.student_status??"1"} onChange={hi}><option value="1">Activo</option><option value="0">Inactivo</option></Form.Select></Form.Group>
                <Form.Group><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Última escola</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_lastschool" value={form.student_lastschool||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
                <Form.Group><Form.Label style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:"#64748b"}}>Última classe</Form.Label><Form.Control size="sm" id="student_lastclass" value={form.student_lastclass||""} onChange={hi}/></Form.Group>
              </div>
              {PF("father","Pai")}
              <FormControlLabel onChange={()=>setGuardion(guardion===1?null:1)} control={<Checkbox checked={guardion===1} size="small" sx={{color:PURPLE_RAW,"&.Mui-checked":{color:PURPLE_RAW}}}/>} label={<span style={{fontSize:12}}>Pai como encarregado principal</span>}/>
              {PF("mother","Mãe")}
              <FormControlLabel onChange={()=>setGuardion(guardion===0?null:0)} control={<Checkbox checked={guardion===0} size="small" sx={{color:PURPLE_RAW,"&.Mui-checked":{color:PURPLE_RAW}}}/>} label={<span style={{fontSize:12}}>Mãe como encarregada principal</span>}/>
              {PF("guardion","Responsável")}
              <FormControlLabel onChange={()=>setGuardion(guardion===2?null:2)} control={<Checkbox checked={guardion===2} size="small" sx={{color:PURPLE_RAW,"&.Mui-checked":{color:PURPLE_RAW}}}/>} label={<span style={{fontSize:12}}>Responsável como encarregado principal</span>}/>
            </>
          )}
        </Modal.Body>
        <Modal.Footer>
          <div className="ed-space"><div className="ed-flex"/><div className="ed-flex">
            <Button className="bg-light text-dark" onClick={onHide}>Cancelar</Button>
            <SaveButton icon={<Save/>} title="Guardar" status={saving?0:null}/>
          </div></div>
        </Modal.Footer>
      </Form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PDF EXPORT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
async function exportCertPDF({ students, certModel, overrides, studentGrades, headerLines, logoUrl, institute, columns, passValue=10, fontSize=11, borderMode="none", colorGrades=true }) {
  const PW=210,PH=297,M=14,IW=PW-M*2;
  // FIX: increased lateral margin for PDF
  const ML=18, MR=18, IW2=PW-ML-MR;
  const BK=[0,0,0];
  const pdf = new jsPDF({orientation:"portrait",unit:"mm",format:"a4",compress:true});
  const coatB64 = await loadImgB64(logoUrl||ANGOLA_COAT);
  const isPrimario = certModel === "primario";
  const pv = passValue;

  const drawHdr = (y) => {
    if (coatB64) { try { pdf.addImage(coatB64,"PNG",PW/2-8,y,16,20); } catch {} }
    y += 22;
    (headerLines||[]).forEach((hl,li)=>{
      pdf.setFont("helvetica",hl.bold?"bold":"normal"); pdf.setFontSize(hl.bold?8:7); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
      pdf.text(hl.text,PW/2,y+li*3.5,{align:"center",maxWidth:IW2-20});
    });
    return y+(headerLines||[]).length*3.5+3;
  };

  const rp=(segs,startY,lh=4.8)=>{
    let cx=ML,cy=startY;
    for(const seg of segs){
      pdf.setFont("helvetica",seg.bold?"bold":"normal"); pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(...(seg.color||BK));
      const words=String(seg.text||"").split(" ");
      for(let wi=0;wi<words.length;wi++){
        const word=wi<words.length-1?words[wi]+" ":words[wi]; const ww=pdf.getTextWidth(word);
        if(cx>ML&&cx+ww>ML+IW2){cy+=lh;cx=ML;}
        pdf.text(word,cx,cy); cx+=ww;
      }
    }
    return cy+lh+2;
  };

  for (let ri=0; ri<students.length; ri++) {
    if (ri>0) pdf.addPage([PW,PH],"portrait");
    const s   = students[ri];
    const ovr = overrides[s.student_id]||{};
    const d   = {...s,...ovr};
    const city = institute?.ed_institute_state||"Luanda";
    const studentName   = d.student_name||s.label||"N/D";
    const fatherName    = d.father_name||"N/D";
    const motherName    = d.mother_name||"N/D";
    const naturalness   = d.student_naturalness||"N/D";
    const bi            = d.student_identityCard||"N/D";
    const bday          = d.student_birthday_text||(d.student_birthday?formatDatePT(d.student_birthday):"N/D");
    const municipality  = d.municipality||"";
    const province      = d.province||city;
    const nationality   = d.student_nationality||d.student_nacionality||"";
    const biDate        = d.bi_issued_date||"";
    const academicYear  = d.academic_year_override||d.academic_year||s.academic_year||"N/D";
    const cycleName     = d.cycle_name||(isPrimario?"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO":"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO GERAL");
    const schoolFN      = d.school_full_name||institute?.ed_institute_name||"";
    const dirName       = d.director_name||institute?.ed_institute_principal||"";
    const decree        = d.decree_number||"";
    const decreeDate    = d.decree_date||"";
    const regNum        = d.reg_number||"___";
    const regFolha      = d.reg_folha||"___";
    const sigLeft       = d.sig_left_name||"";
    const sigRight      = d.sig_right_name||dirName;
    const subtitle      = d.cert_subtitle||(isPrimario?"ENSINO PRIMÁRIO":"I CICLO DO ENSINO SECUNDÁRIO");
    const certTitle     = d.cert_title||"CERTIFICADO";
    const locFull       = d.school_location_full||city;
    const schoolArt     = isPrimario?"a":"o";
    const cols          = columns||[];

    drawPdfBorder(pdf,borderMode,PW,PH,M);
    let y = M+(borderMode&&borderMode!=="none"?10:0);
    y = drawHdr(y);

    pdf.setFont("helvetica","normal"); pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    // FIX: more vertical gap between subtitle and title
    pdf.text(subtitle,PW/2,y,{align:"center"}); y+=7;
    pdf.setFont("helvetica","bold"); pdf.setFontSize(14);
    pdf.text(certTitle,PW/2,y,{align:"center"});
    const tw=pdf.getTextWidth(certTitle); pdf.setLineWidth(0.4);
    pdf.line(PW/2-tw/2,y+0.8,PW/2+tw/2,y+0.8);
    y+=5; pdf.setLineWidth(0.3); y+=5;

    y=rp([{text:`  a) `,bold:false},{text:dirName,bold:true},{text:`, Director${isPrimario?" d":""}${schoolArt} `,bold:false},{text:schoolFN,bold:true},{text:`, criada sob Decreto Executivo nº ${decree} de ${decreeDate}.`,bold:false}],y);

    y=rp([{text:`  Certifica que `,bold:false},{text:studentName,bold:true,color:RED_RGB},{text:`, filho(a) de `,bold:false},{text:fatherName,bold:true},{text:` e de `,bold:false},{text:motherName,bold:true},{text:`, nascido(a) a ${bday}, natural de ${naturalness}, Município de ${municipality}, Província de ${province}, nacionalidade ${nationality}, portador(a) do B.I ou Cédula nº `,bold:false},{text:bi,bold:true},{text:`, emitido pela Direcção Nacional de Identificação aos ${biDate}.`,bold:false}],y);

    // Calcular média final — FIX: suporta override manual
    const stuGrades = studentGrades?.[s.student_id]||[];
    const allM = stuGrades.filter(g=>!isNoGradeSubject(g.name)).map(g=>calcMedia(g.grades,cols)).filter(v=>v!==null);
    const mFinalCalc = allM.length>0 ? Math.ceil(allM.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/allM.length) : null;
    const mFinal = (d.media_override !== undefined && d.media_override !== "")
      ? parseInt(d.media_override)||0
      : (mFinalCalc !== null ? mFinalCalc : 0);
    const mColor = gcRGB(mFinal,pv,colorGrades);

    const lawRef = isPrimario
      ? "conforme o disposto na alínea b) do artigo 109º da LBSEE 17/16 de 7 de Outubro, com a"
      : "conforme o disposto na alínea c) do artigo 109º da LBSEE nº 17/16, de 07 de Outubro, com a";

    y=rp([{text:`  Concluiu no Ano Lectivo de `,bold:false},{text:academicYear,bold:true},{text:`, o `,bold:false},{text:cycleName,bold:true},{text:`, ${lawRef} Média Final de `,bold:false},{text:String(mFinal),bold:true,color:mColor},{text:` Valores, obtida nas seguintes classificações por ciclos de aprendizagem:`,bold:false}],y);

    // Tabela
    if (stuGrades.length>0&&cols.length>0) {
      const headCols = [
        {content:"Disciplina",styles:{halign:"left",fontStyle:"bold",fontSize:7}},
        ...cols.map(c=>({content:c.label+(c.sublabel?`\n${c.sublabel}`:""),styles:{halign:"center",fontStyle:"bold",fontSize:6.5}})),
        {content:"Média final",styles:{halign:"center",fontStyle:"bold",fontSize:6.5}},
        {content:"Média por extenso",styles:{halign:"center",fontStyle:"bold",fontSize:6.5}},
      ];
      const tableBody = stuGrades.map(g=>{
        const noGrade = isNoGradeSubject(g.name);
        const row=[{content:g.name,styles:{halign:"left",fontSize:7,fontStyle:noGrade?"normal":"normal"}}];
        cols.forEach(col=>{
          const v=noGrade?"—":(g.grades?.[col.id]??"");
          const n=parseFloat(v);
          row.push({content:String(v),styles:{halign:"center",fontSize:7,textColor:(!noGrade&&v!==""&&!isNaN(n))?gcRGB(n,pv,colorGrades):BK}});
        });
        const media=noGrade?null:calcMedia(g.grades,cols);
        const mRnd=media!==null?Math.ceil(media):null;
        row.push({content:noGrade?"—":(mRnd!==null?String(mRnd):""),styles:{halign:"center",fontStyle:"bold",fontSize:7,textColor:(!noGrade&&mRnd!==null)?gcRGB(mRnd,pv,colorGrades):BK}});
        row.push({content:noGrade?"—":(mRnd!==null?gradeWord(mRnd):""),styles:{halign:"center",fontSize:6.5,textColor:(!noGrade&&mRnd!==null)?gcRGB(mRnd,pv,colorGrades):BK}});
        return row;
      });
      autoTable(pdf,{
        startY:y, margin:{left:ML,right:MR}, tableWidth:IW2,
        head:[headCols], body:tableBody, theme:"grid",
        styles:{fontSize:7,cellPadding:1.5,lineColor:BK,lineWidth:0.25,textColor:BK,font:"helvetica"},
        headStyles:{fillColor:[248,248,248],textColor:BK,fontStyle:"bold"},
      });
      y=pdf.lastAutoTable.finalY+5;
    }

    y=rp([{text:`Para efeitos legais lhe é passado o presente `,bold:false},{text:"CERTIFICADO",bold:true},{text:`, que consta no livro de registo nº `,bold:false},{text:regNum,bold:true,color:RED_RGB},{text:`, folha, `,bold:false},{text:regFolha,bold:true,color:RED_RGB},{text:`, assinado por mim e autenticado com o carimbo a óleo em uso neste estabelecimento de ensino.`,bold:false}],y);

    pdf.setFont("helvetica","normal"); pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    pdf.text(`${locFull}, aos ${todayPT()}`,PW/2,y,{align:"center"}); y+=12;

    // FIX: assinaturas centradas no PDF
    const sigAreaWidth = 180;
    const sigGap = 20;
    const sigLeftWidth = 55;
    const sigRightWidth = 60;
    const totalSigW = sigLeftWidth + sigGap + sigRightWidth;
    const sigStartX = PW/2 - totalSigW/2;

    pdf.setFontSize(8);
    // Labels centrados sobre cada linha
    pdf.text("Conferido por", sigStartX + sigLeftWidth/2, y, {align:"center"});
    pdf.text("O Director", sigStartX + sigLeftWidth + sigGap + sigRightWidth/2, y, {align:"center"});
    y+=9;
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.line(sigStartX, y, sigStartX+sigLeftWidth, y);
    pdf.line(sigStartX+sigLeftWidth+sigGap, y, sigStartX+sigLeftWidth+sigGap+sigRightWidth, y);
    y+=4;
    pdf.setFontSize(7.5);
    if(sigLeft)  pdf.text(sigLeft,  sigStartX + sigLeftWidth/2,                       y, {align:"center"});
    if(sigRight) pdf.text(sigRight, sigStartX + sigLeftWidth + sigGap + sigRightWidth/2, y, {align:"center"});
  }

  pdf.save(`eduall-certificados-${certModel}-${moment().format("YYYY-MM-DD")}.pdf`);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   HISTORY PANEL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function HistoryPanel({ history, loading, onLoadSingle, onExportSinglePdf }) {
  const [search,    setSearch]    = useState("");
  const [loadingId, setLoadingId] = useState(null);

  const flatRows = useMemo(()=>{
    const rows=[];
    history.forEach(h=>{
      let students=[],grades={},ovrs={};
      try{students=JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_students||"[]");}catch{}
      try{grades  =JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_grades  ||"{}");}catch{}
      try{ovrs    =JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_overrides||"{}");}catch{}
      if(students.length===0){
        rows.push({_id:`${h.ed_cert_id}_s`,_entry:h,_studentData:null,_grades:{},_overrides:{}});
      } else {
        students.forEach((s,idx)=>{
          const sid=s.student_id||s.value;
          rows.push({_id:`${h.ed_cert_id}_${idx}`,_entry:h,_studentData:s,_grades:grades[sid]||[],_overrides:ovrs[sid]||{}});
        });
      }
    });
    return rows;
  },[history]);

  const filtered = useMemo(()=>{
    if(!search.trim()) return flatRows;
    const q=search.toLowerCase();
    return flatRows.filter(r=>{
      const name=(r._studentData?.student_name||r._studentData?.label||"").toLowerCase();
      const turma=(r._entry.ed_cert_class_title||"").toLowerCase();
      return name.includes(q)||turma.includes(q);
    });
  },[flatRows,search]);

  const handleExport=async(row)=>{ setLoadingId(row._id); try{await onExportSinglePdf(row);}finally{setLoadingId(null);} };
  const picSrc=(row)=>{ const p=row._studentData?.picture; if(!p)return null; return p.startsWith("http")?p:Hoot()+p; };
  const sName =(row)=>row._studentData?.student_name||row._studentData?.label||(row._entry.ed_cert_student_names||"").split(",")[0]?.trim()||"—";

  return (
    <div className="cert-panel">
      <div className="cert-phdr">
        <div className="cert-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineHistory size={14}/></div>
        <span className="cert-pttl">Histórico de Certificados</span>
        <span style={{marginLeft:"auto",fontSize:11,color:"#94a3b8",fontFamily:F_SANS}}>
          {flatRows.length} certificado{flatRows.length!==1?"s":""} · {history.length} registo{history.length!==1?"s":""}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="cert-pbody-sm" style={{borderBottom:"1px solid #f1f5f9"}}>
        <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
          <MdOutlineSearch size={15} style={{position:"absolute",left:10,top:"50%",transform:"translateY(-50%)",color:"#94a3b8"}}/>
          <input value={search} onChange={e=>setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Pesquisar por nome ou turma..."
            style={{width:"100%",padding:"8px 12px 8px 32px",border:"1.5px solid #e2e8f0",borderRadius:10,fontSize:12.5,fontFamily:F_SANS,outline:"none",boxSizing:"border-box",color:"#0f172a"}}
            onFocus={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=PURPLE_HEX} onBlur={e=>e.target.style.borderColor="#e2e8f0"}/>
          {search&&<button onClick={()=>setSearch("")} style={{position:"absolute",right:10,top:"50%",transform:"translateY(-50%)",background:"none",border:"none",cursor:"pointer",color:"#94a3b8",fontSize:16,padding:0}}>×</button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{padding:0}}>
        {loading?(
          <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,padding:"24px 20px",color:"#64748b",fontSize:13,fontFamily:F_SANS}}>
            <Spinner animation="border" size="sm"/> A carregar histórico...
          </div>
        ):filtered.length===0?(
          <div style={{padding:"32px 20px",textAlign:"center",color:"#94a3b8",fontSize:13,fontFamily:F_SANS}}>
            <div style={{width:44,height:44,borderRadius:12,background:"#f8fafc",border:"1.5px solid #e2e8f0",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",margin:"0 auto 10px"}}><MdOutlineHistory size={20} color="#cbd5e1"/></div>
            {search?"Nenhum certificado encontrado.":"Nenhum certificado guardado ainda."}
          </div>
        ):(
          <div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"36px 1fr 120px 90px 100px 140px",gap:0,padding:"8px 16px",background:"#f8fafc",borderBottom:"1px solid #e2e8f0",fontSize:10.5,fontWeight:700,color:"#64748b",textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:0.5,fontFamily:F_SANS,position:"sticky",top:0,zIndex:2}}>
              <div>#</div><div>Aluno</div><div>Turma</div><div>Modelo</div><div>Data</div><div style={{textAlign:"center"}}>Acções</div>
            </div>
            <div style={{maxHeight:600,overflowY:"auto",overflowX:"hidden"}}>
              {filtered.map((row,idx)=>{
                const h=row._entry; const name=sName(row); const code=row._studentData?.student_code||"—";
                const pic=picSrc(row); const missing=row._studentData?getMissingFields({...row._studentData,...row._overrides}):[];
                const isLoad=loadingId===row._id;
                return (
                  <div key={row._id}
                    style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"36px 1fr 120px 90px 100px 140px",gap:0,padding:"9px 16px",borderBottom:"1px solid #f1f5f9",background:idx%2===0?"#fff":"#fafafa",alignItems:"center",fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:12.5,transition:"background .1s"}}
                    onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background="rgba(108,63,197,.03)"}
                    onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=idx%2===0?"#fff":"#fafafa"}>
                    <div style={{color:"#94a3b8",fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{idx+1}</div>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:9,minWidth:0}}>
                      <StudentAvatar src={pic} name={name} size={30} className="cert-hist-avatar"/>
                      <div style={{minWidth:0}}>
                        <div style={{fontWeight:700,color:"#0f172a",fontSize:12.5,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>
                          {name}
                          {missing.length>0&&<span style={{marginLeft:6,display:"inline-flex",alignItems:"center",gap:2,padding:"1px 6px",background:"#fffbeb",border:"1px solid #fde68a",borderRadius:999,fontSize:9.5,color:"#92400e",fontWeight:600,verticalAlign:"middle"}}><MdOutlineWarningAmber size={9}/> {missing.length} em falta</span>}
                        </div>
                        {code!=="—"&&<div style={{fontSize:10.5,color:"#94a3b8",marginTop:1}}>Matrícula: {code}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:500,color:"#334155",overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{h.ed_cert_class_title||"—"}</div>
                    <div>
                      <span style={{padding:"3px 8px",borderRadius:999,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,background:h.ed_cert_model==="primario"?"rgba(34,160,107,.08)":"rgba(108,63,197,.08)",color:h.ed_cert_model==="primario"?"#16a34a":PURPLE_RAW,border:`1px solid ${h.ed_cert_model==="primario"?"rgba(22,163,74,.2)":"rgba(108,63,197,.2)"}`}}>
                        {h.ed_cert_model==="primario"?"Primário":"Secundário"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:11,color:"#64748b",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:4}}>
                      <MdOutlineCalendarToday size={11}/>{h.ed_cert_updated_at?new Date(h.ed_cert_updated_at).toLocaleDateString("pt-PT"):"—"}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,justifyContent:"center"}}>
                      <button onClick={()=>onLoadSingle(row)} title="Editar"
                        style={{padding:"4px 10px",border:"1.5px solid #e2e8f0",borderRadius:7,background:"#fff",color:"#475569",cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:4,fontFamily:F_SANS,fontWeight:600,transition:"all .12s"}}
                        onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=PURPLE_HEX;e.currentTarget.style.color=PURPLE_RAW;}}
                        onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor="#e2e8f0";e.currentTarget.style.color="#475569";}}>
                        <Edit style={{fontSize:12}}/> Editar
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={()=>handleExport(row)} disabled={isLoad}
                        style={{padding:"4px 10px",border:`1.5px solid ${PURPLE_HEX}`,borderRadius:7,background:isLoad?`rgba(108,63,197,.7)`:PURPLE_HEX,color:"#fff",cursor:isLoad?"not-allowed":"pointer",fontSize:11,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:4,fontFamily:F_SANS,fontWeight:600,minWidth:64,justifyContent:"center",transition:"filter .12s"}}
                        onMouseEnter={e=>{if(!isLoad)e.currentTarget.style.filter="brightness(1.12)";}}
                        onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.filter="none";}}>
                        {isLoad?<><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{width:11,height:11,borderWidth:2}}/> A gerar</>:<><PiFileDuotone size={12}/> PDF</>}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {!loading&&filtered.length>0&&(
        <div style={{padding:"10px 16px",background:"#f8fafc",borderTop:"1px solid #f1f5f9",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",fontFamily:F_SANS}}>
          <span style={{fontSize:11,color:"#94a3b8"}}>A mostrar {filtered.length} de {flatRows.length}</span>
          <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-p" style={{fontSize:10}}>{history.length} registo{history.length!==1?"s":""} em arquivo</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UI CSS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const UI_CSS = `
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');
.cert-root*,.cert-root*::before,.cert-root*::after{box-sizing:border-box;}
.cert-root{font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;}
.cert-topbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-bottom:22px;}
.cert-topbar-icon{width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:12px;background:${PURPLE_HEX};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(108,63,197,.3);}
.cert-topbar-title{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;}
.cert-topbar-sub{font-size:12.5px;color:#64748b;margin-top:1px;}
.cert-panel{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;margin-bottom:16px;overflow:hidden;}
.cert-phdr{padding:13px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid #f1f5f9;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;}
.cert-phdr-icon{width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;background:#f8fafc;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#475569;flex-shrink:0;}
.cert-pttl{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#334155;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;}
.cert-pbody{padding:16px 20px;}
.cert-pbody-sm{padding:12px 20px;}
.cert-row{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.cert-col{flex:1;min-width:140px;}
.cert-lbl{font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;}
.cert-divider{height:1px;background:#f1f5f9;margin:0;}
.cert-pills{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.cert-pill{padding:7px 16px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;border:1.5px solid #e2e8f0;background:#f8fafc;color:#64748b;transition:all .15s;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;}
.cert-pill:hover{border-color:#cbd5e1;color:#1e293b;background:#f1f5f9;}
.cert-pill.pp{background:${PURPLE_HEX};border-color:${PURPLE_HEX};color:#fff;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(108,63,197,.30);}

.cert-btn-sec:hover:not(:disabled){border-color:${PURPLE_HEX};color:${PURPLE_HEX};background:#f5f0ff;}
.cert-btn-sec:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;}
.cert-btn-sec.active{background:#f59e0b;border-color:#f59e0b;color:#fff;}

.cert-btn-pri:hover:not(:disabled){filter:brightness(1.1);transform:translateY(-1px);}
.cert-btn-pri:disabled{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;transform:none;}

.cert-btn-warn:hover{background:#fef3c7;border-color:#d97706;}
.cert-prev-area{background:var(--ed-background-color,#e8e8e8);border-radius:14px;padding:24px;border:1px solid #d4d4d4;}
.cert-prev-flex{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:24px;align-items:flex-start;}
.cert-doc-thumb{width:298px;height:421px;position:relative;flex-shrink:0;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;border-radius:2px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.22);}
.cert-doc-scaler{transform-origin:top left;transform:scale(0.47);position:absolute;top:0;left:0;pointer-events:none;}

.cert-thumb-warn{position:absolute;top:5px;right:5px;background:#f59e0b;color:#fff;border-radius:50%;width:18px;height:18px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;}
.cert-edit-area{background:var(--ed-background-color,#e8e8e8);border-radius:14px;padding:24px;border:1px solid #d4d4d4;}

.cert-doc-full{display:block;width:210mm;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18);border-radius:4px;flex-shrink:0;}

.cert-edit-banner-icon{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;background:#f59e0b;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;}

.cert-empty{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:52px 20px;gap:10px;text-align:center;}
.cert-empty-icon{width:56px;height:56px;border-radius:16px;background:rgba(108,63,197,.07);border:1.5px solid rgba(108,63,197,.18);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:${PURPLE_HEX};margin-bottom:4px;}
.cert-student-label{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#334155;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
.cert-student-num{width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;background:${PURPLE_HEX};color:#fff;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;}
.cert-hist-avatar{flex-shrink:0 !important;transition:transform .15s !important;}
.cert-hist-avatar:hover{transform:scale(1.12) !important;}
