/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MAIN COMPONENT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export default function PdCertificates() {
  document.title = "Certificados Escolares";

  const [allClasses,       setAllClasses]       = useState([]);
  const [selectedClass,    setSelectedClass]    = useState(null);
  const [studentsOptions,  setStudentsOptions]  = useState([]);
  const [selectedStudents, setSelectedStudents] = useState([]);
  const [loadingStudents,  setLoadingStudents]  = useState(false);

  const [certModel,      setCertModel]      = useState("primario");
  const [fontSize,       setFontSize]       = useState(11);
  const [logoUrl,        setLogoUrl]        = useState("");
  const [logoFile,       setLogoFile]       = useState(null);
  const [institute,      setInstitute]      = useState(null);
  const [allSubjects,    setAllSubjects]    = useState([]);
  const [allAcLevels,    setAllAcLevels]    = useState([]);
  const [tableHeaders,   setTableHeaders]   = useState({ th_disc:"Disciplina", th_media_f:"Média final", th_extenso:"Média por extenso" });
  const [passValue,      setPassValue]      = useState(10);
  const [passValueAuto,  setPassValueAuto]  = useState(true);
  const [borderMode,     setBorderMode]     = useState("none");
  const [colorGrades,    setColorGrades]    = useState(true);
  const [headerLines,    setHeaderLines]    = useState([]);
  const [subjects,       setSubjects]       = useState([]);
  const [columns,        setColumns]        = useState(DEFAULT_COLS_PRIMARIO);
  const [studentGrades,  setStudentGrades]  = useState({});
  const [overrides,      setOverrides]      = useState({});
  const [removedBlocks,  setRemovedBlocks]  = useState({});
  const [editMode,       setEditMode]       = useState(false);
  const [fullEditMode,   setFullEditMode]   = useState(false);
  const [activeIdx,      setActiveIdx]      = useState(0);
  const [exporting,      setExporting]      = useState(false);
  const [exportSaving,   setExportSaving]   = useState(false);
  const [saving,         setSaving]         = useState(false);
  const [currentCertId,  setCurrentCertId]  = useState(null);
  const [modalOpen,      setModalOpen]      = useState(false);
  const [modalStudentId, setModalStudentId] = useState(null);
  const [secHdr,         setSecHdr]         = useState(false);
  const [secGrades,      setSecGrades]      = useState(false);

  const [history,        setHistory]        = useState([]);
  const [historyLoading, setHistoryLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleModelChange = useCallback((model) => {
    setCertModel(model);
    setColumns(model === "primario" ? DEFAULT_COLS_PRIMARIO : DEFAULT_COLS_SECUNDARIO);
    setSubjects((model === "primario" ? PRESET_SUBJ_PRIMARIO : PRESET_SUBJ_SECUNDARIO)
      .map((n,i) => ({ id: `d${i}`, name: n })));
    setPassValue(model === "primario" ? 10 : 20);
    setPassValueAuto(false);
  }, []);

  const loadHistory = useCallback(()=>{
    setHistoryLoading(true);
    axios.get(API.certs()).then(r=>setHistory(Array.isArray(r.data)?r.data:[])).catch(()=>setHistory([])).finally(()=>setHistoryLoading(false));
  },[]);

  useEffect(()=>{
    loadHistory();
    setSubjects(PRESET_SUBJ_PRIMARIO.map((n,i)=>({id:`d${i}`,name:n})));

    axios.get(API.classes()).then(r=>{
      const list=Array.isArray(r.data)?r.data.map(c=>({
        value:c.ed_class_id,label:c.ed_class_title,
        period:PERIODS[Math.floor(c.ed_class_period)]||String(c.ed_class_period||""),
        academic_year:c.ed_academic_year_title,grade_raw:c.ed_academic_level_title,
      })):[];
      setAllClasses(list);
    }).catch(()=>setAllClasses([]));

    axios.get(API.institute()).then(r=>{
      if(r.data?.length){
        const inst=r.data[0]; setInstitute(inst);
        setHeaderLines([
          {text:"REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA",bold:false},
          {text:"MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO",bold:false},
          ...(inst.ed_institute_state?[{text:inst.ed_institute_state.toUpperCase(),bold:false}]:[]),
          {text:(inst.ed_institute_name||"").toUpperCase(),bold:true},
        ]);
      }
    }).catch(()=>setHeaderLines([{text:"REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA",bold:false},{text:"MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO",bold:false},{text:"NOME DA ESCOLA",bold:true}]));

    axios.get(API.subjects()).then(r=>{ if(Array.isArray(r.data)) setAllSubjects(r.data.map(s=>({id:s.ed_subject_id,title:s.ed_subject_title}))); }).catch(()=>{});
    axios.get(API.academicLevels()).then(r=>{ if(Array.isArray(r.data)) setAllAcLevels(r.data.map(l=>({id:l.ed_academic_level_id,title:l.ed_academic_level_title}))); }).catch(()=>{});
  },[]);

  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!passValueAuto||!selectedClass) return;
    setPassValue(detectPassValue(selectedClass.grade_raw||selectedClass.label));
  },[selectedClass,passValueAuto]);

  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!selectedClass){setStudentsOptions([]);setSelectedStudents([]);return;}
    setLoadingStudents(true);
    axios.get(API.studentsByClass(selectedClass.value)).then(r=>{
      const raw=Array.isArray(r.data)?r.data:r.data?.data||[];
      const opts=raw.map(s=>({
        value:s.ed_student_id,label:s.ed_student_name,picture:s.ed_student_picture||null,
        student_id:s.ed_student_id,student_name:s.ed_student_name,
        student_birthday:s.ed_student_birthday,student_identityCard:s.ed_student_identityCard,
        student_naturalness:s.ed_student_naturalness,student_gender:s.ed_student_gender,
        student_code:s.ed_student_code,father_name:s.ed_student_father_name,
        mother_name:s.ed_student_mother_name,class_title:selectedClass.label,
        academic_year:selectedClass.academic_year,
      }));
      setStudentsOptions(opts);setSelectedStudents(opts);setActiveIdx(0);
    }).catch(()=>setStudentsOptions([])).finally(()=>setLoadingStudents(false));
  },[selectedClass]);

  useEffect(()=>{
    setStudentGrades(prev=>{
      const next={...prev};
      selectedStudents.forEach(s=>{
        if(!next[s.value]){
          next[s.value]=subjects.map(subj=>({id:subj.id,name:subj.name,grades:{}}));
        } else {
          next[s.value]=subjects.map(subj=>{
            const ex=next[s.value].find(g=>g.id===subj.id);
            return ex?{...ex,name:subj.name}:{id:subj.id,name:subj.name,grades:{}};
          });
        }
      });
      return next;
    });
  },[selectedStudents,subjects]);

  useEffect(()=>{ if(!logoFile) return; const r=new FileReader(); r.onload=ev=>setLogoUrl(ev.target.result); r.readAsDataURL(logoFile); },[logoFile]);

  const handleOverride=(sid,f,v)=>setOverrides(prev=>({...prev,[sid]:{...(prev[sid]||{}),[f]:v}}));
  const handleGradeChange=(sid,subjId,colId,val)=>setStudentGrades(prev=>({...prev,[sid]:(prev[sid]||[]).map(g=>g.id===subjId?{...g,grades:{...g.grades,[colId]:val}}:g)}));
  const handleToggleBlock=(sid,id)=>setRemovedBlocks(prev=>{const cur=prev[sid]||[];const i=cur.indexOf(id);return {...prev,[sid]:i>=0?cur.filter(b=>b!==id):[...cur,id]};});

  const buildPayload=useCallback(()=>({
    class_id:selectedClass?.value||null,class_title:selectedClass?.label||null,
    academic_year:selectedClass?.academic_year||null,cert_model:certModel,
    pass_value:passValue,font_size:fontSize,border_mode:borderMode,
    logo_url:logoUrl||null,header_lines:headerLines,subjects,columns,
    table_headers:tableHeaders,
    students:selectedStudents,student_grades:studentGrades,overrides,
    student_names:selectedStudents.map(s=>s.student_name||s.label),
    student_codes:selectedStudents.map(s=>s.student_code||""),
  }),[selectedStudents,selectedClass,certModel,passValue,fontSize,borderMode,logoUrl,headerLines,subjects,columns,studentGrades,overrides]);

  const handleSave=useCallback(async()=>{
    if(!selectedStudents.length) return; setSaving(true);
    try{
      const payload=buildPayload();
      if(currentCertId){await axios.put(API.certUpd(currentCertId),payload);toast.success("Certificado actualizado!");}
      else{const r=await axios.post(API.certSave(),payload);setCurrentCertId(r.data?.inserted_id||null);toast.success("Certificado guardado!");}
      loadHistory();
    }catch{toast.error("Erro ao guardar.");}
    finally{setSaving(false);}
  },[buildPayload,currentCertId,loadHistory,selectedStudents]);

  const handleLoadSingle=useCallback((row)=>{
    try{
      const h=row._entry; const s=row._studentData;
      const ovr=row._overrides||{}; const grd=row._grades||[];
      const headerLines_=JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_header_lines||"[]");
      const subjects_   =JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_subjects    ||"[]");
      const columns_    =JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_columns     ||"[]");
      let tHdrs = { th_disc:"Disciplina", th_media_f:"Média final", th_extenso:"Média por extenso" };
      try { if(h.ed_cert_table_headers) tHdrs = typeof h.ed_cert_table_headers==="string" ? JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_table_headers) : h.ed_cert_table_headers; } catch {}
      setHeaderLines(headerLines_); setSubjects(subjects_); setColumns(columns_); setTableHeaders(tHdrs);
      setLogoUrl(h.ed_cert_logo_url||""); setCertModel(h.ed_cert_model||"primario");
      setPassValue(h.ed_cert_pass_value||10); setFontSize(h.ed_cert_font_size||11);
      setBorderMode(h.ed_cert_border_mode||"none"); setCurrentCertId(h.ed_cert_id);
      setRemovedBlocks({});
      if(s){
        const so={...s,value:s.value||s.student_id,label:s.student_name||s.label,student_id:s.student_id||s.value};
        setSelectedStudents([so]); setStudentsOptions([so]);
        setStudentGrades({[so.value]:Array.isArray(grd)?grd:[]});
        setOverrides({[so.value]:ovr});
      }
      setActiveIdx(0); setEditMode(false); setFullEditMode(true);
      toast.success(`Certificado de ${s?.student_name||s?.label||"—"} carregado!`);
    }catch{ toast.error("Erro ao carregar certificado."); }
  },[]);

  const handleExportSinglePdf=useCallback(async(row)=>{
    const h=row._entry; const s=row._studentData; const ovr=row._overrides||{}; const grd=row._grades||[];
    let cols=[],hdls=[];
    try{cols=JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_columns||"[]");}catch{}
    try{hdls=JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_header_lines||"[]");}catch{}
    const sid=s?.student_id||s?.value;
    const student={student_id:sid,student_name:s?.student_name||s?.label||"—",student_birthday:s?.student_birthday,student_identityCard:s?.student_identityCard,student_naturalness:s?.student_naturalness,student_gender:s?.student_gender,student_code:s?.student_code||"—",father_name:s?.father_name,mother_name:s?.mother_name,class_title:h.ed_cert_class_title,academic_year:h.ed_cert_academic_year};
    let tHdrs2 = { th_disc:"Disciplina", th_media_f:"Média final", th_extenso:"Média por extenso" };
    try { if(h.ed_cert_table_headers) tHdrs2 = typeof h.ed_cert_table_headers==="string" ? JSON.parse(h.ed_cert_table_headers) : h.ed_cert_table_headers; } catch {}
    await exportCertPDF({
      students:[student],certModel:h.ed_cert_model||"primario",
      overrides:{[sid]:ovr},studentGrades:{[sid]:Array.isArray(grd)?grd:[]},
      headerLines:hdls,logoUrl:h.ed_cert_logo_url||null,institute,columns:cols,
      passValue:h.ed_cert_pass_value||10,fontSize:h.ed_cert_font_size||11,
      borderMode:h.ed_cert_border_mode||"none",colorGrades,
    });
    toast.success("PDF exportado!");
  },[institute,colorGrades]);

  const doExportPdf=useCallback(async()=>{
    if(!selectedStudents.length) return;
    const mapped=selectedStudents.map(s=>({
      student_id:s.value||s.student_id,student_name:s.student_name||s.label,
      student_birthday:s.student_birthday,student_identityCard:s.student_identityCard,
      student_naturalness:s.student_naturalness,student_gender:s.student_gender,
      student_code:s.student_code,father_name:s.father_name,mother_name:s.mother_name,
      class_title:selectedClass?.label,academic_year:selectedClass?.academic_year,
    }));
    await exportCertPDF({students:mapped,certModel,overrides,studentGrades,headerLines,logoUrl:logoUrl||null,institute,columns,tableHeaders,passValue,fontSize,borderMode,colorGrades});
  },[selectedStudents,certModel,overrides,studentGrades,headerLines,logoUrl,institute,columns,passValue,fontSize,borderMode,colorGrades,selectedClass]);

  const handleExportOnly=useCallback(async()=>{ setExporting(true); try{await doExportPdf();toast.success("PDF exportado!");}catch{toast.error("Erro ao exportar PDF.");}finally{setExporting(false);} },[doExportPdf]);

  const handleExportAndSave=useCallback(async()=>{
    if(!selectedStudents.length) return; setExportSaving(true);
    try{
      const payload=buildPayload();
      if(currentCertId) await axios.put(API.certUpd(currentCertId),payload);
      else{const r=await axios.post(API.certSave(),payload);setCurrentCertId(r.data?.inserted_id||null);}
      loadHistory(); await doExportPdf(); toast.success("Guardado e PDF exportado!");
    }catch{toast.error("Erro ao exportar e guardar.");}
    finally{setExportSaving(false);}
  },[buildPayload,currentCertId,doExportPdf,loadHistory,selectedStudents]);

  const handlePrint=()=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(`cert-doc-${activeIdx}`);if(!el) return;
    const win=window.open("","_blank");
    win.document.write(`<html><head><title>Certificado</title><style>body{margin:0;background:#fff;}@media print{body{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;}}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;}td,th{border:1px solid #999;padding:3px 6px;}</style></head><body>${el.innerHTML}</body></html>`);
    win.document.close(); setTimeout(()=>{win.focus();win.print();},400);
  };

  const classInfo=useMemo(()=>selectedClass?{academic_year:selectedClass.academic_year,period:selectedClass.period,title:selectedClass.label}:null,[selectedClass]);
  const current=selectedStudents[activeIdx];
  const curOvr=overrides[current?.value]||{};
  const missing=current?getMissingFields({...current,...curOvr}):[];
  const hasMissing=missing.length>0;

  const SubHdr=(label,open,setOpen)=>(
    <button onClick={()=>setOpen(!open)} style={{width:"100%",padding:"8px 0",background:"none",border:"none",cursor:"pointer",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:7,fontSize:11.5,fontWeight:600,color:"#334155",fontFamily:F_SANS}}>
      <span style={{opacity:.45,fontSize:9}}>{open?"▾":"▸"}</span>{label}
    </button>
  );

  const CertComponent = certModel === "primario" ? CertPrimario : CertSecundario;

  return (
    <DashboardContainerWrapper content={
      <div className="cert-root">
        <style>{UI_CSS}</style>

        {/* TOP BAR */}
        <div className="cert-topbar">
          <div className="cert-topbar-icon"><TbCertificate size={22} color="#fff"/></div>
          <div>
            <div className="cert-topbar-title">Certificados Escolares</div>
            <div className="cert-topbar-sub">Ensino Primário e Secundário · Todos os textos editáveis · Exportar ou Guardar</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* HISTÓRICO */}
        <HistoryPanel history={history} loading={historyLoading} onLoadSingle={handleLoadSingle} onExportSinglePdf={handleExportSinglePdf}/>

        {/* CONFIG PANEL */}
        <div className="cert-panel">
          <div className="cert-phdr">
            <div className="cert-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineTune size={14}/></div>
            <span className="cert-pttl">Configurações</span>
          </div>

          <div className="cert-pbody-sm">
            <div className="cert-lbl"><MdOutlineSchool size={13}/> Modelo de Certificado</div>
            <div className="cert-pills">
              {CERT_MODELS.map(m=>(
                <button key={m.value} className={`cert-pill${certModel===m.value?" pp":""}`}
                  onClick={()=>handleModelChange(m.value)}>
                  {certModel===m.value?<MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14}/>:<MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14}/>}
                  <span>
                    <span style={{fontWeight:600}}>{m.label}</span>
                    <span style={{fontSize:10,opacity:.75,marginLeft:5}}>{m.subtitle}</span>
                  </span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          <div className="cert-pbody">
            <div className="cert-row">
              <div className="cert-col">
                <div className="cert-lbl"><MdOutlineClass size={13}/> Turma</div>
                <Select options={allClasses} value={selectedClass}
                  onChange={cls=>{setSelectedClass(cls);setOverrides({});setRemovedBlocks({});setActiveIdx(0);setCurrentCertId(null);}}
                  placeholder="Pesquisar ou seleccionar turma..." isClearable isSearchable
                  noOptionsMessage={()=>"Nenhuma turma"} menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed" styles={SEL}/>
                {selectedClass&&(
                  <div style={{marginTop:6,display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
                    {selectedClass.academic_year&&<span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s" style={{fontSize:10}}>{selectedClass.academic_year}</span>}
                    {selectedClass.period&&<span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s" style={{fontSize:10}}>{selectedClass.period}</span>}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="cert-col" style={{flex:2}}>
                <div className="cert-lbl"><MdOutlinePersonSearch size={13}/> Alunos</div>
                {!selectedClass
                  ?<div style={{fontSize:12.5,color:"#94a3b8",padding:"10px 0"}}>Seleccione uma turma para carregar os alunos.</div>
                  :loadingStudents
                    ?<div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,padding:"10px 0",color:"#64748b",fontSize:13}}><Spinner size="sm" animation="border"/> A carregar alunos...</div>
                    :<StudentMultiSelect options={studentsOptions} value={selectedStudents} onChange={v=>{setSelectedStudents(v);setActiveIdx(0);}}/>
                }
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          <div className="cert-pbody">
            <div className="cert-row">
              <div className="cert-col">
                <div className="cert-lbl"><MdOutlineFormatSize size={13}/> Tamanho de fonte</div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
                  <input type="range" min={9} max={13} step={0.5} value={fontSize} onChange={e=>setFontSize(Number(e.target.value))} style={{flex:1,accentColor:PURPLE_RAW}}/>
                  <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,color:PURPLE_HEX,minWidth:28}}>{fontSize}pt</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="cert-col">
                <div className="cert-lbl"><MdOutlineImage size={13}/> Logótipo</div>
                <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
                  <div style={{padding:"9px 12px",border:"1.5px dashed #cbd5e1",borderRadius:10,background:"#f8fafc",fontSize:12.5,color:"#64748b",cursor:"pointer",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
                    <MdOutlineImage size={15}/>{logoFile?logoFile.name:"Carregar logótipo..."}
                  </div>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{position:"absolute",inset:0,opacity:0,cursor:"pointer"}} onChange={e=>setLogoFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)}/>
                </div>
                {logoUrl&&(
                  <div style={{marginTop:6,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
                    <img src={logoUrl} alt="" style={{width:32,height:32,objectFit:"contain",border:"1px solid #e2e8f0",borderRadius:6}}/>
                    <button onClick={()=>{setLogoUrl("");setLogoFile(null);}} style={{fontSize:11,color:"#ef4444",background:"none",border:"none",cursor:"pointer"}}>Remover</button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="cert-col">
                <div className="cert-lbl">Borda</div>
                <div className="cert-pills" style={{flexDirection:"column",gap:5}}>
                  {[["none","Sem borda"],["single","Borda simples"],["double","Borda dupla"],["certificate","Borda certificado"]].map(([val,lbl])=>(
                    <button key={val} className={`cert-pill${borderMode===val?" pp":""}`} style={{fontSize:11.5,padding:"5px 12px"}} onClick={()=>setBorderMode(val)}>
                      {borderMode===val?<MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/>:<MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>} {lbl}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          <div className="cert-pbody-sm">
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:20,flexWrap:"wrap",alignItems:"flex-start"}}>
              <div>
                <div className="cert-lbl" style={{marginBottom:8}}>Critério de avaliação</div>
                <div className="cert-pills">
                  {[[10,"0 — 10"],[20,"0 — 20"]].map(([val,lbl])=>(
                    <button key={val} className={`cert-pill${passValue===val&&!passValueAuto?" pp":""}`}
                      onClick={()=>{setPassValue(val);setPassValueAuto(false);}}>
                      {passValue===val&&!passValueAuto?<MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/>:<MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>} {lbl}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                  <button className={`cert-pill${passValueAuto?" pp":""}`} onClick={()=>setPassValueAuto(true)}>
                    {passValueAuto?<MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/>:<MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>}
                    Auto ({passValue===10?"0-10":"0-20"})
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="cert-lbl" style={{marginBottom:8}}><MdOutlineColorLens size={13}/> Cor das notas</div>
                <label style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,cursor:"pointer",padding:"8px 14px",border:`1.5px solid ${colorGrades?PURPLE_HEX:"#e2e8f0"}`,borderRadius:10,background:colorGrades?`rgba(108,63,197,.06)`:"#f8fafc",transition:"all .15s",userSelect:"none",fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:12.5}}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={colorGrades} onChange={e=>setColorGrades(e.target.checked)} style={{width:15,height:15,accentColor:PURPLE_RAW}}/>
                  <span style={{fontWeight:600,color:colorGrades?PURPLE_RAW:"#64748b"}}>Colorir notas por critério</span>
                  <span style={{display:"flex",gap:4,alignItems:"center"}}>
                    <span style={{width:10,height:10,borderRadius:"50%",background:colorGrades?BLUE_POS_CSS:"#94a3b8",display:"inline-block"}}/>
                    <span style={{fontSize:10,color:colorGrades?BLUE_POS_CSS:"#94a3b8",fontWeight:700}}>Positiva</span>
                    <span style={{width:10,height:10,borderRadius:"50%",background:colorGrades?RED_CSS:"#94a3b8",display:"inline-block",marginLeft:4}}/>
                    <span style={{fontSize:10,color:colorGrades?RED_CSS:"#94a3b8",fontWeight:700}}>Negativa</span>
                  </span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          <div className="cert-pbody-sm">
            {SubHdr("Cabeçalho do Documento", secHdr, setSecHdr)}
            {secHdr&&<HeaderEditor headerLines={headerLines} onChange={setHeaderLines}/>}
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          <div className="cert-pbody-sm">
            {SubHdr("Disciplinas, Colunas e Cabeçalhos da Tabela", secGrades, setSecGrades)}
            {secGrades&&(
              <GradesColumnsPanel
                subjects={subjects} onSubjectsChange={setSubjects}
                columns={columns} onColumnsChange={setColumns}
                tableHeaders={tableHeaders} onTableHeadersChange={setTableHeaders}
                allSubjects={allSubjects} allAcLevels={allAcLevels}/>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="cert-divider"/>

          {(editMode||fullEditMode)&&(
            <div className="cert-edit-banner">
              <div className="cert-edit-banner-icon"><MdOutlineEditNote size={13} color="#fff"/></div>
              <span>{fullEditMode?"Edição completa activa — clique em qualquer texto para editar. Use × vermelho para remover blocos.":"Modo de edição de notas activo."}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="cert-action-bar">
            <div className="cert-stats">
              {(exporting||exportSaving||saving)?(
                <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-y">
                  <Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{width:12,height:12,borderWidth:2}}/>
                  {exporting?" A exportar...":exportSaving?" A guardar e exportar...":" A guardar..."}
                </span>
              ):selectedStudents.length>0&&(
                <>
                  <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-p">{selectedStudents.length} aluno{selectedStudents.length!==1?"s":""}</span>
                  <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s">{CERT_MODELS.find(m=>m.value===certModel)?.label}</span>
                  <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s">{subjects.length} disciplina{subjects.length!==1?"s":""}</span>
                  <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s">{columns.length} coluna{columns.length!==1?"s":""}</span>
                  <span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s">Escala 0-{passValue}</span>
                  {colorGrades&&<span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s" style={{color:BLUE_POS_CSS,borderColor:"rgba(7,42,200,.2)"}}>Notas coloridas</span>}
                  {currentCertId&&<span className="cert-chip cert-chip-s" style={{color:"#22a06b",borderColor:"rgba(34,160,107,.25)",background:"rgba(34,160,107,.08)"}}>Guardado</span>}
                </>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="cert-action-btns">
              {selectedStudents.length>0&&(
                <>
                  <button className={`cert-btn-sec${editMode?" active":""}`} onClick={()=>{setEditMode(v=>!v);setFullEditMode(false);}}>
                    {editMode?<MdOutlineEditOff size={15}/>:<MdOutlineEdit size={15}/>}{editMode?"Fechar Edição":"Editar Notas"}
                  </button>
                  <button className={`cert-btn-sec${fullEditMode?" active":""}`} onClick={()=>{setFullEditMode(v=>!v);setEditMode(false);}}>
                    {fullEditMode?<MdOutlineEditOff size={15}/>:<MdOutlineEditNote size={15}/>}{fullEditMode?"Fechar Edição Completa":"Edição Completa"}
                  </button>
                  <button className="cert-btn-sec" onClick={handlePrint}><PiPrinterDuotone size={16}/> Imprimir</button>
                  <button className="cert-btn-sec" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving}><MdOutlineSave size={15}/> {currentCertId?"Actualizar":"Guardar"}</button>
                  <button className="cert-btn-sec" onClick={handleExportOnly} disabled={exporting||exportSaving} style={{borderColor:PURPLE_HEX,color:PURPLE_HEX}}>
                    {exporting?<><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{width:13,height:13,borderWidth:2}}/> A gerar...</>:<><MdOutlinePictureAsPdf size={16}/> Exportar PDF</>}
                  </button>
                  <button className="cert-btn-pri" onClick={handleExportAndSave} disabled={exporting||exportSaving||saving}>
                    {exportSaving?<><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{width:14,height:14,borderWidth:2}}/> A guardar...</>:<><MdOutlinePictureAsPdf size={16}/> Exportar e Guardar</>}
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* DOCUMENT PREVIEW / EDIT */}
        <CustomTableContainer
          title={`Certificados — ${selectedClass?.label||"—"} · ${selectedStudents.length} aluno${selectedStudents.length!==1?"s":""}`}
          content={
            <div>
              {!selectedStudents.length&&(
                <div className="cert-empty">
                  <div className="cert-empty-icon"><MdOutlineFileDownload size={24}/></div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:600,color:"#334155"}}>Nenhum aluno seleccionado</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:12.5,color:"#94a3b8"}}>{!selectedClass?"Seleccione uma turma para começar.":"Seleccione pelo menos um aluno para gerar certificados."}</div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* EDIT MODE */}
              {selectedStudents.length>0&&(editMode||fullEditMode)&&(
                <div className="cert-edit-area">
                  <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:52}}>
                    {selectedStudents.map((s,i)=>{
                      const ovr=overrides[s.value]||{};
                      const miss=getMissingFields({...s,...ovr});
                      const stuGrades=studentGrades[s.value]||subjects.map(subj=>({id:subj.id,name:subj.name,grades:{}}));
                      const sBocks=removedBlocks[s.value]||[];
                      return (
                        <div key={s.value}>
                          <div className="cert-student-label">
                            <div className="cert-student-num">{i+1}</div>
                            <span>{s.label}</span>
                            {miss.length>0&&(
                              <button className="cert-btn-warn" style={{fontSize:11,padding:"4px 12px",marginLeft:"auto"}} onClick={()=>{setModalStudentId(s.value);setModalOpen(true);}}>
                                <MdOutlineWarningAmber size={12}/> {miss.length} campos em falta
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="cert-edit-doc-wrap">
                            <div className="cert-doc-full" id={`cert-doc-${i}`}>
                              <CertComponent
                                data={s} overrides={ovr} onOverrideChange={(f,v)=>handleOverride(s.value,f,v)}
                                headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl||null} onLogoUpload={b64=>setLogoUrl(b64)}
                                editMode={editMode} fullEditMode={fullEditMode}
                                fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}
                                subjects={subjects} columns={columns} tableHeaders={tableHeaders} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                studentGrades={stuGrades} onStudentGradeChange={(subjId,colId,val)=>handleGradeChange(s.value,subjId,colId,val)}
                                removedBlocks={sBocks} onToggleBlock={id=>handleToggleBlock(s.value,id)}/>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* PREVIEW MODE */}
              {selectedStudents.length>0&&!editMode&&!fullEditMode&&(
                <div>
                  {hasMissing&&(
                    <div className="cert-warn-banner">
                      <div style={{width:22,height:22,borderRadius:6,background:"#f59e0b",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",flexShrink:0,marginTop:1}}><MdOutlineWarningAmber size={13} color="#fff"/></div>
                      <div style={{flex:1}}><div style={{fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,color:"#92400e",marginBottom:4}}>Campos em falta: {missing.join(" · ")}</div></div>
                      <button className="cert-btn-warn" style={{flexShrink:0,fontSize:12,padding:"6px 14px"}} onClick={()=>{setModalStudentId(current?.value);setModalOpen(true);}}>
                        <MdOutlineEdit size={13}/> Completar Dados
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="cert-prev-area">
                    <div className="cert-prev-flex">
                      {selectedStudents.map((s,i)=>{
                        const ovr=overrides[s.value]||{}; const miss=getMissingFields({...s,...ovr});
                        const isAct=i===activeIdx;
                        const stuGrades=studentGrades[s.value]||subjects.map(subj=>({id:subj.id,name:subj.name,grades:{}}));
                        const sBocks=removedBlocks[s.value]||[];
                        return (
                          <div key={s.value} className="cert-doc-thumb"
                            style={{cursor:"pointer",outline:isAct?`3px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}`:"none",outlineOffset:2}}
                            onClick={()=>setActiveIdx(i)}>
                            <div className="cert-doc-scaler">
                              <CertComponent
                                data={s} overrides={ovr} onOverrideChange={()=>{}}
                                headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl||null} onLogoUpload={()=>{}}
                                editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                                fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}
                                subjects={subjects} columns={columns} tableHeaders={tableHeaders} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                studentGrades={stuGrades} onStudentGradeChange={()=>{}}
                                removedBlocks={sBocks} onToggleBlock={()=>{}}/>
                            </div>
                            {miss.length>0&&<div className="cert-thumb-warn">{miss.length}</div>}
                            <div className="cert-thumb-label">{isAct?"▶ ":""}{i+1}. {s.label}</div>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>

                    {current&&(
                      <div style={{marginTop:20,background:"#fff",borderRadius:12,border:"1px solid #e2e8f0",overflow:"hidden"}}>
                        <div style={{background:"#f5f2eb",borderBottom:"1px solid #ddd",padding:"8px 14px",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",gap:8}}>
                          <span style={{fontFamily:F_SANS,fontSize:11,color:"#666"}}>{CERT_MODELS.find(m=>m.value===certModel)?.label} — {current.label}</span>
                          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}>
                            {hasMissing&&<button className="cert-btn-warn" style={{fontSize:11,padding:"4px 12px"}} onClick={()=>{setModalStudentId(current.value);setModalOpen(true);}}><MdOutlineWarningAmber size={12}/> Completar</button>}
                            <button className="cert-btn-sec" style={{fontSize:11,padding:"4px 12px"}} onClick={handlePrint}><PiPrinterDuotone size={14}/> Imprimir</button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div id={`cert-doc-${activeIdx}`} style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
                          <CertComponent
                            data={current} overrides={curOvr} onOverrideChange={(f,v)=>handleOverride(current.value,f,v)}
                            headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl||null} onLogoUpload={b64=>setLogoUrl(b64)}
                            editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                            fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}
                            subjects={subjects} columns={columns} tableHeaders={tableHeaders} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                            studentGrades={studentGrades[current.value]||[]}
                            onStudentGradeChange={(subjId,colId,val)=>handleGradeChange(current.value,subjId,colId,val)}
                            removedBlocks={removedBlocks[current.value]||[]} onToggleBlock={()=>{}}/>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          }
        />

        {/* STUDENT EDIT MODAL */}
        <StudentEditModal
          show={modalOpen} onHide={()=>setModalOpen(false)} studentId={modalStudentId}
          onSaved={()=>{
            if(!modalStudentId) return;
            axios.get(API.studentGet(modalStudentId)).then(r=>{
              if(!r.data?.length) return; const d=r.data[0];
              setSelectedStudents(prev=>prev.map(s=>String(s.value)===String(modalStudentId)?{
                ...s,label:d.ed_student_name,student_name:d.ed_student_name,
                student_birthday:d.ed_student_birthday,student_identityCard:d.ed_student_identityCard,
                student_naturalness:d.ed_student_naturalness,student_gender:d.ed_student_gender,
                father_name:d.ed_student_father_name,mother_name:d.ed_student_mother_name,
                picture:d.ed_student_picture||s.picture,
              }:s));
            }).catch(()=>{});
          }}
        />
      </div>
    }/>
  );
}

// PdQualificationsStatement — VERSÃO FINAL v8
// Alterações v8:
//  - Botão PDF no histórico exporta APENAS a declaração individual daquele aluno
//  - Loader no botão PDF do histórico durante exportação
//  - Botão de eliminar REMOVIDO do histórico
//  - Avatar Material UI para fotos dos alunos no histórico e na lista de selecção
//  - Checkbox para colorir notas (azul/vermelho por critério) ou apenas preto
//  - Modo edição no histórico carrega APENAS o aluno individual (não todos)
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import { Form, Spinner, Modal, Button } from "react-bootstrap";
import axios from "axios";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import moment from "moment";
import jsPDF from "jspdf";
import autoTable from "jspdf-autotable";
import Select from "react-select";
import Avatar from "@mui/material/Avatar";
import DashboardContainerWrapper from "../../components/elements/DashboardContainerWrapper";
import CustomTableContainer from "../../components/elements/CustomTableContainer";
import Hoot from "../../../General/components/Hoot";
import NaturalnessSelect from "../../../General/components/AutoSelectors/NaturalnessSelect";

import { ImagePreview } from "../../components/elements/ImagePreview";

  MdOutlineClass, MdOutlinePersonSearch, MdOutlineTune, MdOutlineDescription,
  MdOutlineWarningAmber, MdOutlineCheckCircle, MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked,
  MdOutlineEdit, MdOutlineFileDownload, MdOutlineEditNote, MdOutlinePictureAsPdf,
  MdOutlineEditOff, MdOutlineAdd, MdOutlineImage, MdOutlineFormatSize, MdOutlineHistory,
  MdOutlineSearch, MdOutlineSave, MdOutlineClose,
  MdOutlinePerson, MdOutlineCalendarToday, MdOutlineColorLens,
} from "react-icons/md";

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CONSTANTES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const PURPLE_HEX   = "var(--ed-purple)";
const PURPLE_RAW   = "var(--ed-purple)";
const BLUE_POS_CSS = "#072AC8";
const BLUE_POS_RGB = [7, 42, 200];
const RED_CSS      = "#cc0000";
const RED_RGB      = [204, 0, 0];
const F_SERIF = "'Times New Roman', Times, serif";
const F_SANS  = "'DM Sans', sans-serif";
const ANGOLA_COAT = "https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9d/Coat_of_arms_of_Angola.svg/800px-Coat_of_arms_of_Angola.svg.png";
const PERIODS = ["Manhã", "Tarde", "Nocturno"];

/* ── Endpoints ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const API = {
  classes:         ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallclassapi/get`,
  studentsByClass: (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallgetstudentsbyclass/${id}`,
  studentUpdate:   (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallstudentupdate/update/${id}`,
  studentGet:      (id) => `${Hoot()}eduallsinglestudentapi/get/${id}`,
  subjects:        ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallsubjectsapi/get/`,
  academicLevels:  ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallacademiclevelsapi/get/`,
  institute:       ()   => `${Hoot()}eduallgetcurentuserinstitute/get/`,
  declarations:    ()   => `${Hoot()}eduall-qualifications-statement/get`,
  declarationSave: ()   => `${Hoot()}eduall-qualifications-statement/post`,
  declarationUpd:  (id) => `${Hoot()}eduall-qualifications-statement/update/${id}`,
  declarationDel:  (id) => `${Hoot()}eduall-qualifications-statement/delete/${id}`,
};

const MESES = [
  "Janeiro","Fevereiro","Março","Abril","Maio","Junho",
  "Julho","Agosto","Setembro","Outubro","Novembro","Dezembro",
];

const formatDatePT = (iso) => {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return iso;
  return `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")} de ${MESES[d.getMonth()]} de ${d.getFullYear()}`;
};

const todayPT = () => {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getDate()} de ${MESES[d.getMonth()]} de ${d.getFullYear()}`;
};

/* ─── Números por extenso em Português ─────────────────────────── */
const UNITS = ["","Um","Dois","Três","Quatro","Cinco","Seis","Sete","Oito","Nove","Dez",
               "Onze","Doze","Treze","Catorze","Quinze","Dezasseis","Dezassete","Dezoito","Dezanove"];
const TENS  = ["","","Vinte","Trinta","Quarenta","Cinquenta","Sessenta","Setenta","Oitenta","Noventa"];

const numPT = (n) => {
  if (n === undefined || n === null || n === "") return "";
  const v = Math.ceil(Number(n));
  if (isNaN(v) || v < 0 || v > 100) return String(n);
  if (v <= 19) return UNITS[v];
  const t = Math.floor(v / 10), u = v % 10;
  return u === 0 ? TENS[t] : `${TENS[t]} e ${UNITS[u]}`;
};

const gradeWord = (n) => { const w = numPT(n); return w ? `${w} Valores` : ""; };

const calcMedia = (grades, cols) => {
  if (!cols || cols.length === 0) {
    const v = grades?.default;
    return v !== "" && v !== undefined && v !== null ? parseFloat(v) : null;
  }
  const vals = cols.map(c => grades?.[c.id]).filter(v => v !== "" && v !== undefined && v !== null).map(Number).filter(n => !isNaN(n));
  if (vals.length === 0) return null;
  return vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length;
};

/* colorGrade — respeita o flag colorGrades */
const gcColor = (v, pv = 10, colorGrades = true) => {
  if (!colorGrades) return "#000";
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? "#000" : n >= pv ? BLUE_POS_CSS : RED_CSS;
};
const gcRGB = (v, pv = 10, colorGrades = true) => {
  if (!colorGrades) return [0, 0, 0];
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return isNaN(n) ? [0,0,0] : n >= pv ? BLUE_POS_RGB : RED_RGB;
};

const detectPassValue = (label) => {
  if (!label) return 10;
  const n = label.toLowerCase().normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,"");
  const low6 = ["1","1a","1.","primeira","2","2a","2.","segunda","3","3a","3.","terceira",
                 "4","4a","4.","quarta","5","5a","5.","quinta","6","6a","6.","sexta"];
  for (const w of low6) {
    if (n.includes(w+" classe") || n.startsWith(w+" ") || n === w) return 10;
  }
  return 20;
};

const getMissingFields = (s) => {
  const m = [];
  if (!s?.student_name?.trim())         m.push("Nome");
  if (!s?.student_birthday)             m.push("Data de nascimento");
  if (!s?.student_identityCard?.trim()) m.push("B.I.");
  if (!s?.student_naturalness?.trim())  m.push("Naturalidade");
  if (!s?.student_gender?.trim())       m.push("Género");
  return m;
};

/* ── Avatar helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function StudentAvatar({ src, name, size = 30, className = "df" }) {
  const initials = (name || "?").split(" ").slice(0, 2).map(w => w[0]).join("").toUpperCase();
  if (src) {
    return (
      <Avatar
        src={src}
        alt={name}
        className={className}
        sx={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size * 0.4, border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0" }}
        onError={e => { e.target.src = ""; }}
      >
        {initials}
      </Avatar>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Avatar
      className={className}
      sx={{
        width: size, height: size,
        fontSize: size * 0.38,  
        fontWeight: 700,
        fontFamily: F_SANS,
      }}
    >
      {initials}
    </Avatar>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EDITABLE TEXT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function EditableText({ value, onChange, placeholder = "—", style = {}, multiline = false, tag = "span", isNd = false }) {
  const Tag = tag;
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [local, setLocal]     = useState(value || "");
  useEffect(() => { setLocal(value || ""); }, [value]);
  const commit = () => { setEditing(false); if (local !== value) onChange(local); };
  const cancel = () => { setEditing(false); setLocal(value || ""); };

  const isEmpty = !local || local === "N/D" || local === "";
  const displayStyle = {
    cursor: "text",
    borderBottom: isEmpty || isNd ? "1.5px dashed #ef4444" : "1px dashed #aaa",
    minWidth: 40, display: "inline",
    background: isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.06)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.04)",
    borderRadius: 2, padding: "0 2px", transition: "background .15s",
    color: isEmpty || isNd ? "#ef4444" : "inherit",
    ...style,
  };

  if (!editing) return (
    <Tag onClick={() => setEditing(true)} title="Clique para editar"
      style={displayStyle}
      onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.14)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.12)"; }}
      onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = isEmpty || isNd ? "rgba(239,68,68,.06)" : "rgba(108,63,197,.04)"; }}>
      {local || <span style={{ fontStyle: "italic", fontSize: "0.92em" }}>{placeholder}</span>}
    </Tag>
  );
  return multiline
    ? <textarea autoFocus value={local} rows={4}
        onChange={e => setLocal(e.target.value)} onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === "Escape") cancel(); }}
        style={{
          fontFamily: "inherit", fontSize: "inherit", color: "inherit", width: "100%",
          border: `1px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}`, borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 4px", outline: "none",
          background: "#fffff0", resize: "vertical", ...style,
        }}/>
    : <input autoFocus value={local}
        onChange={e => setLocal(e.target.value)} onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === "Enter") commit(); if (e.key === "Escape") cancel(); }}
        style={{
          fontFamily: "inherit", fontSize: "inherit", color: "inherit", minWidth: 60,
          border: `1px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}`, borderRadius: 4, padding: "0 4px", outline: "none",
          background: "#fffff0", display: "inline", ...style,
        }}/>;
}

/* ─── Wrapper de bloco removível em fullEditMode ───────────────── */
function RemovableBlock({ children, onRemove, fullEditMode, label = "" }) {
  if (!fullEditMode) return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <div style={{ position: "relative", marginBottom: 2 }}>
      <div style={{
        position: "absolute", top: -10, right: -8, zIndex: 10,
        display: "flex", gap: 3, alignItems: "center",
      }}>
        {label && (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 8, background: "#f1f5f9", color: "#64748b", borderRadius: 4, padding: "1px 5px", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600, border: "1px solid #e2e8f0" }}>
            {label}
          </span>
        )}
        <button onClick={onRemove} title="Remover este bloco"
          style={{ width: 18, height: 18, borderRadius: "50%", border: "none", background: "#ef4444", color: "#fff", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 0, lineHeight: 1 }}>
          ×
        </button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ border: "1px dashed rgba(239,68,68,.35)", borderRadius: 4, padding: "2px 4px", background: "rgba(239,68,68,.02)" }}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SELECT STYLES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const SEL_STYLES = {
  control: (b, s) => ({
    ...b, borderRadius: 10, borderColor: s.isFocused ? PURPLE_HEX : "#e2e8f0",
    boxShadow: s.isFocused ? `0 0 0 3px rgba(108,63,197,.13)` : "none",
    fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 13, minHeight: 40, cursor: "pointer",
  }),
  option: (b, s) => ({
    ...b, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 13,
    background: s.isSelected ? PURPLE_HEX : s.isFocused ? `rgba(108,63,197,.07)` : "#fff",
    color: s.isSelected ? "#fff" : "#0f172a", cursor: "pointer",
  }),
  menuPortal: (b) => ({ ...b, zIndex: 99999 }),
  menu:       (b) => ({ ...b, borderRadius: 10, boxShadow: "0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.12)" }),
  placeholder:(b) => ({ ...b, fontFamily: F_SANS, color: "#94a3b8" }),
  multiValue: (b) => ({ ...b, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.1)`, borderRadius: 6 }),
  multiValueLabel:  (b) => ({ ...b, color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 11 }),
  multiValueRemove: (b) => ({ ...b, color: PURPLE_HEX, ":hover": { background: `rgba(108,63,197,.2)`, color: PURPLE_HEX } }),
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STUDENT MULTI-SELECT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function StudentMultiSelect({ options, value, onChange }) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const filtered = options.filter(o => o.label.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
  const selectedSet = useMemo(() => new Set(value.map(v => v.value)), [value]);

  const toggle = useCallback((opt) => {
    if (selectedSet.has(opt.value)) onChange(value.filter(v => v.value !== opt.value));
    else onChange([...value, opt]);
  }, [selectedSet, value, onChange]);

  const toggleAll = useCallback(() => {
    if (filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value))) {
      const fs = new Set(filtered.map(f => f.value));
      onChange(value.filter(v => !fs.has(v.value)));
    } else {
      const m = [...value];
      filtered.forEach(o => { if (!selectedSet.has(o.value)) m.push(o); });
      onChange(m);
    }
  }, [filtered, selectedSet, value, onChange]);

  const allSel = filtered.length > 0 && filtered.every(o => selectedSet.has(o.value));

  const getPicSrc = (opt) => {
    if (!opt.picture) return null;
    return opt.picture.startsWith("http") ? opt.picture : Hoot() + opt.picture;
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <div style={{ position: "relative", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <input type="text" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Pesquisar aluno..."
          style={{
            width: "100%", padding: "8px 32px 8px 12px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0",
            borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: F_SANS, outline: "none",
            color: "#0f172a", background: "#fff", boxSizing: "border-box",
          }}
          onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
          onBlur={e  => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}/>
        {search && (
          <button onClick={() => setSearch("")}
            style={{ position: "absolute", right: 10, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 16, padding: 0 }}>
            ×
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "4px 8px", marginBottom: 4, background: "#f8fafc", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 11.5, color: "#64748b" }}>
        <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 500 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={allSel} onChange={toggleAll} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: PURPLE_HEX }}/>
          {allSel ? "Desseleccionar todos" : "Seleccionar todos"}
        </label>
        <span style={{ color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600 }}>{value.length}/{options.length}</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{ maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, background: "#fff" }}>
        {filtered.length === 0
          ? <div style={{ padding: "12px 14px", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 13 }}>Nenhum aluno encontrado.</div>
          : filtered.map(opt => {
              const checked = selectedSet.has(opt.value);
              const picSrc  = getPicSrc(opt);
              return (
                <label key={opt.value}
                  style={{
                    display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: "7px 12px",
                    cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid #f1f5f9",
                    background: checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff", fontSize: 13, color: "#0f172a",
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.07)" : "#f8fafc"}
                  onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = checked ? "rgba(108,63,197,.04)" : "#fff"}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={() => toggle(opt)} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, accentColor: PURPLE_HEX, flexShrink: 0 }}/>
                  <StudentAvatar src={picSrc} name={opt.label} size={24}/>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: checked ? 600 : 400 }}>{opt.label}</span>
                </label>
              );
            })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   HEADER EDITOR
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function HeaderEditor({ headerLines, onHeaderLinesChange }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        Edite as linhas do cabeçalho. <strong>N</strong> para negrito, <strong>×</strong> para remover.
      </div>
      {(headerLines || []).map((hl, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, marginBottom: 5 }}>
          <input defaultValue={hl.text}
            onBlur={e => {
              const next = (headerLines || []).map((x, xi) => xi === i ? { ...x, text: e.target.value } : x);
              onHeaderLinesChange(next);
            }}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "5px 8px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 7, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12, outline: "none", fontWeight: hl.bold ? 700 : 400 }}
            onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}/>
          <button onClick={() => onHeaderLinesChange((headerLines || []).map((x, xi) => xi === i ? { ...x, bold: !x.bold } : x))}
            style={{ width: 28, height: 28, border: `1.5px solid ${hl.bold ? PURPLE_HEX : "#e2e8f0"}`, borderRadius: 6, background: hl.bold ? PURPLE_HEX : "#fff", color: hl.bold ? "#fff" : "#64748b", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0 }}>
            N
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onHeaderLinesChange((headerLines || []).filter((_, xi) => xi !== i))}
            style={{ width: 28, height: 28, border: "1.5px solid #fee2e2", borderRadius: 6, background: "#fff", color: "#ef4444", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0, lineHeight: 1 }}>
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <button onClick={() => onHeaderLinesChange([...(headerLines || []), { text: "Nova linha", bold: false }])}
        style={{ marginTop: 4, padding: "5px 12px", border: `1.5px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 7, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
        + Adicionar linha
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   GRADE CELL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function GradeCell({ value, onChange, passValue, editMode, colorGrades = true }) {
  const [local, setLocal] = useState(value ?? "");
  useEffect(() => { setLocal(value ?? ""); }, [value]);
  const n      = parseFloat(local);
  const hasVal = local !== "" && local !== null && local !== undefined;
  const color  = hasVal && !isNaN(n) ? gcColor(n, passValue, colorGrades) : "#000";
  if (!editMode) return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
      <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color }}>{hasVal ? String(local) : ""}</span>
      {hasVal && !isNaN(n) && colorGrades && <div style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: color, margin: "2px auto 0" }}/>}
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: 4 }}>
        <input type="number" min={0} max={passValue} step={0.5} value={local}
          onChange={e => { setLocal(e.target.value); onChange(e.target.value); }}
          style={{ width: 52, border: `1.5px solid ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 6, padding: "2px 4px", textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, color, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: "8pt", outline: "none" }}/>
        {hasVal && !isNaN(n) && colorGrades && <div style={{ width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: "50%", background: color, flexShrink: 0 }}/>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DOC HEADER
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function DocHeader({ headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload, fontSize }) {
  const sc = fontSize / 12;
  return (
    <div style={{ textAlign: "center", fontFamily: F_SERIF, lineHeight: 1.85, marginBottom: 6 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
        <label title="Clique para alterar logótipo" style={{ cursor: "pointer", display: "block" }}>
          <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: "none" }}
            onChange={e => {
              const f = e.target.files?.[0];
              if (f) { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = ev => onLogoUpload?.(ev.target.result); r.readAsDataURL(f); }
            }}/>
          <img src={logoUrl || ANGOLA_COAT} alt="Logótipo" crossOrigin="anonymous"
            style={{ height: 56 * sc, objectFit: "contain", border: "2px dashed transparent", borderRadius: 4, transition: "border-color .15s" }}
            onMouseEnter={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_RAW}
            onMouseLeave={e => e.target.style.borderColor = "transparent"}
            onError={e => e.target.src = ANGOLA_COAT}/>
        </label>
      </div>
      {(headerLines || []).map((hl, i) => (
        <div key={i} style={{ fontSize: `${(hl.bold ? 14 : 12) * sc}pt`, fontWeight: hl.bold ? 700 : 400, textAlign: "center", lineHeight: 1.7 }}>
          {hl.text}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BORDER UTIL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const getBorderStyle = (m) => {
  if (!m || m === "none")  return {};
  if (m === "single")      return { border: "1.5px solid #000",   padding: "32px 38px" };
  if (m === "double")      return { border: "3px double #000",    padding: "34px 40px" };
  if (m === "certificate") return { border: "6px solid #000", outline: "2px solid #000", outlineOffset: "-10px", padding: "42px 48px" };
  return {};
};

const drawPdfBorder = (pdf, borderMode, PW, PH, M) => {
  if (!borderMode || borderMode === "none") return;
  pdf.setDrawColor(0, 0, 0);
  if (borderMode === "single") {
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.5);
    pdf.rect(M - 2, M - 2, PW - (M - 2) * 2, PH - (M - 2) * 2);
  } else if (borderMode === "double") {
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.8);
    pdf.rect(M - 4, M - 4, PW - (M - 4) * 2, PH - (M - 4) * 2);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.3);
    pdf.rect(M - 2, M - 2, PW - (M - 2) * 2, PH - (M - 2) * 2);
  } else if (borderMode === "certificate") {
    pdf.setLineWidth(2);
    pdf.rect(M - 6, M - 6, PW - (M - 6) * 2, PH - (M - 6) * 2);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.5);
    pdf.rect(M - 3, M - 3, PW - (M - 3) * 2, PH - (M - 3) * 2);
  }
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   GRADES EDITOR PANEL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function GradesEditorPanel({ subjects, columns, onSubjectsChange, onColumnsChange, allSubjects, allAcLevels }) {
  const subjOpts = allSubjects.map(s => ({ value: s.id, label: s.title }));
  const lvlOpts  = allAcLevels.map(l => ({ value: l.id, label: l.title }));
  const addSubj  = (opt) => { if (!opt || subjects.find(s => s.id === opt.value)) return; onSubjectsChange([...subjects, { id: opt.value, name: opt.label }]); };
  const remSubj  = (id)  => onSubjectsChange(subjects.filter(s => s.id !== id));
  const addCol   = ()    => { if (columns.length >= 4) return toast.error("Máximo de 4 colunas."); onColumnsChange([...columns, { id: Date.now(), label: "" }]); };
  const remCol   = (i)   => onColumnsChange(columns.filter((_, xi) => xi !== i));
  const updCol   = (i, val) => onColumnsChange(columns.map((c, xi) => xi === i ? val : c));

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: .5, marginBottom: 8 }}>
          Colunas de Classe (máx. 4)
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
          {columns.map((col, i) => (
            <div key={col.id} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "6px 10px", background: "#f8fafc", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Select options={lvlOpts}
                  value={col.label ? lvlOpts.find(o => o.value === col.id) || { value: col.id, label: col.label } : null}
                  onChange={opt => updCol(i, { ...col, id: opt?.value || col.id, label: opt?.label || "" })}
                  placeholder="Seleccionar classe..." isClearable styles={SEL_STYLES}
                  menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed"/>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => remCol(i)}
                style={{ width: 24, height: 24, border: "1.5px solid #fee2e2", borderRadius: 6, background: "#fff", color: "#ef4444", fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {columns.length < 4 && (
            <button onClick={addCol}
              style={{ padding: "7px 14px", border: `1.5px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 10, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
              <MdOutlineAdd size={14}/> Coluna
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: .5, marginBottom: 8 }}>Disciplinas</div>
        <Select options={subjOpts.filter(o => !subjects.find(s => s.id === o.value))}
          onChange={opt => { addSubj(opt); }} value={null}
          placeholder="Adicionar disciplina..." isClearable styles={SEL_STYLES}
          menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed"/>
        {subjects.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 8 }}>
            {subjects.map(s => (
              <div key={s.id} style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "4px 10px", background: `rgba(108,63,197,.07)`, border: `1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.2)`, borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 12, color: PURPLE_RAW }}>
                {s.name}
                <button onClick={() => remSubj(s.id)}
                  style={{ width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: "50%", border: "none", background: "rgba(108,63,197,.2)", color: PURPLE_RAW, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 0 }}>
                  ×
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DECLARAÇÃO COM NOTAS — v8
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function DeclarationWithGrades({
  data, overrides, onOverrideChange, headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload,
  editMode, fullEditMode, fontSize, passValue, borderMode,
  studentSubjects, onStudentSubjectChange, columns, classInfo, institute,
  removedBlocks, onToggleBlock, colorGrades = true,
}) {
  const d  = { ...(data || {}), ...(overrides || {}) };
  const ch = (field, value) => onOverrideChange?.(field, value);
  const fs = fontSize;
  const pv = passValue;

  const E = (field, val, ph = "—", style = {}, bold = false) => {
    const effectiveVal = val || "";
    const isNd = !effectiveVal || effectiveVal === "N/D";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={effectiveVal} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder={ph} style={style} isNd={isNd}/>;
    if (isNd) return <span style={{ color: "#ef4444", fontStyle: "italic", ...style }}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong style={style}>{effectiveVal}</strong> : <span style={style}>{effectiveVal}</span>;
  };

  const D = (field, staticVal, ph = "—", bold = true) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : staticVal;
    const isNd = !val || val === "N/D" || val === "";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val || ""} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder={ph} isNd={isNd}/>;
    if (isNd) return <span style={{ color: "#ef4444", fontStyle: "italic" }}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong>{val}</strong> : <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const T = (field, defaultText) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : defaultText;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder="Editar texto..."/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const TL = (field, defaultText) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : defaultText;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder="Editar texto..." multiline/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const RB = (id, label, content) => {
    if (removedBlocks?.includes(id)) return null;
    if (!fullEditMode) return content;
    return (
      <RemovableBlock key={id} onRemove={() => onToggleBlock?.(id)} fullEditMode={fullEditMode} label={label}>
        {content}
      </RemovableBlock>
    );
  };

  const gradeRows   = studentSubjects || [];
  const cols        = columns || [];
  const borderStyle = getBorderStyle(borderMode);

  return (
    <div style={{ width: "100%", background: "#fff", padding: "28px 40px 32px", fontFamily: F_SERIF, color: "#000", fontSize: `${fs}pt`, lineHeight: 1.85, ...borderStyle }}>
      <DocHeader headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl} onLogoUpload={onLogoUpload} fontSize={fs}/>

      <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "14px 0 4px" }}>
        {fullEditMode
          ? <EditableText value={d.doc_title || "DECLARAÇÃO DE HABILITAÇÕES"} onChange={v => ch("doc_title", v)}
              style={{ fontSize: `${fs + 3}pt`, fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline", letterSpacing: ".08em" }}/>
          : <span style={{ fontSize: `${fs + 3}pt`, fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline", letterSpacing: ".08em" }}>
              {d.doc_title || "DECLARAÇÃO DE HABILITAÇÕES"}
            </span>
        }
      </div>
      <hr style={{ border: "none", borderTop: "1px solid #000", margin: "6px 0 16px" }}/>

      {RB("p_director", "§ Diretor",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {T("director_title", "O(A) Abaixo Assinado(a)")},{" "}
          {E("director_name", d.director_name || institute?.ed_institute_principal, "Nome do Director", {}, true)},{" "}
          {T("director_role_label", "Director(a) d")}{T("school_article", "a")}&nbsp;
          {E("school_full_name", d.school_full_name || institute?.ed_institute_name, "Nome da Escola", {}, true)}.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_student", "§ Aluno",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {T("decl_intro", "Declara que,")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_name", data?.student_name, "Nome do aluno")},{" "}
          {T("filiation_label", "filho(a) de")}&nbsp;
          {D("father_name", data?.father_name, "Nome do pai")}&nbsp;
          {T("and_label", "e de")}&nbsp;
          {D("mother_name", data?.mother_name, "Nome da mãe")},{" "}
          {T("born_label", "nascido(a) aos")}&nbsp;
          {(() => {
            const bday = d.student_birthday || data?.student_birthday;
            if (fullEditMode) {
              return <EditableText
                value={d.student_birthday_text || (bday ? formatDatePT(bday) : "")}
                onChange={v => ch("student_birthday_text", v)}
                placeholder="Data de nascimento"
                isNd={!bday && !d.student_birthday_text}/>;
            }
            const display = d.student_birthday_text || (bday ? formatDatePT(bday) : null);
            return display ? <strong>{display}</strong> : <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>;
          })()},{" "}
          {T("natural_label", "natural de")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_naturalness", data?.student_naturalness, "Naturalidade")},{" "}
          {T("bi_label", "portador(a) do B.I nº")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_identityCard", data?.student_identityCard, "Nº B.I.")},{" "}
          {T("bi_issued_label", "passado pelo Arquivo de Identificação de")}&nbsp;
          {E("bi_issued_by", d.bi_issued_by || institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda", "Local")}{" "}
          {T("bi_issued_pre_date", "aos")}{" "}
          {E("bi_issued_date", d.bi_issued_date, "Data do B.I.")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_freq", "§ Frequência",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {T("freq_intro", "Frequentou n")}{T("school_article2", "a")}&nbsp;
          {E("school_short_name", d.school_short_name || institute?.ed_institute_name, "Nome da Escola")}&nbsp;
          {T("ano_label", "no ano lectivo de")}&nbsp;
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText
                value={d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year || ""}
                onChange={v => ch("academic_year_override", v)}
                placeholder="Ano lectivo"
                isNd={!(d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year)}/>
            : <strong>{d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year || <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          }
          {cols.length > 0 && (
            <> {T("col_a_label", "a")} ({fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.col_labels_override || cols.map(c => c.label).join(" / ")} onChange={v => ch("col_labels_override", v)} placeholder="Classes"/>
              : <strong>{d.col_labels_override || cols.map(c => c.label).join(" / ")}</strong>
            })</>
          )},{" "}
          {T("turma_label", "Turma:")}&nbsp;
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText
                value={d.class_title_override || data?.class_title || ""}
                onChange={v => ch("class_title_override", v)}
                placeholder="Turma"
                isNd={!(d.class_title_override || data?.class_title)}/>
            : <strong>{d.class_title_override || data?.class_title || <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          },{" "}
          {classInfo?.period && (
            <>{T("periodo_label", "Período")}&nbsp;
            {fullEditMode
              ? <EditableText value={d.period_override || classInfo.period} onChange={v => ch("period_override", v)} placeholder="Período"/>
              : <strong>{d.period_override || classInfo.period}</strong>
            },{" "}</>
          )}
          {T("grades_intro", "tendo obtido o resultado final com as seguintes Classificações:")}
        </p>
      )}

      {/* Tabela de notas */}
      {!removedBlocks?.includes("t_grades") ? (
        <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          {fullEditMode && (
            <button onClick={() => onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")} title="Remover tabela"
              style={{ position: "absolute", top: -10, right: -8, zIndex: 10, width: 18, height: 18, borderRadius: "50%", border: "none", background: "#ef4444", color: "#fff", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 0 }}>
              ×
            </button>
          )}
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", margin: "10px 0 14px", fontSize: `${fs - 1}pt` }}>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={{ border: "1px solid #000", padding: "5px 8px", textAlign: "left", fontWeight: 700, background: "#f8f8f8" }}>
                  {fullEditMode
                    ? <EditableText value={d.th_disciplinas || "Disciplinas"} onChange={v => ch("th_disciplinas", v)}/>
                    : (d.th_disciplinas || "Disciplinas")
                  }
                </th>
                {cols.length > 0
                  ? cols.map((col, ci) => (
                      <th key={ci} style={{ border: "1px solid #000", padding: "5px 8px", textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, background: "#f8f8f8" }}>
                        {col.label || `Coluna ${ci + 1}`}
                      </th>
                    ))
                  : <th style={{ border: "1px solid #000", padding: "5px 8px", textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, background: "#f8f8f8" }}>
                      {fullEditMode
                        ? <EditableText value={d.th_classificacao || "Classificação"} onChange={v => ch("th_classificacao", v)}/>
                        : (d.th_classificacao || "Classificação")
                      }
                    </th>
                }
                <th style={{ border: "1px solid #000", padding: "5px 8px", textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, background: "#f8f8f8" }}>
                  {fullEditMode
                    ? <EditableText value={d.th_media || "Média por Extenso"} onChange={v => ch("th_media", v)}/>
                    : (d.th_media || "Média por Extenso")
                  }
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {gradeRows.length > 0
                ? gradeRows.map((g, i) => {
                    const media    = calcMedia(g.grades, cols);
                    const mediaRnd = media !== null ? Math.ceil(media) : null;
                    const color    = mediaRnd !== null ? gcColor(mediaRnd, pv, colorGrades) : "#000";
                    const vals     = cols.length > 0
                      ? cols.map(c => g.grades?.[c.id] ?? "")
                      : [g.grades?.default ?? ""];
                    return (
                      <tr key={i} style={{ background: i % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa" }}>
                        <td style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "3px 8px" }}>{g.name}</td>
                        {vals.map((v, vi) => (
                          <td key={vi} style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "3px 4px", minWidth: 70 }}>
                            <GradeCell value={v}
                              onChange={val => {
                                const colId = cols.length > 0 ? cols[vi]?.id : "default";
                                onStudentSubjectChange?.(g.id, colId, val);
                              }}
                              passValue={pv} editMode={editMode || fullEditMode} colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                          </td>
                        ))}
                        <td style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "3px 8px", textAlign: "center", fontSize: `${fs - 2}pt`, color, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                          {mediaRnd !== null ? gradeWord(mediaRnd) : ""}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                : [0, 1, 2, 3].map(i => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "4px 8px", color: "#ccc" }}>—</td>
                      {cols.length > 0
                        ? cols.map((_, ci) => <td key={ci} style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "4px 8px" }}></td>)
                        : <td style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "4px 8px" }}></td>
                      }
                      <td style={{ border: "1px solid #999", padding: "4px 8px" }}></td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
              }
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      ) : fullEditMode ? (
        <button onClick={() => onToggleBlock?.("t_grades")}
          style={{ padding: "4px 12px", fontSize: 11, border: `1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 6, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, cursor: "pointer", margin: "8px 0", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
          + Restaurar tabela de notas
        </button>
      ) : null}

      {RB("p_purpose", "§ Finalidade",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "left" }}>
          {T("purpose_label", "A presente declaração destina-se para:")}&nbsp;
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.purpose || "Efeitos Legais."} onChange={v => ch("purpose", v)} style={{ color: "#FF0000", fontWeight: 700 }}/>
            : <strong style={{ color: "#FF0000" }}>{d.purpose || "Efeitos Legais."}</strong>
          }
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_closing", "§ Encerramento",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {TL("closing_text", "Por ser verdade e me ter sido solicitada e, assim constar nos documentos que ficam arquivados na Secretaria, mandei passar a presente declaração que, por mim, vai assinada e autenticada com o carimbo a óleo em uso nesta Escola.")}
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_date", "§ Data/Local",
        <p style={{ textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, margin: "16px 0" }}>
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.school_location_full || institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda"} onChange={v => ch("school_location_full", v)} placeholder="Cidade/Escola"/>
            : <span>{d.school_location_full || institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda"}</span>
          }{T("date_connector", ", aos ")}{todayPT()}
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_sigs", "§ Assinaturas",
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginTop: 20, fontSize: `${fs - 1}pt` }}>
          <div>
            {T("sig_left_label", "Buscas")}&nbsp;
            <span style={{ display: "inline-block", borderBottom: "1px solid #000", width: 120 }}></span><br/>
            <span style={{ display: "inline-block", borderTop: "1px solid #000", width: 150, marginTop: 28 }}></span>
          </div>
          <div style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
            {T("sig_right_label", "O(A) Director(a) da Escola")}<br/>
            <span style={{ display: "inline-block", borderTop: "1px solid #000", width: 160, marginTop: 28 }}></span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {fullEditMode && (removedBlocks || []).length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12, padding: "8px 12px", background: "#f8fafc", border: "1px dashed #cbd5e1", borderRadius: 8, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 11 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b", marginBottom: 6 }}>Blocos removidos — clique para restaurar:</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            {removedBlocks.map(id => (
              <button key={id} onClick={() => onToggleBlock?.(id)}
