                style={{ padding: "3px 10px", border: `1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 6, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
                + {id.replace("p_","§ ").replace("t_","Tabela ")}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DECLARAÇÃO SEM NOTAS — v8
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function DeclarationWithoutGrades({
  data, overrides, onOverrideChange, headerLines, logoUrl, onLogoUpload,
  editMode, fullEditMode, fontSize, borderMode, classInfo, institute,
  removedBlocks, onToggleBlock,
}) {
  const d  = { ...(data || {}), ...(overrides || {}) };
  const ch = (field, value) => onOverrideChange?.(field, value);
  const fs = fontSize;
  const city = institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda";
  const borderStyle = getBorderStyle(borderMode);

  const E = (field, val, ph = "—", bold = false) => {
    const effectiveVal = val || "";
    const isNd = !effectiveVal || effectiveVal === "N/D";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={effectiveVal} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder={ph} isNd={isNd}/>;
    if (isNd) return <span style={{ color: "#ef4444", fontStyle: "italic" }}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong>{effectiveVal}</strong> : <span>{effectiveVal}</span>;
  };

  const D = (field, staticVal, ph = "—", bold = true) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : staticVal;
    const isNd = !val || val === "N/D" || val === "";
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val || ""} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder={ph} isNd={isNd}/>;
    if (isNd) return <span style={{ color: "#ef4444", fontStyle: "italic" }}>N/D</span>;
    return bold ? <strong>{val}</strong> : <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const T = (field, defaultText) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : defaultText;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder="Editar texto..."/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const TL = (field, defaultText) => {
    const val = d[field] !== undefined ? d[field] : defaultText;
    if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={val} onChange={v => ch(field, v)} placeholder="Editar texto..." multiline/>;
    return <span>{val}</span>;
  };

  const RB = (id, label, content) => {
    if (removedBlocks?.includes(id)) return null;
    if (!fullEditMode) return content;
    return (
      <RemovableBlock key={id} onRemove={() => onToggleBlock?.(id)} fullEditMode={fullEditMode} label={label}>
        {content}
      </RemovableBlock>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ width: "100%", background: "#fff", padding: "28px 40px 32px", fontFamily: F_SERIF, color: "#000", fontSize: `${fs}pt`, lineHeight: 1.85, ...borderStyle }}>
      <DocHeader headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl} onLogoUpload={onLogoUpload} fontSize={fs}/>

      <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "14px 0 2px" }}>
        {fullEditMode
          ? <EditableText value={d.doc_title || `DECLARAÇÃO Nº ${d.decl_number || "XXXX/XXXX"}`} onChange={v => ch("doc_title", v)}
              style={{ fontSize: `${fs + 4}pt`, fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline", letterSpacing: ".08em" }}/>
          : <span style={{ fontSize: `${fs + 4}pt`, fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline", letterSpacing: ".08em" }}>
              {d.doc_title
                ? d.doc_title
                : <>{T("doc_title_prefix", "DECLARAÇÃO Nº ")}{E("decl_number", d.decl_number, "XXXX/XXXX")}</>
              }
            </span>
        }
      </div>
      <hr style={{ border: "none", borderTop: "1px solid #000", margin: "6px 0 16px" }}/>

      {RB("p_main", "§ Principal",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {T("director_title", "O(A) Abaixo Assinado(a)")},{" "}
          {E("director_name", d.director_name || institute?.ed_institute_principal, "Nome do Director", true)},{" "}
          {T("dir_role", "Director(a) Geral d")}{T("school_article", "a")}&nbsp;
          {E("school_full_name", d.school_full_name || institute?.ed_institute_name, "Nome da Escola", true)},{" "}
          {T("decl_verb", "declara que o(a) aluno(a)")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_name", data?.student_name, "Nome do aluno")},{" "}
          {T("matr_label", "matriculado(a) sob o nº")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_code", data?.student_code, "Nº Matrícula")},{" "}
          {T("filho_label", "filho(a) de")}&nbsp;
          {D("father_name", data?.father_name, "Nome do pai")}&nbsp;
          {T("ede_label", "e de")}&nbsp;
          {D("mother_name", data?.mother_name, "Nome da mãe")},{" "}
          {T("nat_label", "natural de")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_naturalness", data?.student_naturalness, "Naturalidade")},{" "}
          {T("nasc_label", "nascido(a) aos")}&nbsp;
          {(() => {
            const bday = d.student_birthday || data?.student_birthday;
            if (fullEditMode) return <EditableText value={d.student_birthday_text || (bday ? formatDatePT(bday) : "")} onChange={v => ch("student_birthday_text", v)} placeholder="Data de nascimento" isNd={!bday && !d.student_birthday_text}/>;
            const display = d.student_birthday_text || (bday ? formatDatePT(bday) : null);
            return display ? <strong>{display}</strong> : <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>;
          })()},{" "}
          {T("bi_port_label", "portador(a) do B.I Nº")}&nbsp;
          {D("student_identityCard", data?.student_identityCard, "Nº B.I.")},{" "}
          {T("bi_pass_label", "passado pelo Sector de Identificação de")}&nbsp;
          {E("bi_issued_by", d.bi_issued_by || city, "Local")}{" "}
          {T("bi_date_pre", "aos")}{" "}
          {E("bi_issued_date", d.bi_issued_date, "Data do B.I.")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_freq", "§ Frequência",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {T("freq_label", "Frequentou a")}&nbsp;
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.class_title_override || data?.class_title || ""} onChange={v => ch("class_title_override", v)} placeholder="Turma" isNd={!(d.class_title_override || data?.class_title)}/>
            : <strong>{d.class_title_override || data?.class_title || <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          }&nbsp;
          {T("freq_inst_label", "nesta instituição de ensino, no ano lectivo de")}&nbsp;
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year || ""} onChange={v => ch("academic_year_override", v)} placeholder="Ano lectivo" isNd={!(d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year)}/>
            : <strong>{d.academic_year_override || classInfo?.academic_year || data?.academic_year || <span style={{ color: "#ef4444" }}>N/D</span>}</strong>
          }.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_nota", "§ Nota",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px" }}>
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.nota_label || "Nota:"} onChange={v => ch("nota_label", v)} style={{ color: "#C00000", fontWeight: 700 }}/>
            : <strong style={{ color: "#C00000" }}>{d.nota_label || "Nota:"}</strong>
          }{" "}
          {T("note_prefix", "A presente declaração destina-se para")}&nbsp;
          {E("note_purpose", d.note_purpose || "efeitos legais junto da Conservatória do Registo Civil", "finalidade")}.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_closing", "§ Encerramento",
        <p style={{ margin: "0 0 10px", textAlign: "justify" }}>
          {TL("closing_text", "Por ser verdade e me ter sido solicitado mandei passar a presente declaração que vai por mim assinada e autenticada com o carimbo a óleo em uso nesta instituição de ensino.")}
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_date", "§ Data/Local",
        <p style={{ fontWeight: 700, margin: "14px 0" }}>
          {fullEditMode
            ? <EditableText value={d.school_location_full || `${institute?.ed_institute_name || "ESCOLA"} em ${city}`} onChange={v => ch("school_location_full", v)} placeholder="Local/Escola"/>
            : <strong>{d.school_location_full || `${institute?.ed_institute_name || "ESCOLA"} em ${city}`}</strong>
          }{T("date_upper_connector", ", AOS ")}{todayPT().toUpperCase()}.
        </p>
      )}

      {RB("p_sig", "§ Assinatura",
        <div style={{ textAlign: "center", marginTop: 24 }}>
          <p style={{ marginBottom: 28, fontWeight: 700 }}>
            {T("sig_label", "O(A) Director(a) Geral")}
          </p>
          <span style={{ display: "inline-block", borderTop: "1px solid #000", width: 180 }}></span>
        </div>
      )}

      {fullEditMode && (removedBlocks || []).length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12, padding: "8px 12px", background: "#f8fafc", border: "1px dashed #cbd5e1", borderRadius: 8, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 11 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b", marginBottom: 6 }}>Blocos removidos — clique para restaurar:</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            {removedBlocks.map(id => (
              <button key={id} onClick={() => onToggleBlock?.(id)}
                style={{ padding: "3px 10px", border: `1px dashed ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 6, background: `rgba(108,63,197,.05)`, color: PURPLE_HEX, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
                + {id.replace("p_","§ ").replace("t_","Tabela ")}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STUDENT EDIT MODAL
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function StudentEditModal({ show, onHide, studentId, onSaved }) {
  const [showBox, setShowBox]   = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm]         = useState({});
  const [errors, setErrors]     = useState({});
  const [saving, setSaving]     = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(false);
  const [picture, setPicture]   = useState(null);
  const [cover, setCover]       = useState(null);
  const [guardion, setGuardion] = useState(null);
  const [toggleHealth, setToggleHealth] = useState(0);
  const [currentId, setCurrentId] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => { if (show) setShowBox(true); else setTimeout(() => setShowBox(false), 1200); }, [show]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!show || !studentId) return;
    setLoading(true); setErrors({}); setPicture(null);
    axios.get(API.studentGet(studentId))
      .then(r => {
        if (!r.data?.length) { toast.error("Aluno não encontrado."); onHide(); return; }
        const d = r.data[0];
        setCurrentId(d.ed_student_id);
        setCover(d.ed_student_picture ? Hoot() + d.ed_student_picture : null);
        setToggleHealth(d.ed_student_health_problems * 1);
        if      (d.ed_student_father_is_guardion  == 1) setGuardion(1);
        else if (d.ed_student_mother_is_guardion  == 1) setGuardion(0);
        else if (d.ed_student_guardion_is_guardion == 1) setGuardion(2);
        else setGuardion(null);
        setForm({
          student_name: d.ed_student_name || "", student_code: d.ed_student_code || "",
          student_identityCard: d.ed_student_identityCard || "",
          student_birthday: d.ed_student_birthday ? moment(d.ed_student_birthday).format("YYYY-MM-DD") : "",
          student_gender: d.ed_student_gender || "", student_naturalness: d.ed_student_naturalness || "",
          student_nacionality: d.ed_student_nacionality || "", student_address: d.ed_student_address || "",
          student_phone: d.ed_student_phone || "", student_email: d.ed_student_email || "",
          student_religion: d.ed_student_religion || "",
          student_scholarshipHolder: String(d.ed_student_scholarshipHolder ?? "0"),
          student_status: String(d.ed_student_status ?? "1"), student_class: d.ed_student_class || "",
          student_health_problems: String(d.ed_student_health_problems ?? "0"),
          student_health_problems_description: d.ed_student_health_problems_description || "",
          student_lastschool: d.ed_student_lastschool || "", student_lastclass: d.ed_student_lastclass || "",
          father_name: d.ed_student_father_name || "", father_phone: d.ed_student_father_phone || "",
          father_email: d.ed_student_father_email || "", father_job: d.ed_student_father_job || "",
          father_emergency_phone: d.ed_student_father_emergency_phone || "", father_address: d.ed_student_father_address || "",
          mother_name: d.ed_student_mother_name || "", mother_phone: d.ed_student_mother_phone || "",
          mother_email: d.ed_student_mother_email || "", mother_job: d.ed_student_mother_job || "",
          mother_emergency_phone: d.ed_student_mother_emergency_phone || "", mother_address: d.ed_student_mother_address || "",
          guardion_name: d.ed_student_guardion_name || "", guardion_phone: d.ed_student_guardion_phone || "",
          guardion_email: d.ed_student_guardion_email || "", guardion_job: d.ed_student_guardion_job || "",
          guardion_emergency_phone: d.ed_student_guardion_emergency_phone || "", guardion_address: d.ed_student_guardion_address || "",
          guardion_gender: String(d.ed_student_guardion_gender ?? "0"),
          guardion_degree_of_kinship: String(d.ed_student_guardion_degree ?? "0"),
        });
      })
      .catch(() => toast.error("Erro ao carregar dados."))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [show, studentId]);

  const sf = (k, v) => { setForm(p => ({ ...p, [k]: v })); if (errors[k]) setErrors(p => ({ ...p, [k]: null })); };
  const hi = (e) => {
    sf(e.target.id || e.target.name, e.target.value);
    if ((e.target.id || e.target.name) === "student_health_problems") setToggleHealth(parseInt(e.target.value) || 0);
  };
  const validate = () => {
    const e = {};
    if (!form.student_name?.trim())         e.student_name         = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_gender?.trim())        e.student_gender       = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_birthday?.trim())      e.student_birthday     = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_identityCard?.trim())  e.student_identityCard = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_naturalness?.trim())   e.student_naturalness  = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_address?.trim())       e.student_address      = "Campo obrigatório";
    if (!form.student_phone?.toString().trim()) e.student_phone     = "Campo obrigatório";
    return e;
  };
  const handleSave = async (e) => {
    e?.preventDefault();
    const errs = validate();
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) { setErrors(errs); toast.error("Verifique os campos obrigatórios!"); return; }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const fd = new FormData();
      ['student_name','student_code','student_identityCard','student_birthday','student_gender',
       'student_naturalness','student_nacionality','student_address','student_phone','student_email',
       'student_religion','student_scholarshipHolder','student_status','student_class',
       'student_health_problems','student_health_problems_description','student_lastschool','student_lastclass',
       'father_name','father_phone','father_email','father_job','father_emergency_phone','father_address',
       'mother_name','mother_phone','mother_email','mother_job','mother_emergency_phone','mother_address',
       'guardion_name','guardion_phone','guardion_email','guardion_job','guardion_emergency_phone',
       'guardion_address','guardion_gender','guardion_degree_of_kinship']
        .forEach(k => fd.append(k, form[k] ?? ""));
      fd.append('father_is_guardion',  guardion === 1 ? "1" : "0");
      fd.append('mother_is_guardion',  guardion === 0 ? "1" : "0");
      fd.append('guardion_is_guardion',guardion === 2 ? "1" : "0");
      if (picture) fd.append('student_picture', picture);
      await axios.put(API.studentUpdate(currentId || studentId), fd);
      toast.success("Dados actualizados com sucesso!");
      onSaved?.(); onHide();
    } catch (err) { toast.error(err?.response?.data?.msg || "Erro ao guardar."); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const SECT = (label) => (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 1, marginBottom: 8, marginTop: 12, paddingBottom: 4, borderBottom: "1px solid #e2e8f0" }}>{label}</div>
  );
  const PFIELDS = (prefix, title) => (
    <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
      {SECT(title)}
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: 8 }}>
        {[["Nome completo","name","text"],["Telefone","phone","number"],["E-mail","email","email"],
          ["Profissão","job","text"],["Tel. emergência","emergency_phone","number"],["Morada","address","text"]].map(([lbl, sf2, type]) => (
          <Form.Group key={sf2}>
            <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>{lbl}</Form.Label>
            <Form.Control size="sm" type={type} id={`${prefix}_${sf2}`} name={`${prefix}_${sf2}`}
              value={form[`${prefix}_${sf2}`] || ""} onChange={hi}/>
          </Form.Group>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <Modal className={showBox ? ModalAnimationShow() : ModalAnimationClose()} centered size="xl"
      dialogAs={DraggableModal} show={show} onHide={onHide} scrollable>
      <Modal.Header closeButton>
        <Modal.Title>
          <div className="ed-wrap align-center">
            <UserOptionDropDown/>
            <h5>Editar dados do Aluno</h5>
          </div>
        </Modal.Title>
      </Modal.Header>
      <Form onSubmit={handleSave}>
        <Modal.Body className="scrollLimit">
          {loading ? (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: 32, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <Spinner animation="border" size="sm"/> A carregar dados...
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {SECT("Dados Pessoais")}
              <div className="ed-flex mb-3 gap-3">
                <ImagePreview Cover={cover} Picture={f => setPicture(f)}/>
                <div className="block col">
                  <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10 }}>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Nº Matrícula</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_code" value={form.student_code || ""} onChange={hi} placeholder="Auto"/>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Nome completo *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_name" value={form.student_name || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_name}/>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_name}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Nº B.I. *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_identityCard" value={form.student_identityCard || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_identityCard}/>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_identityCard}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Data de Nascimento *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" type="date" id="student_birthday" value={form.student_birthday || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_birthday}/>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_birthday}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Género *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_gender" value={form.student_gender || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_gender}>
                        <option value="">Seleccionar</option>
                        <option value="male">Masculino</option><option value="female">Feminino</option>
                      </Form.Select>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_gender}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Telefone *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" type="number" id="student_phone" value={form.student_phone || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_phone}/>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_phone}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>E-mail</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" type="email" id="student_email" value={form.student_email || ""} onChange={hi}/>
                    </Form.Group>
                    <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                      <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Morada *</Form.Label>
                      <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_address" value={form.student_address || ""} onChange={hi} isInvalid={!!errors.student_address}/>
                      <Form.Control.Feedback type="invalid" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{errors.student_address}</Form.Control.Feedback>
                    </Form.Group>
                  </div>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2 mt-2">
                    <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Naturalidade *</Form.Label>
                    <NaturalnessSelect value={form.student_naturalness || ""} onChange={v => sf("student_naturalness", v)}
                      isInvalid={!!errors.student_naturalness} errorMsg={errors.student_naturalness}/>
                  </Form.Group>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                    <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Nacionalidade</Form.Label>
                    <NationalitySelect value={form.student_nacionality || ""} onChange={v => sf("student_nacionality", v)}/>
                  </Form.Group>
                  <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                    <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Religião</Form.Label>
                    <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_religion" value={form.student_religion || ""} onChange={hi}>
                      <option value="">Seleccionar</option><ReligionOptions/>
                    </Form.Select>
                  </Form.Group>
                </div>
              </div>
              {SECT("Saúde")}
              <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Problemas de saúde</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_health_problems" value={form.student_health_problems ?? "0"} onChange={hi}>
                    <option value="0">Não</option><option value="1">Sim</option>
                  </Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
              </div>
              {toggleHealth === 1 && (
                <Form.Group className="mb-2">
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Descrição</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Control as="textarea" rows={3} id="student_health_problems_description" value={form.student_health_problems_description || ""} onChange={hi}/>
                </Form.Group>
              )}
              {SECT("Dados Escolares")}
              <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Turma</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_class" value={form.student_class || ""} onChange={hi}><ClassDataOptions/></Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Estado</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_status" value={form.student_status ?? "1"} onChange={hi}>
                    <option value="1">Activo</option><option value="0">Inactivo</option>
                  </Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Bolseiro</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="student_scholarshipHolder" value={form.student_scholarshipHolder ?? "0"} onChange={hi}>
                    <option value="0">Não</option><option value="1">Sim</option>
                  </Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Última escola</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_lastschool" value={form.student_lastschool || ""} onChange={hi}/>
                </Form.Group>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Última classe</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Control size="sm" id="student_lastclass" value={form.student_lastclass || ""} onChange={hi}/>
                </Form.Group>
              </div>
              {PFIELDS("father",  "Pai")}
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <FormControlLabel onChange={() => setGuardion(guardion === 1 ? null : 1)}
                  control={<Checkbox checked={guardion === 1} size="small" sx={{ color: PURPLE_RAW, "&.Mui-checked": { color: PURPLE_RAW } }}/>}
                  label={<span style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Definir pai como encarregado principal</span>}/>
              </div>
              {PFIELDS("mother", "Mãe")}
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <FormControlLabel onChange={() => setGuardion(guardion === 0 ? null : 0)}
                  control={<Checkbox checked={guardion === 0} size="small" sx={{ color: PURPLE_RAW, "&.Mui-checked": { color: PURPLE_RAW } }}/>}
                  label={<span style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Definir mãe como encarregada principal</span>}/>
              </div>
              {PFIELDS("guardion", "Responsável")}
              <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Grau de parentesco</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="guardion_degree_of_kinship" value={form.guardion_degree_of_kinship ?? "0"} onChange={hi}>
                    <option value="0">Mãe</option><option value="1">Pai</option>
                    <option value="2">Irmão</option><option value="3">Tio</option><option value="4">Tia</option>
                  </Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
                <Form.Group>
                  <Form.Label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: "#64748b" }}>Género</Form.Label>
                  <Form.Select size="sm" id="guardion_gender" value={form.guardion_gender ?? "0"} onChange={hi}>
                    <option value="0">Masculino</option><option value="1">Feminino</option>
                  </Form.Select>
                </Form.Group>
              </div>
              <FormControlLabel onChange={() => setGuardion(guardion === 2 ? null : 2)}
                control={<Checkbox checked={guardion === 2} size="small" sx={{ color: PURPLE_RAW, "&.Mui-checked": { color: PURPLE_RAW } }}/>}
                label={<span style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Definir responsável como encarregado principal</span>}/>
            </>
          )}
        </Modal.Body>
        <Modal.Footer>
          <div className="ed-space">
            <div className="ed-flex"/>
            <div className="ed-flex">
              <Button className="bg-light text-dark" onClick={onHide}>Cancelar</Button>
              <SaveButton icon={<Save/>} title="Guardar" status={saving ? 0 : null}/>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Modal.Footer>
      </Form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   IMAGE CACHE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const _imgB64Cache = {};
async function loadImgAsBase64(src) {
  if (!src) return null;
  if (_imgB64Cache[src] !== undefined) return _imgB64Cache[src];
  try {
    const b64 = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const img = new Image(); img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
      img.onload  = () => {
        try {
          const c = document.createElement("canvas");
          c.width = img.naturalWidth || img.width; c.height = img.naturalHeight || img.height;
          c.getContext("2d").drawImage(img, 0, 0);
          resolve(c.toDataURL("image/png"));
        } catch (e) { reject(e); }
      };
      img.onerror = reject; img.src = src;
    });
    _imgB64Cache[src] = b64; return b64;
  } catch (_) { _imgB64Cache[src] = null; return null; }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   HISTORY PANEL — v8
   - Botão PDF exporta apenas o aluno individual
   - Loader no botão PDF
   - Sem botão de eliminar
   - Avatar MUI para fotos
   - Botão Editar carrega apenas o aluno individual
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function HistoryPanel({ onLoadSingle, onExportSinglePdf, history, loading }) {
  const [search, setSearch]       = useState("");
  const [loadingId, setLoadingId] = useState(null);

  /* Expandir registos em declarações individuais por aluno */
  const flatRows = useMemo(() => {
    const rows = [];
    history.forEach(h => {
      let students = [];
      let grades   = {};
      let overrides = {};
      try { students = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_students || "[]"); } catch {}
      try { grades   = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_student_grades || "{}"); } catch {}
      try { overrides = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_overrides || "{}"); } catch {}

      if (students.length === 0) {
        rows.push({
          _id: `${h.ed_qs_id}_single`, _parentId: h.ed_qs_id, _studentIdx: 0,
          _entry: h, _studentData: null, _grades: {}, _overrides: {},
        });
      } else {
        students.forEach((s, idx) => {
          const studentId = s.student_id || s.value;
          rows.push({
            _id:          `${h.ed_qs_id}_${idx}`,
            _parentId:    h.ed_qs_id,
            _studentIdx:  idx,
            _entry:       h,
            _studentData: s,
            _grades:      grades[studentId] || [],
            _overrides:   overrides[studentId] || {},
          });
        });
      }
    });
    return rows;
  }, [history]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    if (!search.trim()) return flatRows;
    const q = search.toLowerCase();
    return flatRows.filter(r => {
      const name  = (r._studentData?.student_name || r._studentData?.label || r._entry.ed_qs_student_names || "").toLowerCase();
      const turma = (r._entry.ed_qs_class_title || "").toLowerCase();
      const code  = (r._studentData?.student_code || r._entry.ed_qs_student_codes || "").toLowerCase();
      return name.includes(q) || turma.includes(q) || code.includes(q);
    });
  }, [flatRows, search]);

  const handleExport = async (row) => {
    setLoadingId(row._id);
    try { await onExportSinglePdf(row); }
    finally { setLoadingId(null); }
  };

  const picSrc = (row) => {
    const pic = row._studentData?.picture || null;
    if (!pic) return null;
    return pic.startsWith("http") ? pic : Hoot() + pic;
  };

  const studentName = (row) =>
    row._studentData?.student_name || row._studentData?.label ||
    (row._entry.ed_qs_student_names || "").split(",")[0]?.trim() || "—";

  return (
    <div className="decl-panel">
      <div className="decl-phdr">
        <div className="decl-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineHistory size={14}/></div>
        <span className="decl-pttl">Histórico de Declarações</span>
        <span style={{ marginLeft: "auto", fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
          {flatRows.length} declaraç{flatRows.length === 1 ? "ão" : "ões"} · {history.length} registo{history.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Pesquisa */}
      <div className="decl-pbody-sm" style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #f1f5f9" }}>
        <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <MdOutlineSearch size={15} style={{ position: "absolute", left: 10, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", color: "#94a3b8" }}/>
          <input type="text" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Pesquisar por nome, turma ou matrícula..."
            style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 12px 8px 32px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 12.5, fontFamily: F_SANS, outline: "none", boxSizing: "border-box", color: "#0f172a" }}
            onFocus={e => e.target.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX}
            onBlur={e  => e.target.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"}/>
          {search && (
            <button onClick={() => setSearch("")}
              style={{ position: "absolute", right: 10, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 16, padding: 0 }}>
              ×
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="decl-pbody" style={{ padding: 0 }}>
        {loading ? (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: "24px 20px", color: "#64748b", fontSize: 13, fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
            <Spinner animation="border" size="sm"/> A carregar histórico...
          </div>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: "32px 20px", textAlign: "center", color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 13, fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
            <div style={{ width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 12, background: "#f8fafc", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", margin: "0 auto 10px" }}>
              <MdOutlineHistory size={20} color="#cbd5e1"/>
            </div>
            {search ? "Nenhuma declaração encontrada para essa pesquisa." : "Nenhuma declaração guardada ainda."}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div>
            {/* Cabeçalho fixo — fora do scroll */}
            <div style={{
              display: "grid",
              gridTemplateColumns: "36px 1fr 120px 90px 100px 140px",
              gap: 0, padding: "8px 16px",
              background: "#f8fafc", borderBottom: "1px solid #e2e8f0",
              fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: 700, color: "#64748b",
              textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5, fontFamily: F_SANS,
              position: "sticky", top: 0, zIndex: 2,
            }}>
              <div>#</div>
              <div>Aluno</div>
              <div>Turma</div>
              <div>Tipo</div>
              <div>Data</div>
              <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>Acções</div>
            </div>

            {/* Corpo com scroll — max-height 400px */}
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 400, overflowY: "auto", overflowX: "hidden" }}>
              {filtered.map((row, idx) => {
                const h         = row._entry;
                const name      = studentName(row);
                const code      = row._studentData?.student_code || "—";
                const pic       = picSrc(row);
                const missing   = row._studentData ? getMissingFields({ ...row._studentData, ...row._overrides }) : [];
                const isLoading = loadingId === row._id;

                return (
                  <div key={row._id}
                    style={{
                      display: "grid",
                      gridTemplateColumns: "36px 1fr 120px 90px 100px 140px",
                      gap: 0, padding: "9px 16px",
                      borderBottom: "1px solid #f1f5f9",
                      background: idx % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa",
                      alignItems: "center", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12.5, transition: "background .1s",
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = "rgba(108,63,197,.03)"}
                    onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = idx % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#fafafa"}>

                    {/* Nº */}
                    <div style={{ color: "#94a3b8", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600 }}>{idx + 1}</div>

                    {/* Aluno — Avatar MUI com className */}
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 9, minWidth: 0 }}>
                      <StudentAvatar src={pic} name={name} size={30} className="df"/>
                      <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#0f172a", fontSize: 12.5, overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                          {name}
                          {missing.length > 0 && (
                            <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 2, padding: "1px 6px", background: "#fffbeb", border: "1px solid #fde68a", borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 9.5, color: "#92400e", fontWeight: 600, verticalAlign: "middle" }}>
                              <MdOutlineWarningAmber size={9}/> {missing.length} em falta
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        {code !== "—" && <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: "#94a3b8", marginTop: 1 }}>Matrícula: {code}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Turma */}
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 500, color: "#334155", overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                      {h.ed_qs_class_title || "—"}
                    </div>

                    {/* Tipo */}
                    <div>
                      <span style={{ padding: "3px 8px", borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, background: h.ed_qs_with_notes ? "rgba(108,63,197,.08)" : "rgba(34,160,107,.08)", color: h.ed_qs_with_notes ? PURPLE_RAW : "#16a34a", border: `1px solid ${h.ed_qs_with_notes ? "rgba(108,63,197,.2)" : "rgba(22,163,74,.2)"}` }}>
                        {h.ed_qs_with_notes ? "Com Notas" : "Sem Notas"}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Data */}
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#64748b", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4 }}>
                      <MdOutlineCalendarToday size={11}/>
                      {h.ed_qs_updated_at ? new Date(h.ed_qs_updated_at).toLocaleDateString("pt-PT") : "—"}
                    </div>

                    {/* Acções — Editar + PDF */}
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 5, justifyContent: "center" }}>
                      <button onClick={() => onLoadSingle(row)} title="Editar este aluno"
                        style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: "1.5px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 7, background: "#fff", color: "#475569", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600, transition: "all .12s" }}
                        onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = PURPLE_HEX; e.currentTarget.style.color = PURPLE_RAW; }}
                        onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#e2e8f0"; e.currentTarget.style.color = "#475569"; }}>
                        <Edit style={{ fontSize: 12 }}/> Editar
                      </button>

                      <button onClick={() => handleExport(row)} disabled={isLoading}
                        style={{ padding: "4px 10px", border: `1.5px solid ${PURPLE_HEX}`, borderRadius: 7, background: isLoading ? `rgba(108,63,197,.7)` : PURPLE_HEX, color: "#fff", cursor: isLoading ? "not-allowed" : "pointer", fontSize: 11, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4, fontFamily: F_SANS, fontWeight: 600, minWidth: 64, justifyContent: "center", transition: "filter .12s" }}
                        onMouseEnter={e => { if (!isLoading) e.currentTarget.style.filter = "brightness(1.12)"; }}
                        onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.filter = "none"; }}>
                        {isLoading
                          ? <><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 11, height: 11, borderWidth: 2 }}/> A gerar</>
                          : <><PiFileDuotone size={12}/> PDF</>
                        }
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {!loading && filtered.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ padding: "10px 16px", background: "#f8fafc", borderTop: "1px solid #f1f5f9", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#94a3b8" }}>
            A mostrar {filtered.length} de {flatRows.length} declaraç{flatRows.length === 1 ? "ão" : "ões"}
          </span>
          <span className="decl-chip cp" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>
            {history.length} registo{history.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""} em arquivo
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PDF EXPORT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
async function exportDeclarationPDF({
  students, withNotes, overrides, studentGrades, headerLines,
  logoUrl, institute, columns, passValue = 10, fontSize = 12,
  borderMode = "none", colorGrades = true,
}) {
  const PW = 210, PH = 297, M = 14, IW = PW - M * 2;
  const BK = [0, 0, 0];
  const pdf = new jsPDF({ orientation: "portrait", unit: "mm", format: "a4", compress: true });
  const coatB64 = await loadImgAsBase64(logoUrl || ANGOLA_COAT);

  const drawHdr = (y) => {
    if (coatB64) { try { pdf.addImage(coatB64, "PNG", PW / 2 - 8, y, 16, 20); } catch {} }
    y += 22;
    (headerLines || []).forEach((hl, li) => {
      pdf.setFont("helvetica", hl.bold ? "bold" : "normal");
      pdf.setFontSize(hl.bold ? 8 : 7);
      pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
      pdf.text(hl.text, PW / 2, y + li * 3.5, { align: "center", maxWidth: IW - 20 });
    });
    return y + (headerLines || []).length * 3.5 + 3;
  };

  const rp = (segs, startY, lh = 4.8) => {
    let cx = M, cy = startY;
    for (const seg of segs) {
      pdf.setFont("helvetica", seg.bold ? "bold" : "normal");
      pdf.setFontSize(9);
      pdf.setTextColor(...(seg.color || BK));
      const words = String(seg.text || "").split(" ");
      for (let wi = 0; wi < words.length; wi++) {
        const word = wi < words.length - 1 ? words[wi] + " " : words[wi];
        const ww   = pdf.getTextWidth(word);
        if (cx > M && cx + ww > M + IW) { cy += lh; cx = M; }
        pdf.text(word, cx, cy); cx += ww;
      }
    }
    return cy + lh + 2;
  };

  for (let ri = 0; ri < students.length; ri++) {
    if (ri > 0) pdf.addPage([PW, PH], "portrait");
    const s   = students[ri];
    const ovr = overrides[s.student_id] || {};
    const d   = { ...s, ...ovr };
    const city = institute?.ed_institute_state || "Luanda";

    const studentName    = d.student_name    || s.label || "N/D";
    const fatherName     = d.father_name     || "N/D";
    const motherName     = d.mother_name     || "N/D";
    const naturalness    = d.student_naturalness || "N/D";
    const bi             = d.student_identityCard || "N/D";
    const bday           = d.student_birthday_text || (d.student_birthday ? formatDatePT(d.student_birthday) : "N/D");
    const studentCode    = d.student_code    || "—";
    const classTitle     = d.class_title_override || d.class_title || s.class_title || "N/D";
    const academicYear   = d.academic_year_override || d.academic_year || s.academic_year || "N/D";
    const dTitle         = d.director_title  || "O(A) Abaixo Assinado(a)";
    const dName          = d.director_name   || institute?.ed_institute_principal || "";
    const schoolFN       = d.school_full_name || institute?.ed_institute_name || "";
    const art            = d.school_article  || "a";
    const biDate         = d.bi_issued_date  || "";
    const biLoc          = d.bi_issued_by    || city;

    drawPdfBorder(pdf, borderMode, PW, PH, M);
    let y = M + (borderMode && borderMode !== "none" ? 10 : 0);
    y = drawHdr(y);

    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(12); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    const title = withNotes
      ? (d.doc_title || "DECLARAÇÃO DE HABILITAÇÕES")
      : (d.doc_title || `DECLARAÇÃO Nº ${d.decl_number || "____/____"}`);
    pdf.text(title, PW / 2, y, { align: "center" });
    const tw = pdf.getTextWidth(title);
    pdf.setLineWidth(0.4); pdf.line(PW / 2 - tw / 2, y + 0.7, PW / 2 + tw / 2, y + 0.7);
    y += 4; pdf.setLineWidth(0.3); pdf.line(M, y, PW - M, y); y += 6;

    if (withNotes) {
      y = rp([
        { text: dTitle ? `${dTitle} ` : "", bold: true },
        { text: dName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.director_role_label || "Director(a) d"}${art} ` },
        { text: schoolFN, bold: true }, { text: "." },
      ], y);
      y = rp([
        { text: `${d.decl_intro || "Declara que,"} ` },
        { text: studentName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.filiation_label || "filho(a) de"} ` }, { text: fatherName, bold: true },
        { text: ` ${d.and_label || "e de"} ` },              { text: motherName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.born_label || "nascido(a) aos"} ` },  { text: bday, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.natural_label || "natural de"} ` },   { text: naturalness, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.bi_label || "portador(a) do B.I nº"} ` }, { text: bi, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.bi_issued_label || "passado pelo Arquivo de Identificação de"} ${biLoc} ${d.bi_issued_pre_date || "aos"} ${biDate}.` },
      ], y);
      const colLabels = d.col_labels_override || (columns || []).map(c => c.label).join(" / ");
      y = rp([
        { text: `${d.freq_intro || "Frequentou n"}${d.school_article2 || "a"} ` },
        { text: d.school_short_name || institute?.ed_institute_name || "", bold: true },
        { text: ` ${d.ano_label || "no ano lectivo de"} ` },
        { text: academicYear, bold: true },
        ...(colLabels ? [{ text: ` ${d.col_a_label || "a"} (${colLabels}),` }] : []),
        { text: ` ${d.turma_label || "Turma:"} ` }, { text: classTitle, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.grades_intro || "tendo obtido o resultado final com as seguintes Classificações:"}` },
      ], y);

      const stuGrades = studentGrades?.[s.student_id] || [];
      if (stuGrades.length > 0 && columns?.length > 0) {
        const headRow = [
          { content: d.th_disciplinas || "Disciplinas", styles: { halign: "left", fontStyle: "bold", fontSize: 7 } },
          ...columns.map(c => ({ content: c.label || "", styles: { halign: "center", fontStyle: "bold", fontSize: 7 } })),
          { content: d.th_media || "Média por Extenso", styles: { halign: "center", fontStyle: "bold", fontSize: 7 } },
        ];
        const tableBody = stuGrades.map(g => {
          const row = [{ content: g.name, styles: { halign: "left", fontSize: 7.5 } }];
          columns.forEach(col => {
            const v = g.grades?.[col.id] ?? "";
            const n = parseFloat(v);
            const clr = v !== "" && !isNaN(n) ? gcRGB(n, passValue, colorGrades) : BK;
            row.push({ content: v !== "" ? String(v) : "", styles: { halign: "center", fontStyle: "bold", fontSize: 7.5, textColor: clr } });
          });
          const media    = calcMedia(g.grades, columns);
          const mediaRnd = media !== null ? Math.ceil(media) : null;
          const mClr     = mediaRnd !== null ? gcRGB(mediaRnd, passValue, colorGrades) : BK;
          row.push({ content: mediaRnd !== null ? gradeWord(mediaRnd) : "", styles: { halign: "center", fontSize: 7, textColor: mClr } });
          return row;
        });
        autoTable(pdf, {
          startY: y, margin: { left: M, right: M }, tableWidth: IW,
          head: [headRow], body: tableBody, theme: "grid",
          styles: { fontSize: 7.5, cellPadding: 1.5, lineColor: BK, lineWidth: 0.25, textColor: BK, font: "helvetica" },
          headStyles: { fillColor: [248, 248, 248], textColor: BK, fontStyle: "bold" },
        });
        y = pdf.lastAutoTable.finalY + 5;
      }
      y = rp([
        { text: `${d.purpose_label || "A presente declaração destina-se para:"} ` },
        { text: d.purpose || "Efeitos Legais.", bold: true, color: RED_RGB },
      ], y);
    } else {
      y = rp([
        { text: dTitle ? `${dTitle} ` : "", bold: true }, { text: dName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.dir_role || "Director(a) Geral d"}${art} ` }, { text: schoolFN, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.decl_verb || "declara que o(a) aluno(a)"} ` }, { text: studentName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.matr_label || "matriculado(a) sob o nº"} ` }, { text: studentCode, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.filho_label || "filho(a) de"} ` }, { text: fatherName, bold: true },
        { text: ` ${d.ede_label || "e de"} ` }, { text: motherName, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.nat_label || "natural de"} ` }, { text: naturalness, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.nasc_label || "nascido(a) aos"} ` }, { text: bday, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.bi_port_label || "portador(a) do B.I Nº"} ` }, { text: bi, bold: true },
        { text: `, ${d.bi_pass_label || "passado pelo Sector de Identificação de"} ${biLoc} ${d.bi_date_pre || "aos"} ${biDate}.` },
      ], y);
      y = rp([
        { text: `${d.freq_label || "Frequentou a"} ` }, { text: classTitle, bold: true },
        { text: ` ${d.freq_inst_label || "nesta instituição de ensino, no ano lectivo de"} ` },
        { text: academicYear, bold: true }, { text: "." },
      ], y);
      y = rp([
        { text: `${d.nota_label || "Nota:"} `, bold: true, color: RED_RGB },
        { text: `${d.note_prefix || "A presente declaração destina-se para"} ${d.note_purpose || "efeitos legais junto da Conservatória do Registo Civil"}.` },
      ], y);
    }

    const closing = d.closing_text || (withNotes
      ? "Por ser verdade e me ter sido solicitada e, assim constar nos documentos que ficam arquivados na Secretaria, mandei passar a presente declaração que, por mim, vai assinada e autenticada com o carimbo a óleo em uso nesta Escola."
      : "Por ser verdade e me ter sido solicitado mandei passar a presente declaração que vai por mim assinada e autenticada com o carimbo a óleo em uso nesta instituição de ensino."
    );
    y = rp([{ text: closing }], y); y += 2;

    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); pdf.setFontSize(9); pdf.setTextColor(...BK);
    const locText = d.school_location_full || city;
    const dateConnector = d.date_connector || ", aos ";
    pdf.text(`${locText}${dateConnector}${todayPT()}`, PW / 2, y, { align: "center" });
    y += 12;

    pdf.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); pdf.setFontSize(8);
    const sigLeft  = d.sig_left_label  || (withNotes ? "Buscas" : "");
    const sigRight = d.sig_right_label || (withNotes ? "O(A) Director(a) da Escola" : d.sig_label || "O(A) Director(a) Geral");
    if (sigLeft) { pdf.text(sigLeft, M, y); pdf.line(M + 15, y, M + 60, y); }
    pdf.text(sigRight, PW - M, y, { align: "right" });
    y += 9;
    if (sigLeft) pdf.line(M, y, M + 60, y);
    pdf.line(PW - M - 60, y, PW - M, y);
  }

  pdf.save(`eduall-declaracoes-${withNotes ? "com-notas" : "sem-notas"}-${moment().format("YYYY-MM-DD")}.pdf`);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UI CSS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const UI_CSS = `
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');
.decl-root*,.decl-root*::before,.decl-root*::after{box-sizing:border-box;}
.decl-root{font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;}
.decl-topbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-bottom:22px;}
.decl-topbar-icon{width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:12px;background:${PURPLE_HEX};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(108,63,197,.3);}
.decl-topbar-title{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;}
.decl-topbar-sub{font-size:12.5px;color:#64748b;margin-top:1px;}
.decl-panel{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;margin-bottom:16px;overflow:hidden;}
.decl-phdr{padding:13px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid #f1f5f9;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;}
.decl-phdr-icon{width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;background:#f8fafc;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#475569;flex-shrink:0;}
.decl-pttl{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#334155;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;}
.decl-pbody{padding:16px 20px;}
.decl-pbody-sm{padding:12px 20px;}
.decl-row{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.decl-col{flex:1;min-width:140px;}
.decl-lbl{font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;}
.decl-divider{height:1px;background:#f1f5f9;margin:0;}
.decl-pills{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.decl-pill{padding:7px 16px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;border:1.5px solid #e2e8f0;background:#f8fafc;color:#64748b;transition:all .15s;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;}
.decl-pill:hover{border-color:#cbd5e1;color:#1e293b;background:#f1f5f9;}
.decl-pill.pp{background:${PURPLE_HEX};border-color:${PURPLE_HEX};color:#fff;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(108,63,197,.30);}
.decl-action-bar{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;padding:14px 20px;border-top:1px solid #f1f5f9;background:#fff;}
.decl-stats{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;}
.decl-chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;padding:5px 16px;border-radius:999px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;}
.decl-chip.cp{background:rgba(108,63,197,.08);color:${PURPLE_HEX};border:1px solid rgba(108,63,197,.22);}
.decl-chip.cs{background:#f8fafc;color:#475569;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;}
.decl-chip.cy{background:rgba(245,158,11,.08);color:#b45309;border:1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.25);}
.decl-action-btns{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.decl-btn-secondary{padding:9px 18px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px solid #e2e8f0;background:#f8fafc;color:#475569;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;transition:all .15s;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;}
.decl-btn-secondary:hover:not(:disabled){border-color:${PURPLE_HEX};color:${PURPLE_HEX};background:#f5f0ff;}
.decl-btn-secondary:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;}
.decl-btn-secondary.active{background:#f59e0b;border-color:#f59e0b;color:#fff;}
.decl-btn-primary{padding:9px 22px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;border-radius:10px;border:none;background:${PURPLE_HEX};color:#fff;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(108,63,197,.35);transition:filter .15s,transform .1s;}
.decl-btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled){filter:brightness(1.1);transform:translateY(-1px);}
.decl-btn-primary:disabled{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;transform:none;}
.decl-btn-primary.green{background:#16a34a;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(22,163,74,.35);}
.decl-btn-primary.green:hover:not(:disabled){filter:brightness(1.08);}
.decl-btn-warn{padding:7px 14px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px solid #f59e0b;background:#fffbeb;color:#b45309;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;transition:all .15s;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;}
.decl-btn-warn:hover{background:#fef3c7;border-color:#d97706;}
.decl-prev-area{background:var(--ed-background-color,#e8e8e8);border-radius:14px;padding:24px;border:1px solid #d4d4d4;}
.decl-prev-flex{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:24px;align-items:flex-start;}
.decl-doc-thumb{width:298px;height:421px;position:relative;flex-shrink:0;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;border-radius:2px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.22);}
.decl-doc-scaler{transform-origin:top left;transform:scale(0.47);position:absolute;top:0;left:0;pointer-events:none;}
.decl-thumb-label{position:absolute;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.55);color:#fff;font-size:9px;font-weight:600;padding:3px 6px;text-overflow:ellipsis;overflow:hidden;white-space:nowrap;font-family:'DM Sans',sans-serif;}
.decl-thumb-warn{position:absolute;top:5px;right:5px;background:#f59e0b;color:#fff;border-radius:50%;width:18px;height:18px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;}
.decl-edit-area{background:var(--ed-background-color,#e8e8e8);border-radius:14px;padding:24px;border:1px solid #d4d4d4;}
.decl-edit-doc-wrap{display:flex;justify-content:center;}
.decl-doc-full{display:block;width:210mm;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18);border-radius:4px;flex-shrink:0;}
.decl-edit-banner{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 20px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(245,158,11,.12),rgba(245,158,11,.05));border-bottom:1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.25);font-size:12px;color:#92400e;font-weight:500;}
.decl-edit-banner-icon{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;background:#f59e0b;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;}
.decl-warn-banner{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;padding:10px 16px;background:#fffbeb;border:1px solid #fde68a;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:12px;}
.decl-empty{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:52px 20px;gap:10px;text-align:center;}
.decl-empty-icon{width:56px;height:56px;border-radius:16px;background:rgba(108,63,197,.07);border:1.5px solid rgba(108,63,197,.18);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:${PURPLE_HEX};margin-bottom:4px;}
.decl-student-label{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#334155;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
.decl-student-num{width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;background:${PURPLE_HEX};color:#fff;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;}
.decl-hist-avatar{flex-shrink:0 !important;transition:transform .15s !important;}
.decl-hist-avatar:hover{transform:scale(1.12) !important;}
`;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MAIN COMPONENT
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export default function PdQualificationsStatement() {
  document.title = "Declaração de Habilitações";

  const [allClasses,       setAllClasses]       = useState([]);
  const [selectedClass,    setSelectedClass]    = useState(null);
  const [studentsOptions,  setStudentsOptions]  = useState([]);
  const [selectedStudents, setSelectedStudents] = useState([]);
  const [loadingStudents,  setLoadingStudents]  = useState(false);

  const [withNotes,      setWithNotes]      = useState(true);
  const [fontSize,       setFontSize]       = useState(12);
  const [logoUrl,        setLogoUrl]        = useState("");
  const [logoFile,       setLogoFile]       = useState(null);
  const [institute,      setInstitute]      = useState(null);
  const [allSubjects,    setAllSubjects]    = useState([]);
  const [allAcLevels,    setAllAcLevels]    = useState([]);
  const [passValue,      setPassValue]      = useState(10);
  const [passValueAuto,  setPassValueAuto]  = useState(true);
  const [borderMode,     setBorderMode]     = useState("none");
  const [headerLines,    setHeaderLines]    = useState([]);
  const [subjects,       setSubjects]       = useState([]);
  const [columns,        setColumns]        = useState([]);
  const [studentGrades,  setStudentGrades]  = useState({});
  const [overrides,      setOverrides]      = useState({});
  const [removedBlocks,  setRemovedBlocks]  = useState({});
  const [editMode,       setEditMode]       = useState(false);
  const [fullEditMode,   setFullEditMode]   = useState(false);
  const [activeIdx,      setActiveIdx]      = useState(0);
  const [exporting,      setExporting]      = useState(false);
  const [exportSaving,   setExportSaving]   = useState(false);
  const [saving,         setSaving]         = useState(false);
  const [currentDeclId,  setCurrentDeclId]  = useState(null);
  const [modalOpen,      setModalOpen]      = useState(false);
  const [modalStudentId, setModalStudentId] = useState(null);
  const [secHdr,         setSecHdr]         = useState(false);
  const [secGrades,      setSecGrades]      = useState(false);

  /* ── NOVO: colorir notas por critério ──────────────────────── */
  const [colorGrades, setColorGrades] = useState(true);

  const [history,        setHistory]        = useState([]);
  const [historyLoading, setHistoryLoading] = useState(false);

  /* ── Histórico ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const loadHistory = useCallback(() => {
    setHistoryLoading(true);
    axios.get(API.declarations())
      .then(r => setHistory(Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : []))
      .catch(() => setHistory([]))
      .finally(() => setHistoryLoading(false));
  }, []);

  /* ── Bootstrap ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  useEffect(() => {
    loadHistory();
    axios.get(API.classes())
      .then(r => {
        const list = Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data.map(c => ({
          value:         c.ed_class_id,
          label:         c.ed_class_title,
          period:        PERIODS[Math.floor(c.ed_class_period)] || String(c.ed_class_period || ""),
          academic_year: c.ed_academic_year_title,
          classroom:     c.ed_classroom_title,
          grade:         c.ed_academic_level_title,
          grade_raw:     c.ed_academic_level_title,
        })) : [];
        setAllClasses(list);
      })
      .catch(() => setAllClasses([]));

    axios.get(API.institute())
      .then(r => {
        if (r.data?.length) {
          const inst = r.data[0];
          setInstitute(inst);
          setHeaderLines([
            { text: "REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA",    bold: false },
            { text: "MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO", bold: false },
            ...(inst.ed_institute_state ? [{ text: inst.ed_institute_state.toUpperCase(), bold: false }] : []),
            { text: (inst.ed_institute_name || "").toUpperCase(), bold: true },
          ]);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setHeaderLines([
          { text: "REPÚBLICA DE ANGOLA",    bold: false },
          { text: "MINISTÉRIO DA EDUCAÇÃO", bold: false },
          { text: "NOME DA ESCOLA", bold: true },
        ]);
      });

    axios.get(API.subjects())
      .then(r => { if (Array.isArray(r.data)) setAllSubjects(r.data.map(s => ({ id: s.ed_subject_id, title: s.ed_subject_title }))); })
      .catch(() => {});

    axios.get(API.academicLevels())
      .then(r => { if (Array.isArray(r.data)) setAllAcLevels(r.data.map(l => ({ id: l.ed_academic_level_id, title: l.ed_academic_level_title }))); })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!passValueAuto || !selectedClass) return;
    setPassValue(detectPassValue(selectedClass.grade_raw || selectedClass.label));
  }, [selectedClass, passValueAuto]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedClass) { setStudentsOptions([]); setSelectedStudents([]); return; }
    setLoadingStudents(true);
    axios.get(API.studentsByClass(selectedClass.value))
      .then(r => {
        const raw = Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : r.data?.data || [];
        const opts = raw.map(s => ({
          value:               s.ed_student_id,
          label:               s.ed_student_name,
          picture:             s.ed_student_picture || null,
          student_id:          s.ed_student_id,
          student_name:        s.ed_student_name,
          student_birthday:    s.ed_student_birthday,
          student_identityCard: s.ed_student_identityCard,
          student_naturalness: s.ed_student_naturalness,
          student_gender:      s.ed_student_gender,
          student_code:        s.ed_student_code,
          father_name:         s.ed_student_father_name,
          mother_name:         s.ed_student_mother_name,
          class_title:         selectedClass.label,
          academic_year:       selectedClass.academic_year,
          class_period:        selectedClass.period,
        }));
        setStudentsOptions(opts);
        setSelectedStudents(opts);
        setActiveIdx(0);
      })
      .catch(() => setStudentsOptions([]))
      .finally(() => setLoadingStudents(false));
  }, [selectedClass]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setStudentGrades(prev => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      selectedStudents.forEach(s => {
        if (!next[s.value]) {
          next[s.value] = subjects.map(subj => ({ id: subj.id, name: subj.name, grades: {} }));
        } else {
          next[s.value] = subjects.map(subj => {
            const existing = next[s.value].find(g => g.id === subj.id);
            return existing ? { ...existing, name: subj.name } : { id: subj.id, name: subj.name, grades: {} };
          });
        }
      });
      return next;
    });
  }, [selectedStudents, subjects]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!logoFile) return;
    const r = new FileReader();
    r.onload = ev => setLogoUrl(ev.target.result);
    r.readAsDataURL(logoFile);
  }, [logoFile]);

  const handleOverride = (studentId, field, value) => {
    setOverrides(prev => ({ ...prev, [studentId]: { ...(prev[studentId] || {}), [field]: value } }));
  };

  const handleStudentGradeChange = (studentId, subjectId, colId, val) => {
    setStudentGrades(prev => {
      const stuGrades = (prev[studentId] || []).map(g =>
        g.id === subjectId ? { ...g, grades: { ...g.grades, [colId]: val } } : g
      );
      return { ...prev, [studentId]: stuGrades };
    });
  };

  const handleToggleBlock = (studentId, blockId) => {
    setRemovedBlocks(prev => {
      const cur = prev[studentId] || [];
      const idx = cur.indexOf(blockId);
      return { ...prev, [studentId]: idx >= 0 ? cur.filter(b => b !== blockId) : [...cur, blockId] };
    });
  };

  /* ── Guardar declaração ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  const buildPayload = useCallback(() => ({
    class_id:       selectedClass?.value || null,
    class_title:    selectedClass?.label || null,
    academic_year:  selectedClass?.academic_year || null,
    with_notes:     withNotes,
    pass_value:     passValue,
    font_size:      fontSize,
    border_mode:    borderMode,
    logo_url:       logoUrl || null,
    header_lines:   headerLines,
    subjects,
    columns,
    students:       selectedStudents,
    student_grades: studentGrades,
    overrides,
    student_names:  selectedStudents.map(s => s.student_name || s.label),
    student_codes:  selectedStudents.map(s => s.student_code || ""),
    student_pics:   selectedStudents.map(s => s.picture || null),
  }), [selectedStudents, selectedClass, withNotes, passValue, fontSize, borderMode,
       logoUrl, headerLines, subjects, columns, studentGrades, overrides]);

  const handleSaveDeclaration = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedStudents.length) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload = buildPayload();
      if (currentDeclId) {
        await axios.put(API.declarationUpd(currentDeclId), payload);
        toast.success("Declaração actualizada!");
      } else {
        const r = await axios.post(API.declarationSave(), payload);
        setCurrentDeclId(r.data?.inserted_id || null);
        toast.success("Declaração guardada!");
      }
      loadHistory();
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error("Erro ao guardar a declaração.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }, [buildPayload, currentDeclId, loadHistory, selectedStudents]);

  /* ── Carregar aluno individual do histórico ─────────────────── */
  /* v8: carrega apenas o aluno daquela linha, não todos */
  const handleLoadSingle = useCallback((row) => {
    try {
      const h   = row._entry;
      const s   = row._studentData;
      const ovr = row._overrides || {};
      const grd = row._grades || [];

      const headerLines_ = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_header_lines || "[]");
      const subjects_    = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_subjects      || "[]");
      const columns_     = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_columns       || "[]");

      setHeaderLines(headerLines_);
      setSubjects(subjects_);
      setColumns(columns_);
      setLogoUrl(h.ed_qs_logo_url || "");
      setWithNotes(!!h.ed_qs_with_notes);
      setPassValue(h.ed_qs_pass_value || 10);
      setFontSize(h.ed_qs_font_size || 12);
      setBorderMode(h.ed_qs_border_mode || "none");
      setCurrentDeclId(h.ed_qs_id);
      setRemovedBlocks({});

      if (s) {
        const studentObj = {
          ...s,
          value:      s.value || s.student_id,
          label:      s.student_name || s.label,
          student_id: s.student_id || s.value,
        };
        setSelectedStudents([studentObj]);
        setStudentsOptions([studentObj]);
        const studentId = studentObj.value;
        setStudentGrades({ [studentId]: Array.isArray(grd) ? grd : [] });
        setOverrides({ [studentId]: ovr });
      }

      setActiveIdx(0);
      setEditMode(false);
      setFullEditMode(true);
      toast.success(`Declaração de ${s?.student_name || s?.label || "—"} carregada!`);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error("Erro ao carregar declaração.");
    }
  }, []);

  /* ── Exportar PDF de um aluno individual do histórico ───────── */
  const handleExportSinglePdf = useCallback(async (row) => {
    const h   = row._entry;
    const s   = row._studentData;
    const ovr = row._overrides || {};
    const grd = row._grades    || [];
    let columns_ = [];
    let headerLines_ = [];
    try { columns_     = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_columns      || "[]"); } catch {}
    try { headerLines_ = JSON.parse(h.ed_qs_header_lines || "[]"); } catch {}

    const studentId = s?.student_id || s?.value;
    const student = {
      student_id:           studentId,
      student_name:         s?.student_name || s?.label || "—",
      student_birthday:     s?.student_birthday,
      student_identityCard: s?.student_identityCard,
      student_naturalness:  s?.student_naturalness,
      student_gender:       s?.student_gender,
      student_code:         s?.student_code || "—",
      father_name:          s?.father_name,
      mother_name:          s?.mother_name,
      class_title:          h.ed_qs_class_title,
      academic_year:        h.ed_qs_academic_year,
    };

    await exportDeclarationPDF({
      students:      [student],
      withNotes:     !!h.ed_qs_with_notes,
      overrides:     { [studentId]: ovr },
      studentGrades: { [studentId]: Array.isArray(grd) ? grd : [] },
      headerLines:   headerLines_,
      logoUrl:       h.ed_qs_logo_url || null,
      institute,
      columns:       columns_,
      passValue:     h.ed_qs_pass_value || 10,
      fontSize:      h.ed_qs_font_size  || 12,
      borderMode:    h.ed_qs_border_mode || "none",
      colorGrades,
    });
    toast.success("PDF exportado com sucesso!");
  }, [institute, colorGrades]);

  /* ── Exportar PDF (sessão actual, sem guardar) ──────────────── */
  const doExportPdf = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedStudents.length) return;
    const mapped = selectedStudents.map(s => ({
      student_id:           s.value || s.student_id,
      student_name:         s.student_name || s.label,
      student_birthday:     s.student_birthday,
      student_identityCard: s.student_identityCard,
      student_naturalness:  s.student_naturalness,
      student_gender:       s.student_gender,
      student_code:         s.student_code,
      father_name:          s.father_name,
      mother_name:          s.mother_name,
      class_title:          selectedClass?.label,
      academic_year:        selectedClass?.academic_year,
    }));
    await exportDeclarationPDF({
      students:      mapped,
      withNotes,
      overrides,
      studentGrades,
      headerLines,
      logoUrl:       logoUrl || null,
      institute,
      columns,
      passValue,
      fontSize,
      borderMode,
      colorGrades,
    });
  }, [selectedStudents, withNotes, overrides, studentGrades, headerLines,
      logoUrl, institute, columns, passValue, fontSize, borderMode, colorGrades, selectedClass]);

  const handleExportOnly = useCallback(async () => {
    setExporting(true);
    try { await doExportPdf(); toast.success("PDF exportado com sucesso!"); }
    catch (e) { console.error(e); toast.error("Erro ao exportar PDF."); }
    finally { setExporting(false); }
  }, [doExportPdf]);

  const handleExportAndSave = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedStudents.length) return;
    setExportSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload = buildPayload();
      if (currentDeclId) {
        await axios.put(API.declarationUpd(currentDeclId), payload);
      } else {
        const r = await axios.post(API.declarationSave(), payload);
        setCurrentDeclId(r.data?.inserted_id || null);
      }
      loadHistory();
      await doExportPdf();
      toast.success("Declaração guardada e PDF exportado!");
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e); toast.error("Erro ao exportar e guardar.");
    } finally {
      setExportSaving(false);
    }
  }, [buildPayload, currentDeclId, doExportPdf, loadHistory, selectedStudents]);

  const handlePrint = () => {
    const el = document.getElementById(`decl-doc-${activeIdx}`);
    if (!el) return;
    const win = window.open("", "_blank");
    win.document.write(`<html><head><title>Declaração</title>
      <style>body{margin:0;background:#fff;}@media print{body{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;}}
      table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;}td,th{border:1px solid #999;padding:4px 8px;}</style>
      </head><body>${el.innerHTML}</body></html>`);
    win.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => { win.focus(); win.print(); }, 400);
  };

  const classInfo = useMemo(() => selectedClass ? {
    academic_year: selectedClass.academic_year,
    period:        selectedClass.period,
    classroom:     selectedClass.classroom,
    grade:         selectedClass.grade,
    title:         selectedClass.label,
  } : null, [selectedClass]);

  const current    = selectedStudents[activeIdx];
  const curOvr     = overrides[current?.value] || {};
  const missing    = current ? getMissingFields({ ...current, ...curOvr }) : [];
  const hasMissing = missing.length > 0;

  const SubHdr = (label, open, setOpen) => (
    <button onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
      style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 0", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 7, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: 600, color: "#334155", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
      <span style={{ opacity: .45, fontSize: 9 }}>{open ? "▾" : "▸"}</span>{label}
    </button>
  );

  /* ── Render ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  return (
    <DashboardContainerWrapper content={
      <div className="decl-root">
        <style>{UI_CSS}</style>

        {/* TOP BAR */}
        <div className="decl-topbar">
          <div className="decl-topbar-icon"><TbReportAnalytics size={22} color="#fff"/></div>
          <div>
            <div className="decl-topbar-title">Declaração de Habilitações</div>
            <div className="decl-topbar-sub">Com e sem notas · Todos os textos editáveis · Blocos removíveis · Exportar ou Guardar</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* HISTÓRICO */}
        <HistoryPanel
          history={history}
          loading={historyLoading}
          onLoadSingle={handleLoadSingle}
          onExportSinglePdf={handleExportSinglePdf}
        />

        {/* CONFIG PANEL */}
        <div className="decl-panel">
          <div className="decl-phdr">
            <div className="decl-phdr-icon"><MdOutlineTune size={14}/></div>
            <span className="decl-pttl">Configurações</span>
          </div>

          {/* Tipo */}
          <div className="decl-pbody-sm">
            <div className="decl-lbl"><MdOutlineDescription size={13}/> Tipo de Declaração</div>
            <div className="decl-pills">
              {[[true, "Com Notas"], [false, "Sem Notas"]].map(([val, lbl]) => (
                <button key={String(val)} className={`decl-pill${withNotes === val ? " pp" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() => setWithNotes(val)}>
                  {withNotes === val ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={14}/> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={14}/>}
                  {lbl}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="decl-divider"/>

          {/* Turma + Alunos */}
          <div className="decl-pbody">
            <div className="decl-row">
              <div className="decl-col">
                <div className="decl-lbl"><MdOutlineClass size={13}/> Turma</div>
                <Select
                  options={allClasses}
                  value={selectedClass}
                  onChange={cls => { setSelectedClass(cls); setOverrides({}); setRemovedBlocks({}); setActiveIdx(0); setCurrentDeclId(null); }}
                  placeholder="Pesquisar ou seleccionar turma..."
                  isClearable isSearchable
                  noOptionsMessage={() => "Nenhuma turma encontrada"}
                  menuPortalTarget={document.body} menuPosition="fixed"
                  styles={SEL_STYLES}/>
                {selectedClass && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    {selectedClass.academic_year && <span className="decl-chip cs" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{selectedClass.academic_year}</span>}
                    {selectedClass.period        && <span className="decl-chip cs" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{selectedClass.period}</span>}
                    {selectedClass.grade         && <span className="decl-chip cs" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{selectedClass.grade}</span>}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="decl-col" style={{ flex: 2 }}>
                <div className="decl-lbl"><MdOutlinePersonSearch size={13}/> Alunos</div>
                {!selectedClass
