                  ? <div style={{ fontSize: 12.5, color: "#94a3b8", padding: "10px 0" }}>Seleccione uma turma para carregar os alunos.</div>
                  : loadingStudents
                    ? <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, padding: "10px 0", color: "#64748b", fontSize: 13 }}>
                        <Spinner size="sm" animation="border"/> A carregar alunos...
                      </div>
                    : <StudentMultiSelect
                        options={studentsOptions}
                        value={selectedStudents}
                        onChange={v => { setSelectedStudents(v); setActiveIdx(0); }}/>
                }
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="decl-divider"/>

          {/* Opções visuais */}
          <div className="decl-pbody">
            <div className="decl-row">
              <div className="decl-col">
                <div className="decl-lbl"><MdOutlineFormatSize size={13}/> Tamanho de fonte</div>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                  <input type="range" min={9} max={14} step={0.5} value={fontSize}
                    onChange={e => setFontSize(Number(e.target.value))}
                    style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: PURPLE_RAW }}/>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: PURPLE_HEX, minWidth: 28 }}>{fontSize}pt</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="decl-col">
                <div className="decl-lbl"><MdOutlineImage size={13}/> Logótipo</div>
                <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
                  <div style={{ padding: "9px 12px", border: "1.5px dashed #cbd5e1", borderRadius: 10, background: "#f8fafc", fontSize: 12.5, color: "#64748b", cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <MdOutlineImage size={15}/>{logoFile ? logoFile.name : "Carregar logótipo..."}
                  </div>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*"
                    style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, opacity: 0, cursor: "pointer" }}
                    onChange={e => setLogoFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)}/>
                </div>
                {logoUrl && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <img src={logoUrl} alt="" style={{ width: 32, height: 32, objectFit: "contain", border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", borderRadius: 6 }}/>
                    <button onClick={() => { setLogoUrl(""); setLogoFile(null); }}
                      style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#ef4444", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                      Remover
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="decl-col">
                <div className="decl-lbl">Borda do documento</div>
                <div className="decl-pills" style={{ flexDirection: "column", gap: 5 }}>
                  {[["none","Sem borda"],["single","Borda simples"],["double","Borda dupla"],["certificate","Borda certificado"]].map(([val, lbl]) => (
                    <button key={val} className={`decl-pill${borderMode === val ? " pp" : ""}`}
                      style={{ fontSize: 11.5, padding: "5px 12px" }}
                      onClick={() => setBorderMode(val)}>
                      {borderMode === val ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>} {lbl}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Critério + Cor das notas */}
          {withNotes && (
            <>
              <div className="decl-divider"/>
              <div className="decl-pbody-sm">
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 20, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
                  <div>
                    <div className="decl-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>Critério de avaliação</div>
                    <div className="decl-pills">
                      {[[10,"0 — 10"],[20,"0 — 20"]].map(([val, lbl]) => (
                        <button key={val} className={`decl-pill${passValue === val && !passValueAuto ? " pp" : ""}`}
                          onClick={() => { setPassValue(val); setPassValueAuto(false); }}>
                          {passValue === val && !passValueAuto ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>} {lbl}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                      <button className={`decl-pill${passValueAuto ? " pp" : ""}`} onClick={() => setPassValueAuto(true)}>
                        {passValueAuto ? <MdOutlineCheckCircle size={13}/> : <MdOutlineRadioButtonUnchecked size={13}/>}
                        Auto ({passValue === 10 ? "0-10" : "0-20"} detectado)
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* ── NOVO: Checkbox cores das notas ────────────────── */}
                  <div>
                    <div className="decl-lbl" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
                      <MdOutlineColorLens size={13}/> Cor das notas
                    </div>
                    <label style={{
                      display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, cursor: "pointer",
                      padding: "8px 14px", border: `1.5px solid ${colorGrades ? PURPLE_HEX : "#e2e8f0"}`,
                      borderRadius: 10, background: colorGrades ? `rgba(108,63,197,.06)` : "#f8fafc",
                      transition: "all .15s", userSelect: "none", fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12.5,
                    }}>
                      <input type="checkbox" checked={colorGrades} onChange={e => setColorGrades(e.target.checked)}
                        style={{ width: 15, height: 15, accentColor: PURPLE_RAW }}/>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: colorGrades ? PURPLE_RAW : "#64748b" }}>
                        Colorir notas por critério
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ display: "flex", gap: 4, alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <span style={{ width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: "50%", background: colorGrades ? BLUE_POS_CSS : "#94a3b8", display: "inline-block" }}/>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: colorGrades ? BLUE_POS_CSS : "#94a3b8", fontWeight: 700 }}>Positiva</span>
                        <span style={{ width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: "50%", background: colorGrades ? RED_CSS : "#94a3b8", display: "inline-block", marginLeft: 4 }}/>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: colorGrades ? RED_CSS : "#94a3b8", fontWeight: 700 }}>Negativa</span>
                      </span>
                    </label>
                    {!colorGrades && (
                      <div style={{ marginTop: 5, fontSize: 10.5, color: "#94a3b8", fontFamily: F_SANS }}>
                        Notas e extenso em preto — sem coloração por critério.
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <div className="decl-divider"/>
          <div className="decl-pbody-sm">
            {SubHdr("Cabeçalho do Documento", secHdr, setSecHdr)}
            {secHdr && <HeaderEditor headerLines={headerLines} onHeaderLinesChange={setHeaderLines}/>}
          </div>

          {withNotes && (
            <>
              <div className="decl-divider"/>
              <div className="decl-pbody-sm">
                {SubHdr("Disciplinas e Colunas de Classe", secGrades, setSecGrades)}
                {secGrades && (
                  <GradesEditorPanel
                    subjects={subjects} columns={columns}
                    onSubjectsChange={setSubjects} onColumnsChange={setColumns}
                    allSubjects={allSubjects} allAcLevels={allAcLevels} passValue={passValue}/>
                )}
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <div className="decl-divider"/>

          {(editMode || fullEditMode) && (
            <div className="decl-edit-banner">
              <div className="decl-edit-banner-icon"><MdOutlineEditNote size={13} color="#fff"/></div>
              <span>
                {fullEditMode
                  ? "Edição completa activa — todos os textos são editáveis inline. Clique em qualquer texto para editar. Use × vermelho para remover blocos."
                  : "Modo de edição de notas activo — altere os valores directamente na tabela."}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Action bar */}
          <div className="decl-action-bar">
            <div className="decl-stats">
              {(exporting || exportSaving || saving) ? (
                <span className="decl-chip cy">
                  <Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 12, height: 12, borderWidth: 2 }}/>
                  {exporting ? " A exportar..." : exportSaving ? " A guardar e exportar..." : " A guardar..."}
                </span>
              ) : selectedStudents.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <span className="decl-chip cp">{selectedStudents.length} aluno{selectedStudents.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>
                  <span className="decl-chip cs">{withNotes ? "Com notas" : "Sem notas"}</span>
                  {withNotes && <span className="decl-chip cs">Escala 0-{passValue}</span>}
                  {withNotes && (
                    <span className="decl-chip cs" style={{ color: colorGrades ? BLUE_POS_CSS : "#64748b", borderColor: colorGrades ? "rgba(7,42,200,.2)" : "#e2e8f0" }}>
                      {colorGrades ? "Notas coloridas" : "Notas a preto"}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {borderMode !== "none" && <span className="decl-chip cs">Borda: {borderMode}</span>}
                  {currentDeclId && <span className="decl-chip cs" style={{ color: "#22a06b", borderColor: "rgba(34,160,107,.25)", background: "rgba(34,160,107,.08)" }}>Guardado</span>}
                </>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="decl-action-btns">
              {selectedStudents.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <button className={`decl-btn-secondary${editMode ? " active" : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => { setEditMode(v => !v); setFullEditMode(false); }}>
                    {editMode ? <MdOutlineEditOff size={15}/> : <MdOutlineEdit size={15}/>}
                    {editMode ? "Fechar Edição" : "Editar Notas"}
                  </button>
                  <button className={`decl-btn-secondary${fullEditMode ? " active" : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => { setFullEditMode(v => !v); setEditMode(false); }}>
                    {fullEditMode ? <MdOutlineEditOff size={15}/> : <MdOutlineEditNote size={15}/>}
                    {fullEditMode ? "Fechar Edição Completa" : "Edição Completa"}
                  </button>
                  <button className="decl-btn-secondary" onClick={handlePrint}>
                    <PiPrinterDuotone size={16}/> Imprimir
                  </button>
                  <button className="decl-btn-secondary" onClick={handleSaveDeclaration} disabled={saving}>
                    <MdOutlineSave size={15}/> {currentDeclId ? "Actualizar" : "Guardar"}
                  </button>
                  <button className="decl-btn-secondary" onClick={handleExportOnly} disabled={exporting || exportSaving}
                    style={{ borderColor: PURPLE_HEX, color: PURPLE_HEX }}>
                    {exporting
                      ? <><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 13, height: 13, borderWidth: 2 }}/> A gerar...</>
                      : <><MdOutlinePictureAsPdf size={16}/> Exportar PDF</>
                    }
                  </button>
                  <button className="decl-btn-primary" onClick={handleExportAndSave} disabled={exporting || exportSaving || saving}>
                    {exportSaving
                      ? <><Spinner size="sm" animation="border" style={{ width: 14, height: 14, borderWidth: 2 }}/> A guardar...</>
                      : <><MdOutlinePictureAsPdf size={16}/> Exportar e Guardar</>
                    }
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* DOCUMENT PREVIEW / EDIT */}
        <CustomTableContainer
          title={`Declarações — ${selectedClass?.label || "—"} · ${selectedStudents.length} aluno${selectedStudents.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`}
          content={
            <div>
              {!selectedStudents.length && (
                <div className="decl-empty">
                  <div className="decl-empty-icon"><MdOutlineFileDownload size={24}/></div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: "#334155" }}>Nenhum aluno seleccionado</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 12.5, color: "#94a3b8" }}>
                    {!selectedClass ? "Seleccione uma turma para começar." : "Seleccione pelo menos um aluno para gerar declarações."}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* EDIT MODE — documentos completos */}
              {selectedStudents.length > 0 && (editMode || fullEditMode) && (
                <div className="decl-edit-area">
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 52 }}>
                    {selectedStudents.map((s, i) => {
                      const ovr       = overrides[s.value] || {};
                      const miss      = getMissingFields({ ...s, ...ovr });
                      const stuGrades = studentGrades[s.value] || subjects.map(subj => ({ id: subj.id, name: subj.name, grades: {} }));
                      const sBocks    = removedBlocks[s.value] || [];
                      return (
                        <div key={s.value}>
                          <div className="decl-student-label">
                            <div className="decl-student-num">{i + 1}</div>
                            <span>{s.label}</span>
                            {miss.length > 0 && (
                              <button className="decl-btn-warn"
                                style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 12px", marginLeft: "auto" }}
                                onClick={() => { setModalStudentId(s.value); setModalOpen(true); }}>
                                <MdOutlineWarningAmber size={12}/> {miss.length} campos em falta
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="decl-edit-doc-wrap">
                            <div className="decl-doc-full" id={`decl-doc-${i}`}>
                              {withNotes
                                ? <DeclarationWithGrades
                                    data={s} overrides={ovr}
                                    onOverrideChange={(f, v) => handleOverride(s.value, f, v)}
                                    headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                    onLogoUpload={b64 => setLogoUrl(b64)}
                                    editMode={editMode} fullEditMode={fullEditMode}
                                    fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode}
                                    studentSubjects={stuGrades}
                                    onStudentSubjectChange={(subjId, colId, val) => handleStudentGradeChange(s.value, subjId, colId, val)}
                                    columns={columns} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                    removedBlocks={sBocks}
                                    onToggleBlock={(id) => handleToggleBlock(s.value, id)}
                                    colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                                : <DeclarationWithoutGrades
                                    data={s} overrides={ovr}
                                    onOverrideChange={(f, v) => handleOverride(s.value, f, v)}
                                    headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                    onLogoUpload={b64 => setLogoUrl(b64)}
                                    editMode={editMode} fullEditMode={fullEditMode}
                                    fontSize={fontSize} borderMode={borderMode}
                                    classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                    removedBlocks={sBocks}
                                    onToggleBlock={(id) => handleToggleBlock(s.value, id)}/>
                              }
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* PREVIEW MODE — miniaturas */}
              {selectedStudents.length > 0 && !editMode && !fullEditMode && (
                <div>
                  {hasMissing && (
                    <div className="decl-warn-banner">
                      <div style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 6, background: "#f59e0b", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", flexShrink: 0, marginTop: 1 }}>
                        <MdOutlineWarningAmber size={13} color="#fff"/>
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                        <div style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: "#92400e", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                          Campos em falta: {missing.join(" · ")}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <button className="decl-btn-warn" style={{ flexShrink: 0, fontSize: 12, padding: "6px 14px" }}
                        onClick={() => { setModalStudentId(current?.value); setModalOpen(true); }}>
                        <MdOutlineEdit size={13}/> Completar Dados
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="decl-prev-area">
                    <div className="decl-prev-flex">
                      {selectedStudents.map((s, i) => {
                        const ovr       = overrides[s.value] || {};
                        const miss      = getMissingFields({ ...s, ...ovr });
                        const isAct     = i === activeIdx;
                        const stuGrades = studentGrades[s.value] || subjects.map(subj => ({ id: subj.id, name: subj.name, grades: {} }));
                        const sBocks    = removedBlocks[s.value] || [];
                        return (
                          <div key={s.value} className="decl-doc-thumb"
                            style={{ cursor: "pointer", outline: isAct ? `3px solid ${PURPLE_RAW}` : "none", outlineOffset: 2 }}
                            onClick={() => setActiveIdx(i)}>
                            <div className="decl-doc-scaler">
                              {withNotes
                                ? <DeclarationWithGrades
                                    data={s} overrides={ovr}
                                    onOverrideChange={() => {}}
                                    headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                    onLogoUpload={() => {}}
                                    editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                                    fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode}
                                    studentSubjects={stuGrades}
                                    onStudentSubjectChange={() => {}}
                                    columns={columns} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                    removedBlocks={sBocks} onToggleBlock={() => {}}
                                    colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                                : <DeclarationWithoutGrades
                                    data={s} overrides={ovr}
                                    onOverrideChange={() => {}}
                                    headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                    onLogoUpload={() => {}}
                                    editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                                    fontSize={fontSize} borderMode={borderMode}
                                    classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                    removedBlocks={sBocks} onToggleBlock={() => {}}/>
                              }
                            </div>
                            {miss.length > 0 && <div className="decl-thumb-warn">{miss.length}</div>}
                            <div className="decl-thumb-label">{isAct ? "▶ " : ""}{i + 1}. {s.label}</div>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>

                    {current && (
                      <div style={{ marginTop: 20, background: "#fff", borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", overflow: "hidden" }}>
                        <div style={{ background: "#f5f2eb", borderBottom: "1px solid #ddd", padding: "8px 14px", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
                          <span style={{ fontFamily: F_SANS, fontSize: 11, color: "#666" }}>
                            {withNotes ? "Com Notas" : "Sem Notas"} — {current.label}
                          </span>
                          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6 }}>
                            {hasMissing && (
                              <button className="decl-btn-warn" style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 12px" }}
                                onClick={() => { setModalStudentId(current.value); setModalOpen(true); }}>
                                <MdOutlineWarningAmber size={12}/> Completar
                              </button>
                            )}
                            <button className="decl-btn-secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11, padding: "4px 12px" }} onClick={handlePrint}>
                              <PiPrinterDuotone size={14}/> Imprimir
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div id={`decl-doc-${activeIdx}`} style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
                          {withNotes
                            ? <DeclarationWithGrades
                                data={current} overrides={curOvr}
                                onOverrideChange={(f, v) => handleOverride(current.value, f, v)}
                                headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                onLogoUpload={b64 => setLogoUrl(b64)}
                                editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                                fontSize={fontSize} passValue={passValue} borderMode={borderMode}
                                studentSubjects={studentGrades[current.value] || []}
                                onStudentSubjectChange={(subjId, colId, val) => handleStudentGradeChange(current.value, subjId, colId, val)}
                                columns={columns} classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                removedBlocks={removedBlocks[current.value] || []} onToggleBlock={() => {}}
                                colorGrades={colorGrades}/>
                            : <DeclarationWithoutGrades
                                data={current} overrides={curOvr}
                                onOverrideChange={(f, v) => handleOverride(current.value, f, v)}
                                headerLines={headerLines} logoUrl={logoUrl || null}
                                onLogoUpload={b64 => setLogoUrl(b64)}
                                editMode={false} fullEditMode={false}
                                fontSize={fontSize} borderMode={borderMode}
                                classInfo={classInfo} institute={institute}
                                removedBlocks={removedBlocks[current.value] || []} onToggleBlock={() => {}}/>
                          }
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          }
        />

        {/* STUDENT EDIT MODAL */}
        <StudentEditModal
          show={modalOpen}
          onHide={() => setModalOpen(false)}
          studentId={modalStudentId}
          onSaved={() => {
            if (!modalStudentId) return;
            axios.get(API.studentGet(modalStudentId))
              .then(r => {
                if (!r.data?.length) return;
                const d = r.data[0];
                setSelectedStudents(prev => prev.map(s =>
                  String(s.value) === String(modalStudentId) ? {
                    ...s,
                    label:               d.ed_student_name,
                    student_name:        d.ed_student_name,
                    student_birthday:    d.ed_student_birthday,
                    student_identityCard: d.ed_student_identityCard,
                    student_naturalness: d.ed_student_naturalness,
                    student_gender:      d.ed_student_gender,
                    father_name:         d.ed_student_father_name,
                    mother_name:         d.ed_student_mother_name,
                    picture:             d.ed_student_picture || s.picture,
                  } : s
                ));
              })
              .catch(() => {});
          }}
        />
      </div>
    }/>
  );
}

const multer = require(`multer`);
const path = require(`path`);
const CheckInternet = require("../config/CheckInternet");
const { DB_SQLITE, DATABASE } = require("../config/Database");
const { GetCurrentUserData } = require("./GetCurrentUserData");
const ExecuteQuery = require("../Functions/ExecuteQuery");
const { RegisterAuditAction } = require("./Auditory");
 

 const GetStudents = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const instituteCode  = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
    const isNormalSchool = (req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type * 1) === 0;

    const BASE_SELECT = `SELECT s.*, c.*, ci.*, cr.*, ay.*, co.*, al.*`;

    const BASE_JOINS = `
      FROM eduall_students s

      LEFT JOIN eduall_class c
        ON s.ed_student_class = c.ed_class_id
       AND c.ed_class_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ci
        ON ci.ed_cicle_id = c.ed_class_cicle
       AND ci.ed_cicle_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_classrooms cr
        ON cr.ed_classroom_id = c.ed_class_room
       AND cr.ed_classroom_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_year ay
        ON ay.ed_academic_year_id = c.ed_class_year
       AND ay.ed_academic_year_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_courses co
        ON co.ed_course_id = c.ed_class_course
       AND co.ed_course_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_level al
        ON al.ed_academic_level_id = c.ed_class_academic_level
       AND al.ed_academic_level_deleted = 0
    `;

    // Condição que oculta alunos sem turma quando existe outro
    // com o mesmo nome que já tem turma atribuída
    const DUPLICATE_FILTER = `
      AND NOT (
        s.ed_student_class = 0
        AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1
          FROM eduall_students s2
          WHERE s2.ed_student_name           = s.ed_student_name
            AND s2.ed_student_institute_code = s.ed_student_institute_code
            AND s2.ed_student_deleted        = 0
            AND s2.ed_student_class         != 0
            AND s2.ed_student_id            != s.ed_student_id
        )
      )
    `;

    let query, PARAMS;

    if (isNormalSchool) {
      query = `
        ${BASE_SELECT}
        ${BASE_JOINS}
        WHERE s.ed_student_deleted          = 0
          AND s.ed_student_institute_code   = ?
        ${DUPLICATE_FILTER}
        ORDER BY s.ed_student_name ASC
      `;
      PARAMS = [instituteCode];
    } else {
      query = `
        ${BASE_SELECT}, sd.*
        ${BASE_JOINS}

        LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details sd
          ON sd.ed_detail_student_code = s.ed_student_id

        WHERE s.ed_student_deleted          = 0
          AND s.ed_student_institute_code   = ?
        ${DUPLICATE_FILTER}
        ORDER BY s.ed_student_name ASC
      `;
      PARAMS = [instituteCode];
    }

    const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, {
      db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req
    });

    res.status(200).json(rows);
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch students!",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};


const GetStudentsByClass = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const CLASS = parseInt(req.params.CLASS, 10);

    if (isNaN(CLASS)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Parâmetro CLASS inválido."
      });
    }

    const instituteCode  = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
    const isNormalSchool = (req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type * 1) === 0;

    const BASE_SELECT = `SELECT s.*, c.*, ci.*, cr.*, ay.*, co.*, al.*`;

    const BASE_JOINS = `
      FROM eduall_students s

      LEFT JOIN eduall_class c
        ON s.ed_student_class = c.ed_class_id

      LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ci
        ON ci.ed_cicle_id = c.ed_class_cicle
       AND ci.ed_cicle_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_classrooms cr
        ON cr.ed_classroom_id = c.ed_class_room
       AND cr.ed_classroom_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_year ay
        ON ay.ed_academic_year_id = c.ed_class_year
       AND ay.ed_academic_year_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_courses co
        ON co.ed_course_id = c.ed_class_course
       AND co.ed_course_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_level al
        ON al.ed_academic_level_id = c.ed_class_academic_level
       AND al.ed_academic_level_deleted = 0

      LEFT JOIN eduall_transport_passengers tp
        ON tp.ed_transport_passenger_code = s.ed_student_id
    `;

    // Mesma condição anti-duplicado
    const DUPLICATE_FILTER = `
      AND NOT (
        s.ed_student_class = 0
        AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1
          FROM eduall_students s2
          WHERE s2.ed_student_name           = s.ed_student_name
            AND s2.ed_student_institute_code = s.ed_student_institute_code
            AND s2.ed_student_deleted        = 0
            AND s2.ed_student_class         != 0
            AND s2.ed_student_id            != s.ed_student_id
        )
      )
    `;

    let query, PARAMS;

    if (isNormalSchool) {
      query = `
        ${BASE_SELECT}
        ${BASE_JOINS}
        WHERE s.ed_student_deleted          = 0
          AND s.ed_student_institute_code   = ?
          AND s.ed_student_class            = ?
          AND c.ed_class_deleted            = 0
        ${DUPLICATE_FILTER}
        ORDER BY s.ed_student_name ASC
      `;
      PARAMS = [instituteCode, CLASS];
    } else {
      query = `
        ${BASE_SELECT}, sd.*
        ${BASE_JOINS}

        LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details sd
          ON sd.ed_detail_student_code = s.ed_student_id

        WHERE s.ed_student_deleted          = 0
          AND s.ed_student_institute_code   = ?
          AND s.ed_student_class            = ?
          AND c.ed_class_deleted            = 0
        ${DUPLICATE_FILTER}
        ORDER BY s.ed_student_name ASC
      `;
      PARAMS = [instituteCode, CLASS];
    }

    const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, {
      db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req
    });

    res.status(200).json(rows);
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch students by class!",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};



const GetSTDS = async (req, res) => {
   try {
       const query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
                      LEFT JOIN eduall_class ON eduall_students.ed_student_class = eduall_class.ed_class_id  
                      LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ON ed_cicle_id = eduall_class.ed_class_cicle  
                      WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 
                      ORDER BY ed_student_name ASC`;
       const PARAMS = [];
       const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, { db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req });
       res.status(200).json(rows);
   } catch (error) {
       res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch student details!", error: error.message });
   }
};

const GetStudentsByInstitute = async (req, res) => {
   try {
       const { CODE } = req.params;
       const instituteCode = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
       const instituteType = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type;
       let query, PARAMS;

       if (instituteType * 1 === 0) {
           query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_class ON eduall_students.ed_student_class = eduall_class.ed_class_id  
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ON ed_cicle_id = eduall_class.ed_class_cicle 
                    

                    WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND eduall_students.ed_student_institute_code = ? 

                    ORDER BY ed_student_name ASC`;
           PARAMS = [CODE];
       } else {
           query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_class ON eduall_students.ed_student_class = eduall_class.ed_class_id  
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ON ed_cicle_id = eduall_class.ed_class_cicle 
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
                    WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND eduall_students.ed_student_institute_code = ?  
                    AND eduall_students_details.ed_detail_deleted = 0 
                    ORDER BY ed_student_name ASC`;
           PARAMS = [CODE];
       }

       const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, { db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req });
       res.status(200).json(rows);
   } catch (error) {
       res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch students by institute!", error: error.message });
   }
};

const GetSingleStudent = async (req, res) => {
   try {
     const { ID } = req.params;
     const instituteCode = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
     const instituteType = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type;
     let query, PARAMS;
 
     if (instituteType * 1 === 0) {
       query = `
         SELECT * FROM eduall_students
         LEFT JOIN eduall_institutes ON ed_institute_code = ed_student_institute_code
         LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
         LEFT JOIN eduall_class ON ed_class_id = ed_student_class AND ed_class_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_faculty ON ed_faculty_id = ed_class_faculty AND ed_faculty_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_classrooms ON ed_classroom_id = ed_class_room AND ed_classroom_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ON ed_cicle_id = ed_class_cicle AND ed_cicle_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_courses ON ed_course_id = ed_class_course AND ed_course_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_level ON ed_academic_level_id = ed_class_academic_level AND ed_academic_level_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_year ON ed_academic_year_id = ed_class_year AND ed_academic_year_deleted = 0
         WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_id = ? AND ed_student_institute_code = ?
       `;
       PARAMS = [ID, instituteCode];
     } else {
       query = `
         SELECT * FROM eduall_students
         LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
         LEFT JOIN eduall_institutes ON ed_institute_code = ed_student_institute_code
         LEFT JOIN eduall_class ON ed_class_id = ed_student_class AND ed_class_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_faculty ON ed_faculty_id = ed_class_faculty AND ed_faculty_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_classrooms ON ed_classroom_id = ed_class_room AND ed_classroom_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_cicles ON ed_cicle_id = ed_class_cicle AND ed_cicle_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_courses ON ed_course_id = ed_class_course AND ed_course_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_level ON ed_academic_level_id = ed_class_academic_level AND ed_academic_level_deleted = 0
         LEFT JOIN eduall_academic_year ON ed_academic_year_id = ed_class_year AND ed_academic_year_deleted = 0
         WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_id = ? AND ed_student_institute_code = ?
       `;
       PARAMS = [ID, instituteCode];
     }
 
     const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, {
       db: "eduall_students",
       type: "select",
       data: req,
     });
 
     res.status(200).json(rows);
   } catch (error) {
     res.status(500).json({
       success: false,
       message: "Failed to fetch student!",
       error: error.message,
     });
   }
 };
 

const GetSingleStudentByIdentityCard = async (req, res) => {
   try {
       const { IDENTITITYCARD } = req.params;
       const instituteCode = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
       const instituteType = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type;
       const identityCard = IDENTITITYCARD.toLowerCase();
       let query, PARAMS;

       if (instituteType * 1 === 0) {
           query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
                    WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_identityCard = ? 
                    AND ed_student_institute_code = ?`;
           PARAMS = [identityCard, instituteCode];
       } else {
           query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
                    LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
                    WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_identityCard = ? 
                    AND eduall_students_details.ed_detail_deleted = 0 
                    AND ed_student_institute_code = ?`;
           PARAMS = [identityCard, instituteCode];
       }

       const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, { db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req });
       res.status(200).json(rows);
   } catch (error) {
       res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch student by identity card!", error: error.message });
   }
};




const GetSingleStudentByCode = async (req, res) => {
   try {
      const { CODE } = req.params;
      let query, PARAMS;

      if (req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type * 1 === 0) {
         query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_code = ?`;
         PARAMS = [CODE];
      } else {
         query = `SELECT * FROM eduall_students LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details 
                  ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
                  WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND eduall_students_details.ed_detail_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_code = ?`;
         PARAMS = [CODE];
      }

      const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, true, { db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req });
      res.status(200).json(rows);
   } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch student by code!", error: error.message });
   }
};
 

const GetTransportPassengersByClass = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { CLASS } = req.params; // ID da turma (opcional, se quiser filtrar por turma)
    const institute = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;

    let query = `
      SELECT tp.ed_transport_passenger_id, tp.ed_transport_passenger_code, tp.ed_transport_passenger_startMonth,
             s.ed_student_id, s.ed_student_name, s.ed_student_class, s.ed_student_picture,
             c.ed_class_title
      FROM eduall_transport_passengers tp
      INNER JOIN eduall_students s ON s.ed_student_id = tp.ed_transport_passenger_code
      INNER JOIN eduall_class c ON s.ed_student_class = c.ed_class_id
      WHERE tp.ed_transport_passenger_deleted = 0
        AND s.ed_student_deleted = 0
        AND tp.ed_transport_passenger_institute_code = ?
        AND s.ed_student_institute_code = ?
    `;
    
    const params = [institute, institute];

    if (CLASS) {
      query += ` AND s.ed_student_class = ?`;
      params.push(CLASS);
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY s.ed_student_name ASC`;

    const rows = await ExecuteQuery(res, query, params, true, { db: "eduall_students", type: "select", data: req });
    res.status(200).json(rows);

  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Erro ao buscar passageiros da turma!", error: error.message });
  }
};



/// preciso que melhores estas duas funções uando o numero de matricula eesta vazio aparece a mengsame. de que ja existem alguem com aauele numero emboara esteja vazio e , se estuver vaziio gerara automaticmante um numero de matricula , e aoa atualizar o emmso se naoa tuvwr um gerara um code ucnico d ematricula , fazeer isso e. devolver fullcode de mabas as funç~eos:

 

/**
 * =============================================================
 * FUNÇÃO: Registrar Estudante (refatorada com ExecuteQuery aprimorada)
 * =============================================================
 */


 

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  RegisterStudent.js  (Backend Controller – Full Code)
//  Handles: Student registration + optional enrollment at registration time
//
//  Body fields (além dos existentes):
//    enrollment_service  — ID do serviço de propinas (string/int).
//                          Se presente e válido → cria matrícula imediatamente.
//                          Se ausente / vazio   → regista aluno SEM matrícula.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const RegisterStudent = async (req, res) => {
  const pad = (n, w = 6) => String(n).padStart(w, "0");

  // ── Garante linha do contador para esta escola ──────────────────────────────
  const ensureCounterRow = async (inst) => {
    await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `INSERT INTO eduall_institute_counters (ed_institute_code, ed_lastest_student_code)
       VALUES (?, 0)
       ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE ed_lastest_student_code = ed_lastest_student_code`,
      [inst],
      false
    );
  };

  // ── Gera código sequencial com lock de linha ────────────────────────────────
  const lockAndGenerateCode = async (inst) => {
    const row = await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `SELECT ed_lastest_student_code
         FROM eduall_institute_counters
        WHERE ed_institute_code = ?
        FOR UPDATE`,
      [inst],
      true
    );
    const next = ((row[0]?.ed_lastest_student_code ?? 0) * 1) + 1;
    await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `UPDATE eduall_institute_counters
          SET ed_lastest_student_code = ?
        WHERE ed_institute_code = ?`,
      [next, inst],
      false
    );
    return pad(next, 6);
  };

  // ── Helper: verifica se o serviço existe e pertence à instituição ───────────
  const validateService = async (serviceId, inst) => {
    if (!serviceId) return null;
    const sid = parseInt(serviceId, 10);
    if (!sid || isNaN(sid)) return null;

    const rows = await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `SELECT ed_service_id
         FROM eduall_services
        WHERE ed_service_id             = ?
          AND ed_service_institute_code = ?
          AND ed_service_deleted        = 0
        LIMIT 1`,
      [sid, inst],
      true
    );
    return rows.length ? sid : null;   // null = não encontrado / inválido
  };

  // ── Helper: cria a matrícula e devolve o ID inserido ───────────────────────
  const createEnrollment = async (studentId, serviceId, inst) => {
    const result = await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `INSERT INTO eduall_enrollment
         (ed_enrollment_student, ed_enrollment_service, ed_enrollment_institute_code)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?)`,
      [studentId, serviceId, inst],
      false,
      { db: "eduall_enrollment", type: "insert", data: req }
    );
    return result.insertId;   // ID da nova matrícula
  };

  // ── Helper: auditoria de matrícula ─────────────────────────────────────────
  const auditEnrollment = async (studentCode, studentName) => {
    await RegisterAuditAction({
      session:     req.session,
      action:      1,
      form:        "Matrículas",
      description: `Matrícula criada no registo do estudante nº #${studentCode} – ${studentName}`,
    }).catch((err) => console.error("Erro na auditoria de matrícula:", err));
  };

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  INÍCIO DO FLUXO PRINCIPAL
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  try {
    // ── 1. Validação da data de nascimento ──────────────────────────────────
    const birthday = new Date(req.body.student_birthday);
    const today    = new Date();
    const diffDays = (today - birthday) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);

    if (isNaN(birthday.getTime()) || birthday > today || diffDays < 4) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        msg: "Data de nascimento inválida ou com menos de 4 dias da data atual.",
      });
    }

    const instituteCode = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;
    const instituteType = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute_type * 1;

    await ensureCounterRow(instituteCode);

    // ── 2. Código de matrícula ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    let studentCode = (req.body.student_code || "").trim();

    if (studentCode) {
      // Código fornecido manualmente → verificar duplicado
      const dup = await ExecuteQuery(
        res,
        `SELECT 1
           FROM eduall_students
          WHERE ed_student_institute_code = ?
            AND ed_student_code           = ?
            AND ed_student_deleted        = 0
          LIMIT 1`,
        [instituteCode, studentCode],
        true
      );
      if (dup.length) {
        return res.status(409).json({
          success: false,
          msg: `Já existe um estudante com o número ${studentCode} nesta escola.`,
        });
      }
    } else {
      // Gerar sequencial automático
      studentCode = await lockAndGenerateCode(instituteCode);
    }

    // ── 3. Validar serviço de propinas (matrícula opcional) ─────────────────
    //    enrollment_service vem do body quando o utilizador escolhe matricular
    //    o aluno no mesmo momento do registo. Se não vier / for vazio → sem matrícula.
    const enrollmentServiceId = await validateService(
      req.body.enrollment_service,
      instituteCode
    );
    // enrollmentServiceId === null  → sem matrícula
    // enrollmentServiceId === <int> → criar matrícula após inserir o aluno

    // ── 4. INSERT principal — eduall_students ───────────────────────────────
    const result = await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `INSERT INTO eduall_students (
         ed_student_lastclass, ed_student_lastschool,
         ed_student_name, ed_student_address, ed_student_nacionality,
         ed_student_gender, ed_student_religion, ed_student_birthday,
         ed_student_phone, ed_student_email,
         ed_student_class, ed_student_code, ed_student_picture,
         ed_student_identityCard,
         ed_student_health_problems, ed_student_health_problems_description,
         ed_student_status, ed_student_naturalness,
         ed_student_institute_code, ed_student_type,
         ed_student_scholarshipHolder,
         ed_student_father_name, ed_student_father_phone, ed_student_father_email,
         ed_student_father_job, ed_student_father_emergency_phone,
         ed_student_father_address, ed_student_father_is_guardion,
         ed_student_mother_name, ed_student_mother_phone, ed_student_mother_email,
         ed_student_mother_job, ed_student_mother_emergency_phone,
         ed_student_mother_address, ed_student_mother_is_guardion,
         ed_student_guardion_name, ed_student_guardion_phone, ed_student_guardion_email,
         ed_student_guardion_job, ed_student_guardion_emergency_phone,
         ed_student_guardion_address, ed_student_guardion_gender,
         ed_student_guardion_degree, ed_student_guardion_is_guardion
       ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`,
      [
        // Dados do aluno
        req.body.student_lastclass    ?? "",
        req.body.student_lastschool   ?? "",
        req.body.student_name,
        req.body.student_address,
        req.body.student_nacionality,
        req.body.student_gender,
        req.body.student_religion     ?? "",
        req.body.student_birthday,
        req.body.student_phone,
        req.body.student_email        ?? "",
        req.body.student_class,
        studentCode,
        req.file ? "images/students/" + req.file.filename : "",
        req.body.student_identityCard,
        req.body.student_health_problems             ?? 0,
        req.body.student_health_problems_description ?? "",
        req.body.student_status       ?? 1,
        req.body.student_naturalness  ?? "",
        instituteCode,
        instituteType,
        req.body.student_scholarshipHolder ?? 0,
        // Pai
        req.body.father_name            ?? null,
        req.body.father_phone           ?? null,
        req.body.father_email           ?? null,
        req.body.father_job             ?? null,
        req.body.father_emergency_phone ?? null,
        req.body.father_address         ?? null,
        req.body.father_is_guardion === "1" ? 1 : 0,
        // Mãe
        req.body.mother_name            ?? null,
        req.body.mother_phone           ?? null,
        req.body.mother_email           ?? null,
        req.body.mother_job             ?? null,
        req.body.mother_emergency_phone ?? null,
        req.body.mother_address         ?? null,
        req.body.mother_is_guardion === "1" ? 1 : 0,
        // Responsável
        req.body.guardion_name              ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_phone             ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_email             ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_job               ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_emergency_phone   ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_address           ?? null,
        req.body.guardion_gender            ?? 0,
        req.body.guardion_degree_of_kinship ?? 0,
        req.body.guardion_is_guardion === "1" ? 1 : 0,
      ],
      false,
      { db: "eduall_students", type: "insert", data: req }
    );

    const studentId = result.insertId;

    // ── 5. INSERT detalhes — eduall_students_details ────────────────────────
    await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `INSERT INTO eduall_students_details (
         ed_detail_student_code,
         ed_detail_lastschool_course, ed_detail_lastschool_score,
         ed_detail_lastschool_formationfield, ed_detail_lastschool_conclusionyear,
         ed_detail_lifeconditions, ed_detail_socialsecurity, ed_detail_sociallevel,
         ed_detail_work, ed_detail_workplace, ed_detail_workfunction
       ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`,
      [
        studentId,
        req.body.student_lastschool_course         ?? null,
        req.body.student_lastschool_score          ?? null,
        req.body.student_lastschool_formationfield ?? null,
        req.body.student_lastschool_conclusionyear ?? null,
        req.body.student_lifeconditions ?? 0,
        req.body.student_socialsecurity ?? 0,
        req.body.student_sociallevel    ?? 0,
        req.body.student_workstatus     ?? 0,
        req.body.student_workplace      ?? null,
        req.body.student_worktitle      ?? null,
      ],
      false,
      { db: "eduall_students_details", type: "insert", data: req }
    );

    // ── 6. Matrícula opcional ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    //    Só executada quando o utilizador escolheu um serviço de propinas.
    let enrollmentId  = null;
    let enrolled      = false;

    if (enrollmentServiceId !== null) {
      // 6a. Verificar se já existe matrícula para este aluno
      //     (improvável logo após o INSERT, mas cobre re-tentativas)
      const alreadyEnrolled = await ExecuteQuery(
        res,
        `SELECT ed_enrollment_id
           FROM eduall_enrollment
          WHERE ed_enrollment_deleted        = 0
            AND ed_enrollment_institute_code = ?
            AND ed_enrollment_student        = ?
          LIMIT 1`,
        [instituteCode, studentId],
        true
      );

      if (alreadyEnrolled.length === 0) {
        // 6b. Criar matrícula
        enrollmentId = await createEnrollment(studentId, enrollmentServiceId, instituteCode);
        enrolled     = true;

        // 6c. Auditoria da matrícula (não bloqueia a resposta)
        auditEnrollment(studentCode, req.body.student_name);
      }
      // Se por algum motivo já existia, simplesmente devolve enrolled: false
      // e não duplica a matrícula
    }

    // ── 7. Auditoria do registo do aluno ────────────────────────────────────
    await RegisterAuditAction({
      session:     req.session,
      action:      1,
      form:        "Estudantes",
      description: `Registou estudante (${req.body.student_name}) – Matrícula: ${studentCode}${enrolled ? " | Matriculado no acto de registo" : ""}`,
    });

    // ── 8. Resposta ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //    O frontend verifica enrolled + enrollment_id para mostrar o modal
    //    correcto e o link para o recibo de matrícula.
    return res.status(201).json({
      success:               true,
      msg:                   enrolled
                               ? "Estudante registado e matriculado com sucesso!"
                               : "Estudante registado com sucesso!",
      student_code:          studentCode,
      student_id:            studentId,
      student_identity_card: req.body.student_identityCard,
      // Campos novos — usados pelo frontend
      enrolled:              enrolled,           // boolean
      enrollment_id:         enrollmentId,       // int | null
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro ao registar estudante:", error);
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      msg:     "Erro ao registar estudante.",
      error:   error.message,
    });
  }
};




 
const StudentUpdate = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { ID } = req.params;
    const {
      student_name, student_address, student_nacionality, student_gender,
      student_religion, student_birthday, student_phone, student_phone2,
      student_email, student_class, student_code, student_identityCard,
      student_health_problems, student_health_problems_description, student_status,
      student_naturalness, student_lastschool_course, student_lastschool_score,
      student_lastschool_formationfield, student_lastschool_conclusionyear,
      student_lifeconditions, student_socialsecurity, student_sociallevel,
      student_lastclass, student_lastschool,
      student_workstatus, student_workplace, student_worktitle,
      student_scholarshipHolder,
      // Pai
      father_name, father_phone, father_email, father_job,
      father_emergency_phone, father_address, father_is_guardion,
      // Mãe
      mother_name, mother_phone, mother_email, mother_job,
      mother_emergency_phone, mother_address, mother_is_guardion,
      // Responsável
      guardion_name, guardion_phone, guardion_email, guardion_job,
      guardion_emergency_phone, guardion_address,
      guardion_gender, guardion_degree_of_kinship, guardion_is_guardion,
    } = req.body;

    const instituteCode = req.session.user.eduall_user_session_curentinstitute;

    // ── Validação de data ────────────────────────────────────────
    const birthday = new Date(student_birthday);
    const today = new Date();
    if (isNaN(birthday.getTime()) || birthday > today) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, msg: "Data de nascimento inválida." });
    }
    if ((today - birthday) / 86400000 < 4) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, msg: "A data de nascimento deve ter pelo menos 4 dias." });
    }

    // ── Buscar estudante atual ───────────────────────────────────
    const existing = await ExecuteQuery(
      res,
      `SELECT ed_student_code, ed_student_class
       FROM eduall_students
       WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0
         AND ed_student_id = ?
         AND ed_student_institute_code = ?`,
      [ID, instituteCode],
      true
    );
    if (existing.length === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, msg: "Estudante não encontrado." });
    }

    const oldClass    = existing[0].ed_student_class;
    const currentCode = String(existing[0].ed_student_code || "").trim();
    let   newCode     = String(student_code || "").trim();

    // ── Verificar mudança de turma ───────────────────────────────
    if (oldClass && student_class && oldClass != student_class) {
      const [oldYear] = await ExecuteQuery(res,
        `SELECT ed_class_year FROM eduall_class WHERE ed_class_id = ?`, [oldClass], true);
      const [newYear] = await ExecuteQuery(res,
        `SELECT ed_class_year FROM eduall_class WHERE ed_class_id = ?`, [student_class], true);
      if (oldYear?.ed_class_year && newYear?.ed_class_year && oldYear.ed_class_year !== newYear.ed_class_year) {
        await ExecuteQuery(res,
          `INSERT INTO eduall_student_old_classes (ed_student_old_class_student, ed_student_old_class) VALUES (?,?)`,
          [ID, oldClass], false);
      }
    }

    // ── Gerar código se necessário ───────────────────────────────
    if (!newCode) {
      const counter = await ExecuteQuery(res,
        `SELECT ed_lastest_student_code FROM eduall_institute_counters WHERE ed_institute_code = ? FOR UPDATE`,
        [instituteCode], true);
      let next = ((counter[0]?.ed_lastest_student_code ?? 0) * 1) + 1;
      newCode = String(next).padStart(6, "0");
      await ExecuteQuery(res,
        `UPDATE eduall_institute_counters SET ed_lastest_student_code = ? WHERE ed_institute_code = ?`,
        [next, instituteCode], false);
    }

    // ── Verificar duplicação de matrícula ────────────────────────
    if (newCode !== currentCode) {
      const dup = await ExecuteQuery(res,
        `SELECT 1 FROM eduall_students WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_institute_code = ?
         AND ed_student_code = ? AND ed_student_id <> ? LIMIT 1`,
        [instituteCode, newCode, ID], true);
      if (dup.length > 0) {
        return res.status(409).json({ success: false, msg: `Já existe outro estudante com o número ${newCode}.` });
      }
    }

    // ── UPDATE principal + pais — tudo em eduall_students ────────
    const picturePath = req.file ? "images/students/" + req.file.filename : null;

    const updateQuery = `
      UPDATE eduall_students SET
        ed_student_lastclass                   = ?,
        ed_student_lastschool                  = ?,
        ed_student_name                        = ?,
        ed_student_address                     = ?,
        ed_student_nacionality                 = ?,
        ed_student_gender                      = ?,
        ed_student_religion                    = ?,
        ed_student_birthday                    = ?,
        ed_student_phone                       = ?,
        ed_student_phone2                      = ?,
        ed_student_email                       = ?,
        ed_student_class                       = ?,
        ed_student_code                        = ?,
        ed_student_identityCard                = ?,
        ed_student_health_problems             = ?,
        ed_student_health_problems_description = ?,
        ed_student_status                      = ?,
        ed_student_naturalness                 = ?,
        ed_student_scholarshipHolder           = ?,
        ed_student_father_name                 = ?,
        ed_student_father_phone                = ?,
        ed_student_father_email                = ?,
        ed_student_father_job                  = ?,
        ed_student_father_emergency_phone      = ?,
        ed_student_father_address              = ?,
        ed_student_father_is_guardion          = ?,
        ed_student_mother_name                 = ?,
        ed_student_mother_phone                = ?,
        ed_student_mother_email                = ?,
        ed_student_mother_job                  = ?,
        ed_student_mother_emergency_phone      = ?,
        ed_student_mother_address              = ?,
        ed_student_mother_is_guardion          = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_name               = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_phone              = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_email              = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_job                = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_emergency_phone    = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_address            = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_gender             = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_degree             = ?,
        ed_student_guardion_is_guardion        = ?
        ${picturePath ? ", ed_student_picture = ?" : ""}
      WHERE ed_student_id = ?
        AND ed_student_deleted = 0
        AND ed_student_institute_code = ?`;

    await ExecuteQuery(res, updateQuery, [
      // Dados do aluno
      student_lastclass            ?? '',
      student_lastschool           ?? '',
      student_name,
      student_address,
      student_nacionality,
      student_gender,
      student_religion             ?? '',
      student_birthday,
      student_phone,
      student_phone2               ?? '',
      student_email                ?? '',
      student_class,
      newCode,
      student_identityCard,
      student_health_problems             ?? 0,
      student_health_problems_description ?? '',
      student_status               ?? 1,
      student_naturalness          ?? '',
      student_scholarshipHolder    ?? 0,
      // Pai
      father_name            ?? null,
      father_phone           ?? null,
      father_email           ?? null,
      father_job             ?? null,
      father_emergency_phone ?? null,
      father_address         ?? null,
      father_is_guardion === '1' ? 1 : 0,
      // Mãe
      mother_name            ?? null,
      mother_phone           ?? null,
      mother_email           ?? null,
      mother_job             ?? null,
      mother_emergency_phone ?? null,
      mother_address         ?? null,
      mother_is_guardion === '1' ? 1 : 0,
      // Responsável
      guardion_name              ?? null,
      guardion_phone             ?? null,
      guardion_email             ?? null,
      guardion_job               ?? null,
      guardion_emergency_phone   ?? null,
      guardion_address           ?? null,
      guardion_gender            ?? 0,
      guardion_degree_of_kinship ?? 0,
      guardion_is_guardion === '1' ? 1 : 0,
      // Foto (condicional)
      ...(picturePath ? [picturePath] : []),
      // WHERE
      ID,
      instituteCode
    ], false, { db: "eduall_students", type: "update", data: req });

    // ── Upsert detalhes — eduall_students_details ────────────────
    await ExecuteQuery(res,
      `INSERT INTO eduall_students_details (
         ed_detail_student_code,
         ed_detail_lastschool_course, ed_detail_lastschool_score,
         ed_detail_lastschool_formationfield, ed_detail_lastschool_conclusionyear,
         ed_detail_lifeconditions, ed_detail_socialsecurity, ed_detail_sociallevel,
         ed_detail_work, ed_detail_workplace, ed_detail_workfunction
       ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
       ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
         ed_detail_lastschool_course         = VALUES(ed_detail_lastschool_course),
         ed_detail_lastschool_score          = VALUES(ed_detail_lastschool_score),
         ed_detail_lastschool_formationfield = VALUES(ed_detail_lastschool_formationfield),
         ed_detail_lastschool_conclusionyear = VALUES(ed_detail_lastschool_conclusionyear),
         ed_detail_lifeconditions            = VALUES(ed_detail_lifeconditions),
         ed_detail_socialsecurity            = VALUES(ed_detail_socialsecurity),
         ed_detail_sociallevel               = VALUES(ed_detail_sociallevel),
         ed_detail_work                      = VALUES(ed_detail_work),
         ed_detail_workplace                 = VALUES(ed_detail_workplace),
         ed_detail_workfunction              = VALUES(ed_detail_workfunction)`,
      [
        ID,
        student_lastschool_course         ?? null,
        student_lastschool_score          ?? null,
        student_lastschool_formationfield ?? null,
        student_lastschool_conclusionyear ?? null,
        student_lifeconditions ?? 0,
        student_socialsecurity ?? 0,
        student_sociallevel    ?? 0,
        student_workstatus     ?? 0,
        student_workplace      ?? null,
        student_worktitle      ?? null
      ], false, { db: "eduall_students_details", type: "update", data: req });

    // ── Auditoria ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    await RegisterAuditAction({
      session: req.session, action: 2, form: "Estudantes",
      description: `Atualizou dados do estudante #${ID} (matrícula: ${newCode})`
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      msg: "Estudante atualizado com sucesso!",
      student_code: newCode
    });

  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Erro ao atualizar estudante:", err);
    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, msg: err.message });
  }
};

     const resp =  await ExecuteQuery(res, query, PARAMS, false, {
         db: "eduall_students",

      console.log(" res = ", resp);

async function getSingleStudentByInstitute(instituteCode, instituteType, ID) {
   try {
     let query, PARAMS;
 
     if (parseInt(instituteType) === 0) {
       query = `
         SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
         WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_id = ? AND ed_student_institute_code = ?
       `;
       PARAMS = [ID, instituteCode];
     } else {
       query = `
         SELECT * FROM eduall_students 
         LEFT JOIN eduall_students_details 
         ON eduall_students_details.ed_detail_student_code = eduall_students.ed_student_id
         WHERE ed_student_deleted = 0 AND ed_student_id = ? AND ed_student_institute_code = ?
       `;
       PARAMS = [ID, instituteCode];
     }
 
     const rows = await ExecuteQuery(null, query, PARAMS, true, {
       db: "eduall_students",
       type: "select",
     });
 
     return rows;
   } catch (error) {
     throw new Error(`Erro ao buscar estudante: ${error.message}`);
   }
 }

   }
